    const cz = Math.sin(ang) * rad;

    dummy.position.set(cx, cy, cz);
    dummy.rotation.set(0, -ang, 0);
    dummy.scale.set(24, 4.5, 13);
    dummy.updateMatrix();
    cloudInstancedMesh.setMatrixAt(i * 2, dummy.matrix);

    puff2Offset.set(4, 2.5, 1).applyAxisAngle(THREE.Object3D.DEFAULT_UP, -ang);
    dummy.position.set(cx + puff2Offset.x, cy + puff2Offset.y, cz + puff2Offset.z);
    dummy.scale.set(15, 5.5, 9);
    dummy.updateMatrix();
    cloudInstancedMesh.setMatrixAt(i * 2 + 1, dummy.matrix);
  }
  cloudInstancedMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
}
skyGroup.add(cloudInstancedMesh);

skyGroup.visible = true;
scene.add(skyGroup);

// PS3-Era Motion Vignette & Camera FOV Kick Controller State
const prevCamQuat = new THREE.Quaternion();
const curCamQuat = new THREE.Quaternion();
let turnSpeedSmooth = 0;
let motionBlurOverlayEl = null;
let lastBlurOpacity = '';
let cachedFlareEl = null;
let cachedFlareGhosts = null;
let cachedFlareSource = null;
let lastFlareOpacity = '';
const BASE_FOV = 72;
const GHOST_POSITIONS = [0.42, 0.78, 1.18];

export function updateSunLensFlare() {
  camera.updateMatrixWorld();

  // ---- Camera Rotation Speed Tracking & Dynamic FOV Kick / Vignette Overlay ----
  camera.getWorldQuaternion(curCamQuat);
  const angleDiff = prevCamQuat.angleTo(curCamQuat);
  prevCamQuat.copy(curCamQuat);

  const rawTurnSpeed = Math.min(1.0, angleDiff * 16.0);
  turnSpeedSmooth += (rawTurnSpeed - turnSpeedSmooth) * 0.18;

  // Dynamic FOV Kick on fast camera turns
  const targetFov = BASE_FOV + turnSpeedSmooth * 6.5;
  if (Math.abs(camera.fov - targetFov) > 0.01) {
    camera.fov = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(camera.fov, targetFov, 0.15);
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  }



  // GPU-friendly CSS Edge Vignette Overlay (avoids full-screen backdrop-filter readbacks)
  if (!motionBlurOverlayEl && typeof document !== 'undefined') {
    motionBlurOverlayEl = document.getElementById('ps3-motion-blur');
    if (!motionBlurOverlayEl) {
      motionBlurOverlayEl = document.createElement('div');
      motionBlurOverlayEl.id = 'ps3-motion-blur';
      motionBlurOverlayEl.style.cssText =
        'position:fixed;inset:0;pointer-events:none;z-index:1;opacity:0;will-change:opacity;' +
        'box-shadow:inset 0 0 95px rgba(0,0,0,0.58);' +
        'background:radial-gradient(circle, transparent 48%, rgba(15,12,10,0.34) 94%);';
      document.body.appendChild(motionBlurOverlayEl);
    }
  }

  if (motionBlurOverlayEl) {
    const blurOpacity = turnSpeedSmooth < 0.005 ? '0' : Math.min(0.85, turnSpeedSmooth * 1.25).toFixed(2);
    if (blurOpacity !== lastBlurOpacity) {
      motionBlurOverlayEl.style.opacity = blurOpacity;
      lastBlurOpacity = blurOpacity;
    }
  }

  // ---- Sun Lens Flare logic (with cached DOM nodes) ----
  if (!cachedFlareEl || !cachedFlareEl.isConnected) {
    cachedFlareEl = typeof document !== 'undefined' ? document.getElementById('sun-lens-flare') : null;
    if (cachedFlareEl) {
      cachedFlareGhosts = Array.from(cachedFlareEl.querySelectorAll('.flare-ghost'));
      cachedFlareSource = cachedFlareEl.querySelector('.flare-source');
      lastFlareOpacity = '';
    }
  }
  const flare = cachedFlareEl;
  if (!flare) return;

  if (!skyGroup.visible) {
    if (lastFlareOpacity !== '0') {
      flare.style.opacity = '0';
      lastFlareOpacity = '0';
    }
    return;
  }

  sunDisc.getWorldPosition(sunScreenPosition).project(camera);
  const edge = Math.max(Math.abs(sunScreenPosition.x), Math.abs(sunScreenPosition.y));
  if (sunScreenPosition.z < -1 || sunScreenPosition.z > 1 || edge > 1.18) {
    if (lastFlareOpacity !== '0') {
      flare.style.opacity = '0';
      lastFlareOpacity = '0';
    }
    return;
  }

  const sunX = (sunScreenPosition.x + 1) * 50;
  const sunY = (1 - sunScreenPosition.y) * 50;
  const edgeFade = Math.min(1, (1.18 - edge) / 0.2);
  const centerFade = 0.65 + 0.35 * (1 - Math.min(1, Math.hypot(sunScreenPosition.x, sunScreenPosition.y) / 1.5));
  const nextOpacity = (edgeFade * centerFade * 0.8).toFixed(3);
  if (nextOpacity !== lastFlareOpacity) {
    flare.style.opacity = nextOpacity;
    lastFlareOpacity = nextOpacity;
  }

  const sunXStr = `${sunX.toFixed(2)}%`;
  const sunYStr = `${sunY.toFixed(2)}%`;

  if (cachedFlareGhosts) {
    for (let index = 0; index < cachedFlareGhosts.length; index++) {
      const ghost = cachedFlareGhosts[index];
      const t = GHOST_POSITIONS[index] ?? 1.0;
      ghost.style.left = `${(sunX + (50 - sunX) * t).toFixed(2)}%`;
      ghost.style.top = `${(sunY + (50 - sunY) * t).toFixed(2)}%`;
    }
  }
  if (cachedFlareSource) {
    cachedFlareSource.style.left = sunXStr;
    cachedFlareSource.style.top = sunYStr;
  }
}
// Subtle psychedelic world breathing & liquid shear (zero extra draw calls)
export function updateWorldWobble(elapsed, intensity = 1.0) {
  if (!camera) return;

  // Reset from current fov/aspect so values never drift over time
  camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  if (intensity <= 0) return;

  // Dampen slightly while aiming so ranged shots stay accurate
  const amt = (gameState.isAiming ? 0.25 : 1.0) * intensity;

  // Layered incommensurate sine waves for an organic, non-repeating pulse
  const waveA = Math.sin(elapsed * 0.85);
  const waveB = Math.cos(elapsed * 0.68);
  const drift = Math.sin(elapsed * 0.43);

  // ~0.65% counter-phase squash & stretch ("breathing" horizon)
  const scaleX = 1.0 + waveA * 0.0065 * amt;
  const scaleY = 1.0 + waveB * 0.0065 * amt;

  // Gentle diagonal shear wobble (~0.25 degrees)
  const shearX = Math.sin(elapsed * 1.15 + waveB * 0.6) * 0.0045 * amt;
  const shearY = Math.cos(elapsed * 0.92 + drift * 0.6) * 0.0035 * amt;

  const e = camera.projectionMatrix.elements;
  e[0] *= scaleX;
  e[5] *= scaleY;
  e[4] += shearX;
  e[1] += shearY;

  camera.projectionMatrixInverse.copy(camera.projectionMatrix).invert();
}

export function buildTexture(type) {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = 128;
  c.height = 128;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');

  if (type === 'asphalt') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#232936';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    for (let i = 0; i < 1400; i++) {
      const v = 30 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 45);
      const isGlint = Math.random() > 0.96;
      ctx.fillStyle = isGlint ? '#e2e8f0' : `rgb(${v},${v + 4},${v + 10})`;
      ctx.fillRect(Math.random() * 128, Math.random() * 128, isGlint ? 1 : 2, isGlint ? 1 : 2);
    }
  } else if (type === 'sidewalk') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#cbd5e1';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#64748b';
    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
    ctx.strokeRect(1.5, 1.5, 61, 61);
    ctx.strokeRect(65.5, 1.5, 61, 61);
    ctx.strokeRect(1.5, 65.5, 61, 61);
    ctx.strokeRect(65.5, 65.5, 61, 61);
    for (let i = 0; i < 400; i++) {
      ctx.fillStyle = Math.random() > 0.5 ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)' : 'rgba(15,23,42,0.1)';
      ctx.fillRect(Math.random() * 128, Math.random() * 128, 2, 2);
    }
  } else if (type === 'cobblestone') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#6b6661';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    const tones = ['#9e9791', '#c7c4c0', '#8593a6', '#a8a298'];
    for (let row = 0; row < 8; row++) {
      const off = (row % 2) * 8;
      for (let col = -1; col < 8; col++) {
        ctx.fillStyle = tones[(row * 3 + col + 4) % tones.length];
        ctx.fillRect(col * 16 + off + 1, row * 16 + 1, 14, 14);
      }
    }
  } else if (type === 'marble') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#f8fafc';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.42)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(0, i * 16);
      ctx.bezierCurveTo(42, i * 16 + 12, 86, i * 16 - 10, 128, i * 16 + 6);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  } else if (type === 'granite') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#1e293b';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    const specks = ['#f8fafc', '#94a3b8', '#38bdf8', '#0f172a'];
    for (let i = 0; i < 900; i++) {
      ctx.fillStyle = specks[i % specks.length];
      ctx.fillRect(Math.random() * 128, Math.random() * 128, 2, 2);
    }
  } else if (type === 'concrete') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#94a3b8';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    for (let i = 0; i < 900; i++) {
      const v = 125 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 45);
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${v},${v},${v + 4})`;
      ctx.fillRect(Math.random() * 128, Math.random() * 128, 2, 2);
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.22)';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 62, 128, 2);
    ctx.fillRect(62, 0, 2, 128);
  } else if (type === 'diamondplate') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#cbd5e1';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#475569';
    for (let y = 8; y < 128; y += 16) {
      for (let x = 8; x < 128; x += 16) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(x, y);
        ctx.rotate(((x + y) / 16) % 2 === 0 ? Math.PI / 4 : -Math.PI / 4);
        ctx.fillRect(-4, -1.5, 8, 3);
        ctx.restore();
      }
    }
  } else if (type === 'brushedsteel') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#e2e8f0';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    for (let y = 0; y < 128; y += 2) {
      ctx.fillStyle = y % 4 === 0 ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.45)' : 'rgba(71,85,105,0.25)';
      ctx.fillRect(0, y, 128, 1);
    }
  } else if (type === 'corrugated') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    for (let x = 0; x < 128; x += 8) {
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.28)';
      ctx.fillRect(x, 0, 3, 128);
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.28)';
      ctx.fillRect(x + 3, 0, 2, 128);
    }
  } else if (type === 'fabric') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.14)';
    for (let y = 0; y < 128; y += 4) ctx.fillRect(0, y, 128, 1);
    for (let x = 0; x < 128; x += 4) ctx.fillRect(x, 0, 1, 128);
  } else if (type === 'water') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#0284c7';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(224, 242, 254, 0.65)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    for (let y = 8; y < 128; y += 16) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      for (let x = 0; x <= 128; x += 16) {
        ctx.lineTo(x, y + Math.sin((x / 128) * Math.PI * 4) * 3);
      }
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  } else if (type === 'sand') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#d99b52';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    for (let i = 0; i < 950; i++) {
      ctx.fillStyle = i % 2 === 0 ? 'rgba(180, 105, 42, 0.25)' : 'rgba(253, 230, 138, 0.22)';
      ctx.fillRect(Math.random() * 128, Math.random() * 128, 3, 2);
    }
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(146, 64, 14, 0.18)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    for (let y = 12; y < 128; y += 24) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(0, y);
      ctx.bezierCurveTo(42, y + 6, 84, y - 6, 128, y + 2);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  } else if (type === 'redrock') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#b45309';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    const strata = ['#9a3412', '#c2410c', '#d97706', '#7c2d12'];
    for (let y = 0; y < 128; y += 16) {
      ctx.fillStyle = strata[(y / 16) % strata.length];
      ctx.fillRect(0, y, 128, 14);
    }
  } else if (type === 'metallicflake') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    for (let i = 0; i < 1100; i++) {
      ctx.fillStyle = i % 2 === 0 ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.22)' : 'rgba(15,23,42,0.12)';
      ctx.fillRect(Math.random() * 128, Math.random() * 128, 1.5, 1.5);
    }
  } else if (type === 'grass') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#60a152';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    for (let i = 0; i < 650; i++) {
      ctx.fillStyle = i % 2 === 0 ? '#539146' : '#71b362';
      ctx.fillRect(Math.random() * 128, Math.random() * 128, 2, 4);
    }
  } else if (type === 'siding') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.14)';
    for (let y = 0; y < 128; y += 16) ctx.fillRect(0, y, 128, 3);
  } else if (type === 'shingles') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.26)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    for (let y = 0; y < 128; y += 16) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(0, y);
      ctx.lineTo(128, y);
      ctx.stroke();
      const off = (y / 16) % 2 === 0 ? 0 : 16;
      for (let x = off; x < 128; x += 32) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(x, y);
        ctx.lineTo(x, y + 16);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
    }
  } else if (type === 'brick') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.22)';
    for (let y = 0; y < 128; y += 16) {
      ctx.fillRect(0, y, 128, 2);
      const off = (y / 16) % 2 === 0 ? 0 : 16;
      for (let x = off; x < 128; x += 32) ctx.fillRect(x, y, 2, 16);
    }
  } else if (type === 'wood') {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(69, 26, 3, 0.22)';
    for (let y = 0; y < 128; y += 21) ctx.fillRect(0, y, 128, 2);
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(69, 26, 3, 0.35)';
    ctx.lineWidth = 6;
    ctx.strokeRect(3, 3, 122, 122);
  }

  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
  tex.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  tex.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
  return tex;
}

// Lazily construct procedural textures on first access so 'low' graphics tier skips startup generation
const PROC_TEXTURE_TYPES = [
  'asphalt', 'sidewalk', 'cobblestone', 'marble', 'granite', 'concrete',
  'diamondplate', 'brushedsteel', 'corrugated', 'fabric', 'water', 'sand',
  'redrock', 'metallicflake', 'grass', 'siding', 'shingles', 'brick', 'wood'
];

export const procTextures = {};
for (const texType of PROC_TEXTURE_TYPES) {
  let cachedTex = null;
  Object.defineProperty(procTextures, texType, {
    enumerable: true,
    configurable: true,
    get() {
      if (!cachedTex) cachedTex = buildTexture(texType);
      return cachedTex;
    }
  });
}

export const registeredMaterials = [];
export const highDetailObjects = [];
export let animatedWaterTex = null;

export function makeMat(hex, opts = {}) {
  const usePhysical = opts.clearcoat !== undefined || opts.isCarMetal;

  const defaultRoughness = opts.roughness ?? (opts.isCarMetal ? 0.35 : (opts.metalness > 0.4 ? 0.45 : 0.72));
  const emissiveVal = opts.emissive ?? 0x000000;
  const defaultEmissiveIntensity = opts.emissiveIntensity ?? (emissiveVal ? 2.5 : 0);

  const params = {
    color: hex,
    flatShading: opts.flatShading ?? true,
    roughness: defaultRoughness,
    metalness: opts.metalness ?? (opts.isCarMetal ? 0.75 : 0.06),
    emissive: emissiveVal,
    emissiveIntensity: defaultEmissiveIntensity,
    transparent: opts.transparent ?? false,
    opacity: opts.opacity ?? 1,
    envMapIntensity: opts.envMapIntensity ?? (opts.isCarMetal ? 2.0 : (opts.metalness > 0.4 ? 1.5 : 0.85))
  };

  if (usePhysical) {
    params.clearcoat = opts.clearcoat ?? 0.88;
    params.clearcoatRoughness = opts.clearcoatRoughness ?? 0.06;
  }

  const mat = usePhysical
    ? new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial(params)
    : new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial(params);

  if (opts.texType) {
    const defaultBumpScales = {
      asphalt: 0.05,
      concrete: 0.035,
      cobblestone: 0.08,
      brick: 0.07,
      diamondplate: 0.06,
      granite: 0.03,
      sand: 0.04,
      redrock: 0.06,
      shingles: 0.05,
      wood: 0.04,
      metallicflake: 0.025,
      sidewalk: 0.03
    };

    registeredMaterials.push({
      mat,
      texType: opts.texType,
      repeatX: opts.repeatX ?? 2,
      repeatY: opts.repeatY ?? 2,
      bumpScale: opts.bumpScale ?? (defaultBumpScales[opts.texType] || 0),
      keepSmooth: opts.flatShading === false
    });
  }
  return mat;
}

const sharedMatCache = new Map();
export function makeSharedMat(hex, opts = {}) {
  const keys = Object.keys(opts).sort();
  let key = String(hex);
  for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
    const k = keys[i];
    key += '|' + k + ':' + opts[k];
  }
  let mat = sharedMatCache.get(key);
  if (!mat) {
    mat = makeMat(hex, opts);
    mat.userData.shared = true;
    sharedMatCache.set(key, mat);
  }
  return mat;
}

// Shared Real Transparent Glass Material for All Windows (FrontSide halves fragment overdraw)
export const realWindowGlassMat = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({
  color: 0xbae6fd,
  roughness: 0.07,
  metalness: 0.15,
  transparent: true,
  opacity: 0.34,
  clearcoat: 1.0,
  clearcoatRoughness: 0.05,
  depthWrite: false,
  side: THREE.FrontSide
});

export function regHigh(obj) {
  obj.visible = gameState.settings.graphics === 'high';
  highDetailObjects.push(obj);
  return obj;
}

const tierTexCache = new Map();
function tierTexture(texType, repeatX, repeatY, anisotropy) {
  const key = texType + '|' + repeatX + '|' + repeatY;
  let tex = tierTexCache.get(key);
  if (!tex) {
    const baseTex = procTextures[texType];
    if (!baseTex) return null;
    tex = baseTex.clone();
    tex.needsUpdate = true;
    tex.repeat.set(repeatX, repeatY);
    tierTexCache.set(key, tex);
  }
  if (tex.anisotropy !== anisotropy) {
    tex.anisotropy = anisotropy;
    tex.needsUpdate = true;
  }
  return tex;
}

export function applyGraphicsTier(tier) {
  gameState.settings.graphics = tier;
  const useTextures = tier === 'medium' || tier === 'high';
  const isHigh = tier === 'high';
  const maxAniso = renderer ? Math.min(4, renderer.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy()) : 1;
  const targetAniso = isHigh ? maxAniso : 1;

  for (let i = 0; i < registeredMaterials.length; i++) {
    const entry = registeredMaterials[i];
    const wantMap = useTextures && PROC_TEXTURE_TYPES.includes(entry.texType)
      ? tierTexture(entry.texType, entry.repeatX, entry.repeatY, targetAniso)
      : null;
    const wantBump = isHigh && entry.bumpScale > 0 ? wantMap : null;
    const wantFlat = entry.keepSmooth ? false : true;
    let changed = false;
    if (entry.mat.map !== wantMap) { entry.mat.map = wantMap; changed = true; }
    if (entry.mat.bumpMap !== wantBump) { entry.mat.bumpMap = wantBump; entry.mat.bumpScale = wantBump ? entry.bumpScale : 0; changed = true; }
    if (entry.mat.flatShading !== wantFlat) { entry.mat.flatShading = wantFlat; changed = true; }
    if (wantMap && entry.texType === 'water') animatedWaterTex = wantMap;
    if (changed) entry.mat.needsUpdate = true;
  }

  // Keep full clearcoat reflections on 'high' tier while avoiding dual-lobe cost on 'low'/'medium'
  const wantGlassClearcoat = isHigh ? 1.0 : 0.0;
  if (realWindowGlassMat.clearcoat !== wantGlassClearcoat) {
    realWindowGlassMat.clearcoat = wantGlassClearcoat;
    realWindowGlassMat.needsUpdate = true;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < highDetailObjects.length; i++) {
    highDetailObjects[i].visible = isHigh;
  }
  skyGroup.visible = true;
  rimLight.intensity = isHigh ? 0.65 : 0;
  sunLight.intensity = isHigh ? 2.4 : 2.0;
  hemiLight.intensity = isHigh ? 0.52 : 0.46;

  if (renderer) {
    if (tier === 'low') {
      renderer.shadowMap.enabled = false;
    } else {
      renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
      const targetSize = isHigh ? 2048 : 1024;
      if (sunLight.shadow.mapSize.width !== targetSize) {
        sunLight.shadow.mapSize.width = targetSize;
        sunLight.shadow.mapSize.height = targetSize;
        if (sunLight.shadow.map) { sunLight.shadow.map.dispose(); sunLight.shadow.map = null; }
      }
      renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate = true;
    }

    const maxDpr = tier === 'low' ? 1.0 : tier === 'medium' ? 1.35 : 1.85;
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, maxDpr) * PIXEL_RENDER_SCALE);
    resizeRenderer();
  }
}

// ---- Underground atmosphere, UI, minimap ----
export const AT = { sun: sunLight.intensity, hemi: hemiLight.intensity, bg: scene.background.clone(), on: false };

export const uLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffe2b0, 22, 18, 2);
uLight.visible = false;
scene.add(uLight);

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// World Root Container
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
export const worldRoot = new THREE.Group();
worldRoot.name = 'WorldRoot';
scene.add(worldRoot);

const viewport = { container: null, observer: null, callbacks: [] };

/** Size of the render area: the canvas' parent in Studio, the whole window in the game. */
export function getViewportSize() {
  if (viewport.container) return { width: Math.max(1, viewport.container.clientWidth), height: Math.max(1, viewport.container.clientHeight) };
  return { width: window.innerWidth, height: window.innerHeight };
}

export function resizeRenderer() {
  if (!renderer) return;
  const { width, height } = getViewportSize();
  renderer.setSize(width, height, !viewport.container);
}

/** Register a callback fired with (width, height) whenever the viewport container is resized (Studio). */
export function onViewportResize(cb) { viewport.callbacks.push(cb); }

/**
 * Create the WebGL renderer.
 *  - initEngine()            -> full-screen canvas on document.body (game).
 *  - initEngine({ canvas })  -> render into the given canvas and follow its parent's size (Studio).
 */
export async function initEngine({ canvas: providedCanvas } = {}) {
  let el = providedCanvas || null;
  if (!el) {
    el = document.getElementById('game-canvas');
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement('canvas');
      el.id = 'game-canvas';
      el.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;width:100vw;height:100vh;display:block;z-index:0';
      document.body.appendChild(el);
    }
  }
  canvas = el;
  renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({
    canvas: el,
    antialias: PIXEL_RENDER_SCALE >= 1.0,
    powerPreference: 'high-performance',
    stencil: false
  });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 1.35) * PIXEL_RENDER_SCALE);
  renderer.shadowMap.enabled = gameState.settings.graphics !== 'low';
  renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
  renderer.shadowMap.autoUpdate = false;
  renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate = true;
  renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.18;
  viewport.container = providedCanvas ? (providedCanvas.parentElement || null) : null;
  resizeRenderer();
  if (viewport.container && typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined') {
    viewport.observer = new ResizeObserver(() => {
      resizeRenderer();
      const { width, height } = getViewportSize();
      viewport.callbacks.forEach((cb) => cb(width, height));
    });
    viewport.observer.observe(viewport.container);
  }
  return { scene, camera, renderer, worldRoot };
}

==================== FILE: js\grass.js ====================
// =============================================================================
// grass.js â€” Forest-only PS3-era instanced grass with static frustum-culled
//            world chunks, GPU distance clumping, and wind/trample shaders.
// =============================================================================
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { worldRoot, makeMat, camera } from './engine.js';
import { WORLD_BOUNDS, biomeAt, gameState, isInWater, player, terrainH, worldBoxes } from './state.js';

const CHUNKS_PER_AXIS = 8; // 8x8 static frustum-culled chunks across the map
const TUFT_SPACING = 1.25; // Lush forest floor spacing

const grassChunks = [];
let sharedGrassMat = null;
let boxBuckets = null;

const windUniform = { value: 0 };
const playerPosUniform = { value: new THREE.Vector3() };
const camPosUniform = { value: new THREE.Vector3() };
const densityUniform = { value: 1.0 };

const _sampleOut = { y: 0 };

// Deterministic 32-bit integer hash -> [0, 1)
function hash2D(ix, iz, salt) {
  let h = Math.imul(ix, 374761393) + Math.imul(iz, 668265263) + Math.imul(salt, 1442695041);
  h = (h ^ (h >>> 13)) >>> 0;
  h = Math.imul(h, 1274126177) >>> 0;
  return ((h ^ (h >>> 16)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
}

// Procedural PS3-style alpha-cutout grass tuft card (serrated blades + root AO gradient)
function buildPs3GrassCardTexture() {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = 64;
  c.height = 64;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, 64, 64);

  const blades = [
    { x: 7,  w: 6, topY: 24, lean: -5, shade: 0.84 },
    { x: 15, w: 7, topY: 11, lean: -3, shade: 0.95 },
    { x: 23, w: 8, topY: 4,  lean: -1, shade: 1.04 },
    { x: 32, w: 8, topY: 2,  lean: 1,  shade: 1.10 },
    { x: 41, w: 8, topY: 7,  lean: 3,  shade: 1.02 },
    { x: 49, w: 7, topY: 15, lean: 5,  shade: 0.92 },
    { x: 57, w: 6, topY: 25, lean: 6,  shade: 0.83 }
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < blades.length; i++) {
    const b = blades[i];
    const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 64, 0, b.topY);
    const r0 = Math.round(92 * b.shade);
    const g0 = Math.round(112 * b.shade);
    const b0 = Math.round(82 * b.shade);
    const r1 = Math.min(255, Math.round(218 * b.shade));
    const g1 = Math.min(255, Math.round(238 * b.shade));
    const b1 = Math.min(255, Math.round(186 * b.shade));

    grad.addColorStop(0.0, `rgb(${r0},${g0},${b0})`);
    grad.addColorStop(0.45, `rgb(${Math.round((r0 + r1) * 0.5)},${Math.round((g0 + g1) * 0.5)},${Math.round((b0 + b1) * 0.5)})`);
    grad.addColorStop(1.0, `rgb(${r1},${g1},${b1})`);

    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(b.x - b.w * 0.5, 64);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(b.x - b.w * 0.25 + b.lean * 0.4, (64 + b.topY) * 0.5, b.x + b.lean, b.topY);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(b.x + b.w * 0.25 + b.lean * 0.4, (64 + b.topY) * 0.5, b.x + b.w * 0.5, 64);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fill();
  }

  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.magFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;
  tex.minFilter = THREE.LinearMipmapLinearFilter;
  tex.needsUpdate = true;
  return tex;
}

// 3 crossed & slightly tilted quads forming a volumetric PS3 grass tuft
function createCrossedTuftGeometry() {
  const positions = [];
  const normals = [];
  const uvs = [];
  const indices = [];

  const planes = [
    { yaw: 0,                 tilt: 0.15, scaleW: 1.00, scaleH: 1.00 },
    { yaw: Math.PI / 3,       tilt: 0.19, scaleW: 0.95, scaleH: 0.93 },
    { yaw: (Math.PI * 2) / 3, tilt: 0.17, scaleW: 0.97, scaleH: 0.96 }
  ];

  const halfW = 0.27;
  const fullH = 0.70;

  for (let p = 0; p < planes.length; p++) {
    const { yaw, tilt, scaleW, scaleH } = planes[p];
    const cosY = Math.cos(yaw);
    const sinY = Math.sin(yaw);
    const w = halfW * scaleW;
    const h = fullH * scaleH;
    const leanZ = Math.sin(tilt) * h;

    const localPts = [
      [-w, 0, 0,             0, 0],
      [ w, 0, 0,             1, 0],
      [-w * 1.15, h, leanZ,  0, 1],
      [ w * 1.15, h, leanZ,  1, 1]
    ];

    const baseVert = p * 4;
    for (let v = 0; v < 4; v++) {
      const [lx, ly, lz, u, uvY] = localPts[v];
      const rx = lx * cosY + lz * sinY;
      const rz = -lx * sinY + lz * cosY;
      positions.push(rx, ly, rz);
      normals.push(sinY * 0.25, 0.92, cosY * 0.25);
      uvs.push(u, uvY);
    }

    indices.push(
      baseVert, baseVert + 1, baseVert + 2,
      baseVert + 2, baseVert + 1, baseVert + 3
    );
  }

  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
  geo.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(normals, 3));
  geo.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(uvs, 2));
  geo.setIndex(indices);
  return geo;
}

// Spatial hash of tree trunks, rocks, and cabins so grass never pokes through forest obstacles
function buildObstacleBuckets() {
  boxBuckets = new Map();
  const bucketSize = 16;
  for (let i = 0; i < worldBoxes.length; i++) {
    const b = worldBoxes[i];
    if ((b.maxX - b.minX) > 45 || (b.maxZ - b.minZ) > 45) continue;
    const x0 = Math.floor(b.minX / bucketSize);
    const x1 = Math.floor(b.maxX / bucketSize);
    const z0 = Math.floor(b.minZ / bucketSize);
    const z1 = Math.floor(b.maxZ / bucketSize);
    for (let cx = x0; cx <= x1; cx++) {
      for (let cz = z0; cz <= z1; cz++) {
        const key = `${cx},${cz}`;
        let list = boxBuckets.get(key);
        if (!list) {
          list = [];
          boxBuckets.set(key, list);
        }
        list.push(b);
      }
    }
  }
}

function isBlockedByObstacle(x, y, z) {
  if (!boxBuckets) return false;
  const key = `${Math.floor(x / 16)},${Math.floor(z / 16)}`;
  const list = boxBuckets.get(key);
  if (!list) return false;
  for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
    const b = list[i];
    if (
      x >= b.minX - 0.18 && x <= b.maxX + 0.18 &&
      z >= b.minZ - 0.18 && z <= b.maxZ + 0.18 &&
      y >= b.minY - 0.30 && y <= b.maxY + 0.30
    ) {
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

function sampleForestGrassPoint(wx, wz, out) {
  if (wx <= WORLD_BOUNDS.minX + 1.5 || wx >= WORLD_BOUNDS.maxX - 1.5) return false;
  if (wz <= WORLD_BOUNDS.minZ + 1.5 || wz >= WORLD_BOUNDS.maxZ - 1.5) return false;
  if (wx >= 164) return false;
  if (biomeAt(wx, wz) !== 'woods') return false;
  if (isInWater(wx, wz)) return false;

  const y = terrainH(wx, wz);

  // Skip near-vertical cliff faces
  const slopeX = Math.abs(terrainH(wx + 0.75, wz) - terrainH(wx - 0.75, wz));
  const slopeZ = Math.abs(terrainH(wx, wz + 0.75) - terrainH(wx, wz - 0.75));
  if (slopeX > 1.15 || slopeZ > 1.15) return false;

  if (isBlockedByObstacle(wx, y, wz)) return false;

  out.y = y;
  return true;
}

function createGrassMaterial() {
  const tuftTex = buildPs3GrassCardTexture();
  const mat = makeMat(0xffffff, {
    roughness: 0.85,
    metalness: 0.0,
    flatShading: false
  });
  mat.map = tuftTex;
  mat.alphaTest = 0.42;
  mat.side = THREE.DoubleSide;

  mat.onBeforeCompile = (shader) => {
    shader.uniforms.uWindTime = windUniform;
    shader.uniforms.uPlayerPos = playerPosUniform;
    shader.uniforms.uCamPos = camPosUniform;
    shader.uniforms.uDensity = densityUniform;

    shader.vertexShader =
      'attribute float aSeed;\n' +
      'uniform float uWindTime;\n' +
      'uniform vec3 uPlayerPos;\n' +
      'uniform vec3 uCamPos;\n' +
      'uniform float uDensity;\n' +
      'varying float vFarT;\n' +
      'varying float vCulled;\n' +
      shader.vertexShader;

    shader.vertexShader = shader.vertexShader.replace(
      '#include <begin_vertex>',
      `
      #include <begin_vertex>
      vFarT = 0.0;
      vCulled = 0.0;
      #ifdef USE_INSTANCING
        vec3 instPos = vec3(instanceMatrix[3][0], instanceMatrix[3][1], instanceMatrix[3][2]);
        float distToCam = length(instPos.xz - uCamPos.xz);

        // Transition from dense individual tufts (0-24m) to wide clumped meadow clusters (24m-520m)
        float farT = smoothstep(22.0, 95.0, distToCam);
        float horizonT = smoothstep(130.0, 440.0, distToCam);
        vFarT = farT;

        // Deterministic GPU density thinning so distant tufts merge into broader clumps
        float keepProb = uDensity * mix(1.0, mix(0.45, 0.22, horizonT), farT);
        if (aSeed > keepProb) {
          vCulled = 1.0;
          transformed = vec3(0.0);
        } else {
          float clumpWidth = 1.0 + farT * 1.45 + horizonT * 1.40;
          float clumpHeight = 1.0 + farT * 0.22 - horizonT * 0.12;
          transformed.xz *= clumpWidth;
          transformed.y *= clumpHeight;

          float bend = uv.y * uv.y;

          // PS3-style layered wind wave + gust ripple
          float windFade = 1.0 - horizonT * 0.75;
          float gust = sin(instPos.x * 0.22 + instPos.z * 0.18 + uWindTime * 1.45);
          float flutter = sin(instPos.x * 0.65 - instPos.z * 0.52 + uWindTime * 3.1) * 0.35;
          float windAmt = (gust + flutter) * 0.14 * bend * windFade;
          transformed.x += windAmt;
          transformed.z += windAmt * 0.65;

          // Interactive proximity parting when player or vehicle passes through
          vec2 toTuft = instPos.xz - uPlayerPos.xz;
          float dSq = dot(toTuft, toTuft);
          if (dSq < 2.56 && abs(instPos.y - uPlayerPos.y) < 2.2) {
            float d = max(0.25, sqrt(dSq));
            float push = (1.0 - d / 1.6) * 0.36 * bend;
            transformed.xz += (toTuft / d) * push;
            transformed.y -= push * 0.25;
          }
        }
      #endif
      `
    );

    shader.vertexShader = shader.vertexShader.replace(
      '#include <project_vertex>',
      `
      #include <project_vertex>
      if (vCulled > 0.5) {
        gl_Position = vec4(2.0, 2.0, 2.0, 1.0);
      }
      `
    );

    shader.fragmentShader =
      'varying float vFarT;\n' +
      shader.fragmentShader;

    shader.fragmentShader = shader.fragmentShader.replace(
      '#include <alphatest_fragment>',
      `
      diffuseColor.a *= (1.0 + vFarT * 0.45);
      #include <alphatest_fragment>
      if (vFarT > 0.01) {
        vec3 forestTurfCol = vec3(0.32, 0.56, 0.26);
        diffuseColor.rgb = mix(diffuseColor.rgb, forestTurfCol, vFarT * 0.32);
      }
      `
    );
  };

  return mat;
}

export function initGrass() {
  for (let i = 0; i < grassChunks.length; i++) {
    worldRoot.remove(grassChunks[i]);
    grassChunks[i].geometry.dispose();
  }
  grassChunks.length = 0;

  buildObstacleBuckets();

  const baseGeo = createCrossedTuftGeometry();
  if (!sharedGrassMat) {
    sharedGrassMat = createGrassMaterial();
  }

  const minX = Number.isFinite(WORLD_BOUNDS?.minX) ? WORLD_BOUNDS.minX : -260;
  const maxX = Number.isFinite(WORLD_BOUNDS?.maxX) ? WORLD_BOUNDS.maxX : 260;
  const minZ = Number.isFinite(WORLD_BOUNDS?.minZ) ? WORLD_BOUNDS.minZ : -260;
  const maxZ = Number.isFinite(WORLD_BOUNDS?.maxZ) ? WORLD_BOUNDS.maxZ : 260;

  const chunkW = (maxX - minX) / CHUNKS_PER_AXIS;
  const chunkD = (maxZ - minZ) / CHUNKS_PER_AXIS;

  for (let cz = 0; cz < CHUNKS_PER_AXIS; cz++) {
    const cMinZ = minZ + cz * chunkD;
    const cMaxZ = cMinZ + chunkD;

    for (let cx = 0; cx < CHUNKS_PER_AXIS; cx++) {
      const cMinX = minX + cx * chunkW;
      const cMaxX = cMinX + chunkW;

      const xStart = Math.floor(cMinX / TUFT_SPACING);
      const xEnd = Math.floor(cMaxX / TUFT_SPACING);
      const zStart = Math.floor(cMinZ / TUFT_SPACING);
      const zEnd = Math.floor(cMaxZ / TUFT_SPACING);

      const maxCells = Math.max(1, (xEnd - xStart + 1) * (zEnd - zStart + 1));
      const matrices = new Float32Array(maxCells * 16);
      const colors = new Float32Array(maxCells * 3);
      const seeds = new Float32Array(maxCells);

      let count = 0;
      let bMinX = Infinity, bMinY = Infinity, bMinZ = Infinity;
      let bMaxX = -Infinity, bMaxY = -Infinity, bMaxZ = -Infinity;

      for (let gz = zStart; gz < zEnd; gz++) {
        for (let gx = xStart; gx < xEnd; gx++) {
          const wx = (gx + hash2D(gx, gz, 1)) * TUFT_SPACING;
          const wz = (gz + hash2D(gx, gz, 2)) * TUFT_SPACING;
          if (wx < cMinX || wx >= cMaxX || wz < cMinZ || wz >= cMaxZ) continue;

          if (!sampleForestGrassPoint(wx, wz, _sampleOut)) continue;

          const rSize = hash2D(gx, gz, 3);
          const seed = hash2D(gx, gz, 4);
          const heightScale = 0.64 + rSize * 0.68;
          const widthScale = 0.92 + rSize * 0.52;
          const yaw = rSize * Math.PI * 2;
          const wy = _sampleOut.y - 0.02;

          const cos = Math.cos(yaw) * widthScale;
          const sin = Math.sin(yaw) * widthScale;
          const off = count * 16;

          matrices[off]      = cos;
          matrices[off + 1]  = 0;
          matrices[off + 2]  = -sin;
          matrices[off + 3]  = 0;
          matrices[off + 4]  = 0;
          matrices[off + 5]  = heightScale;
          matrices[off + 6]  = 0;
          matrices[off + 7]  = 0;
          matrices[off + 8]  = sin;
          matrices[off + 9]  = 0;
          matrices[off + 10] = cos;
          matrices[off + 11] = 0;
          matrices[off + 12] = wx;
          matrices[off + 13] = wy;
          matrices[off + 14] = wz;
          matrices[off + 15] = 1;

          // Rich woodland emerald & sun-dappled moss variations
          const cOff = count * 3;
          colors[cOff]     = 0.42 + rSize * 0.18;
          colors[cOff + 1] = 0.74 + rSize * 0.18;
          colors[cOff + 2] = 0.34 + rSize * 0.13;

          seeds[count] = seed;
          count++;

          if (wx < bMinX) bMinX = wx;
          if (wx > bMaxX) bMaxX = wx;
          if (wy < bMinY) bMinY = wy;
          if (wy + heightScale > bMaxY) bMaxY = wy + heightScale;
          if (wz < bMinZ) bMinZ = wz;
          if (wz > bMaxZ) bMaxZ = wz;
        }
      }

      if (count === 0) continue;

      const chunkGeo = baseGeo.clone();
      chunkGeo.setAttribute('aSeed', new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(seeds.subarray(0, count), 1));

      const pad = 1.6;
      chunkGeo.boundingBox = new THREE.Box3(
        new THREE.Vector3(bMinX - pad, bMinY - 0.2, bMinZ - pad),
        new THREE.Vector3(bMaxX + pad, bMaxY + 0.5, bMaxZ + pad)
      );
      chunkGeo.boundingSphere = new THREE.Sphere();
      chunkGeo.boundingBox.getBoundingSphere(chunkGeo.boundingSphere);

      const mesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(chunkGeo, sharedGrassMat, count);
      mesh.instanceMatrix = new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(matrices.subarray(0, count * 16), 16);
      mesh.instanceColor = new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(colors.subarray(0, count * 3), 3);
      mesh.frustumCulled = true;
      mesh.castShadow = false;
      mesh.receiveShadow = true;

      worldRoot.add(mesh);
      grassChunks.push(mesh);
    }
  }

  baseGeo.dispose();
  updateGrass(0);
  return grassChunks;
}

export function updateGrass(elapsed) {
  if (grassChunks.length === 0) return;

  windUniform.value = elapsed;
  playerPosUniform.value.set(player.x, player.y, player.z);
  if (camera) {
    camPosUniform.value.copy(camera.position);
  } else {
    camPosUniform.value.set(player.x, player.y, player.z);
  }

  const tier = gameState.settings.graphics || 'medium';
  densityUniform.value = tier === 'low' ? 0.50 : tier === 'high' ? 1.0 : 0.78;
}

==================== FILE: js\guatemala.js ====================
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { addWorldBox, enterableZones, hooks, mapShapes, npcs, player } from './state.js';
import { worldRoot } from './engine.js';

const BLUE = 0x4997d0;
const WHITE = 0xf8fafc;

  'Â¡Que viva la fiesta!',
  'My abuela stitched these colors by hand.',
  'Walk with us â€” it is a celebration!',
  'Blue and white forever! Â¡Guatemala!'
];
const CAMPERO_LINES = [
  'The line is long, but the chicken is worth it.',
  'Two-piece combo with a side of frijoles, por favor.',
  'Pollo Campero tastes like home.',
  'I have been craving this all week!',
  'Extra crispy! Always extra crispy.'
];
const festival = { built: false, parade: [], homers: [], floats: [], musicians: [], lead: 70, confettiTimer: 0, musicTimer: 0, musicStep: 0 };
const materialCache = new Map();
const random = (min, max) => min + Math.random() * (max - min);
const choose = (items) => items[Math.floor(Math.random() * items.length)];
const material = (color, options = {}) => {
const key = `${color}:${options.emissive || 0}`;
if (!materialCache.has(key)) {
const m = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color, roughness: 0.8, ...options });
m.userData.shared = true;
materialCache.set(key, m);
}
return materialCache.get(key);
};
const flagTexture = new THREE.TextureLoader().load('assets/textures/chapinflag.png');
flagTexture.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
const flagMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
  map: flagTexture,
  roughness: 0.75,
  side: THREE.DoubleSide
});


function box(parent, width, height, depth, mat, x, y, z, shadow = false) {
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(width, height, depth), mat);
  mesh.position.set(x, y, z);
  mesh.castShadow = shadow;
  mesh.receiveShadow = shadow;
  parent.add(mesh);
  return mesh;
}

function cylinder(parent, top, bottom, height, mat, x, y, z, segments = 10) {
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(top, bottom, height, segments), mat);
  mesh.position.set(x, y, z);
  parent.add(mesh);
  return mesh;
}

function sphere(parent, radius, mat, x, y, z, sx = 1, sy = 1, sz = 1) {
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(radius, 14, 10), mat);
  mesh.position.set(x, y, z);
  mesh.scale.set(sx, sy, sz);
  parent.add(mesh);
  return mesh;
}

function makeFlag(width = 0.75, height = width * (267 / 400)) {
  const flag = new THREE.Group();
  const cloth = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.BoxGeometry(width, height, 0.02),
    flagMaterial
  );
  flag.add(cloth);
  return flag;
}


function decorateFestivalNpc(mesh, npc) {
  const data = mesh.userData;
  const head = data.face.parent;
  if (npc.gtLook === 'woman') {
	for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) box(data.torso, 0.455, 0.045, 0.235, material(PALETTE[(npc.id + i * 2) % PALETTE.length]), 0, 0.14 - i * 0.12, 0);
	const skirt = box(data.hip, 0.5, 0.5, 0.32, material(npc.gtSkirt), 0, -0.2, 0);
	box(skirt, 0.51, 0.06, 0.33, material(PALETTE[(npc.id + 3) % PALETTE.length]), 0, -0.1, 0);
	box(skirt, 0.51, 0.04, 0.33, material(PALETTE[(npc.id + 5) % PALETTE.length]), 0, 0.12, 0);
	box(head, 0.07, 0.36, 0.07, material(0x0b0b0d), 0, -0.16, -0.14);
  } else if (npc.gtHat === 'straw') {
	cylinder(head, 0.27, 0.27, 0.025, material(0xd9b66b), 0, 0.14, 0, 14);
	cylinder(head, 0.14, 0.15, 0.12, material(0xc9a24f), 0, 0.2, 0, 12);
	cylinder(head, 0.151, 0.151, 0.03, material(BLUE), 0, 0.165, 0, 12);
  } else if (npc.gtHat === 'cap') {
	box(head, 0.27, 0.07, 0.27, material(BLUE), 0, 0.14, 0);
	box(head, 0.2, 0.03, 0.14, material(BLUE), 0, 0.12, 0.2);
  }
  if (npc.gtSash) {
	const sash = box(data.torso, 0.58, 0.07, 0.24, material(npc.gtSash), 0, 0.02, 0);
	sash.rotation.z = 0.85;
  }
  if (npc.gtRole === 'flag') {
	box(data.hip, 0.035, 1.5, 0.035, material(0x7c5a35), 0.31, 0.42, 0.16);
	const flag = makeFlag();
	flag.position.set(0.685, 1.0, 0.16);
	data.hip.add(flag);
	data.flag = flag;
  }
}

function addFestivalNpc(def, category) {
  const id = npcs.length;
  const npc = {
	id, name: def.name, role: 'stationary', aggro: 'passive', clanId: null,
	homeX: def.x, homeZ: def.z, x: def.x, z: def.z, yaw: def.yaw || 0,
	shirt: def.shirt, pants: def.pants, bandana: null, line: `"${def.line}"`,
	age: 'adult', skin: def.skin, hair: def.hair, wallet: 0, picked: false, pickCool: 0,
	fleeTimer: 0, kvx: 0, kvz: 0, mesh: null, spawned: false, walkCycle: Math.random() * 6,
	turnTimer: 3, stunTimer: 0, hostile: false, alertState: 'patrol', searchX: def.x,
	searchZ: def.z, searchTimer: 0, attackCooldown: 0, punchTimer: 0, punchSide: 0,
	hp: 100, maxHp: 100, asleep: false, sleepTimer: 0, sleepZGroup: null,
	scale: def.scale || 1, gtFestival: true, gtCategory: category, gtLook: def.look || 'man', gtHat: def.hat,
	gtSkirt: def.skirt, gtSash: def.sash, gtRole: def.gtRole, gtHome: { x: def.x, z: def.z }, gtYaw: def.yaw || 0
  };
  npcs.push(npc);
  if (category === 'parade') festival.parade.push(npc);
  else festival.homers.push(npc);
  return npc;
}

const PR = 8;
const STRAIGHT = 32;
const ARC = Math.PI / 2 * PR;
const LOOP = 4 * STRAIGHT + 4 * ARC;
const SEGMENTS = [
  { line: true, x: -16, z: -24, dx: 1, dz: 0 }, { cx: 16, cz: -16, a0: -Math.PI / 2 },
  { line: true, x: 24, z: -16, dx: 0, dz: 1 }, { cx: 16, cz: 16, a0: 0 },
  { line: true, x: 16, z: 24, dx: -1, dz: 0 }, { cx: -16, cz: 16, a0: Math.PI / 2 },
  { line: true, x: -24, z: 16, dx: 0, dz: -1 }, { cx: -16, cz: -16, a0: Math.PI }
];

function pathAt(distance) {
  let remaining = ((distance % LOOP) + LOOP) % LOOP;
  for (const segment of SEGMENTS) {
	const length = segment.line ? STRAIGHT : ARC;
	if (remaining <= length) {
	  if (segment.line) return { x: segment.x + segment.dx * remaining, z: segment.z + segment.dz * remaining, hx: segment.dx, hz: segment.dz };
	  const angle = segment.a0 + remaining / ARC * Math.PI / 2;
	  return { x: segment.cx + PR * Math.cos(angle), z: segment.cz + PR * Math.sin(angle), hx: -Math.sin(angle), hz: Math.cos(angle) };
	}
	remaining -= length;
  }
  return { x: -16, z: -24, hx: 1, hz: 0 };
}

function paradeSlot(npc) {
  const point = pathAt(festival.lead - 9 - npc.gtRow * 2.7 + npc.gtJitter);
  const lateral = (npc.gtColumn - 2) * 1.55;
  return { x: point.x - point.hz * lateral, z: point.z + point.hx * lateral, yaw: Math.atan2(point.hx, point.hz) };
}

function addFloatBase(group, bodyColor) {
  box(group, 3, 0.5, 6, material(bodyColor), 0, 0.95, 0, true);
  box(group, 3.1, 0.12, 6.1, material(WHITE), 0, 1.22, 0);
  for (const sx of [-1, 1]) for (const sz of [-1, 1]) {
	const wheel = cylinder(group, 0.45, 0.45, 0.3, material(0x111827), sx * 1.5, 0.45, sz * 2, 14);
	wheel.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
	const z = -2.8 + i * 0.7;
	const color = PALETTE[i % PALETTE.length];
	sphere(group, 0.17, material(color), -1.55, 1.3, z);
	sphere(group, 0.17, material(color), 1.55, 1.3, z);
  }
  for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
	const sign = new THREE.Group();
	sign.rotation.y = side * Math.PI / 2;
	sign.position.set(side * 1.53, 0.92, 0);
	createNeonSign(sign, 'FIESTA GUATEMALTECA', 0, 0, 0, '#1d6fb8', '#ffffff', 3.4, 0.7);
	group.add(sign);
  }
}

function buildQuetzalFloat() {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  addFloatBase(group, BLUE);
  box(group, 2.2, 0.5, 0.9, material(0x8b5a2b), 0, 1.55, 2.1, true);
  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) box(group, 0.15, 0.05, 0.7, material(i % 2 ? 0xc47f3a : 0xe0a35a), -0.98 + i * 0.178, 1.83, 2.1);
  cylinder(group, 0.12, 0.12, 2.2, material(0x6b4a2f), 0, 2.6, -0.4, 8).rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  cylinder(group, 0.1, 0.14, 1.3, material(0x6b4a2f), 0, 1.9, -0.4, 8);
  for (const [x, color] of [[-0.55, BLUE], [0.55, WHITE]]) {
	const musician = buildNpcMeshRaw(color, 0x1e293b, null, { id: 700 + festival.musicians.length, age: 'adult', scale: 0.85, skin: SKIN[0], hair: HAIR[0] });
	musician.position.set(x, 1.28, 1.35);
	group.add(musician);
	festival.musicians.push(musician);
  }
  sphere(group, 0.75, material(0x16a34a), 0, 3.15, -0.35, 1, 1, 1.35);
  sphere(group, 0.58, material(0xdc2626), 0, 2.95, 0.3, 1, 0.9, 0.9);
  sphere(group, 0.4, material(0x16a34a), 0, 3.8, 0.55);
  sphere(group, 0.22, material(0x16a34a), 0, 4.2, 0.4, 0.8, 1, 1.4);
  box(group, 0.1, 0.1, 0.28, material(0xfacc15), 0, 3.78, 0.95);
  sphere(group, 0.06, material(0x0b0b0d), 0.2, 3.9, 0.82);
  sphere(group, 0.06, material(0x0b0b0d), -0.2, 3.9, 0.82);
  for (let row = 0; row < 3; row++) for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
	const z = -1.2 - i * 0.55;
	const y = Math.max(1.35, 2.9 - Math.sin(i * 0.45) * 0.55 - i * 0.18);
	box(group, 0.2 - i * 0.012, 0.07, 0.62, material(row === 1 ? 0x0d9488 : 0x16a34a), (row - 1) * 0.28 + Math.sin(i * 0.7 + row) * 0.18, y, z);
  }
  box(group, 1.9, 0.6, 0.12, material(0x16a34a), 0, 1.15, -3.05);
  group.position.y = 0.18;
  worldRoot.add(group);
  return group;
}

function buildTikalFloat() {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  addFloatBase(group, 0x16a34a);
  const stone = material(0x9ca3a0), stone2 = material(0x7f8a86);
  [[2.6, 0.55], [2.1, 0.55], [1.6, 0.55], [1.1, 0.5]].forEach(([size, height], i) => box(group, size, height, size, i % 2 ? stone2 : stone, 0, 1.5 + i * 0.55 + height / 2 - 0.1, 0, true));
  box(group, 0.8, 0.8, 0.8, stone, 0, 3.95, 0, true);
  box(group, 0.3, 0.45, 0.04, material(0x111827), 0, 3.85, 0.42);
  box(group, 1, 0.18, 1, stone2, 0, 4.45, 0);
  for (const [x, z] of [[-1.3, -2.6], [1.3, -2.6], [-1.3, 2.6], [1.3, 2.6]]) {
	box(group, 0.04, 1.4, 0.04, material(0x7c5a35), x, 1.9, z);
	const flag = makeFlag(0.6, 0.38);
	flag.position.set(x + 0.3, 2.35, z);
	group.add(flag);
  }
  box(group, 0.06, 3.6, 0.06, material(0x7c5a35), 0, 3, -2.6);
  const bigFlag = makeFlag(2, 1.25);
  bigFlag.position.set(1, 4.2, -2.6);
  group.add(bigFlag);
  group.position.y = 0.18;
  worldRoot.add(group);
  return group;
}

function buildBunting() {
  const points = [[-30, -30], [30, -30], [30, 30], [-30, 30], [-18, -18], [18, -18], [18, 18], [-18, 18]];
  points.forEach(([x, z]) => cylinder(worldRoot, 0.07, 0.09, 5.4, material(0x475569), x, 2.9, z, 6));
  const lines = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
	lines.push([points[i], points[(i + 1) % 4]], [points[4 + i], points[4 + (i + 1) % 4]]);
  }
  const colors = [BLUE, WHITE, 0xfacc15, 0xdc2626, 0x16a34a, 0xf97316];
  for (const [[ax, az], [bx, bz]] of lines) {
	const dx = bx - ax, dz = bz - az, length = Math.hypot(dx, dz), angle = Math.atan2(dz, dx);
	const ropePoints = [];
	for (let i = 0; i <= 24; i++) {
	  const t = i / 24;
	  ropePoints.push(new THREE.Vector3(ax + dx * t, 5.1 - 0.55 * Math.sin(Math.PI * t), az + dz * t));
	}
	worldRoot.add(new THREE.Line(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(ropePoints), new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x111827 })));
	const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
	geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute([-0.26, 0, 0, 0.26, 0, 0, 0, -0.52, 0], 3));
	geometry.computeVertexNormals();
	const count = Math.floor(length / 1.5);
	for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
	  const t = (i + 0.5) / count;
	  const pennant = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: colors[i % colors.length], side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
	  pennant.position.set(ax + dx * t, 5.1 - 0.55 * Math.sin(Math.PI * t), az + dz * t);
	  pennant.rotation.y = -angle;
	  worldRoot.add(pennant);
	}
  }
}

function buildCampero() {
  const x0 = 49, x1 = 60, z0 = -10, z1 = -2, height = 4, thickness = 0.3, baseY = 0.2;
  const cx = (x0 + x1) / 2, cz = -6;
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  worldRoot.add(group);
  const cream = material(0xfff3dc), red = material(0xc8102e), yellow = material(0xffc72c);
  const floor = box(group, x1 - x0 - 0.2, 0.06, z1 - z0 - 0.2, material(0xf3efe6), cx, baseY + 0.03, cz, true);
  void floor;
  const addCollision = (minX, maxX, minY, maxY, minZ, maxZ, tag = 'campero') => addWorldBox(minX, maxX, minY, maxY, minZ, maxZ, tag, true);
  const wallX = (z, fromX, toX) => {
	const width = toX - fromX, mid = (fromX + toX) / 2;
	box(group, width, 0.9, thickness, cream, mid, baseY + 0.45, z, true);
	box(group, width, height - 2.9, thickness, red, mid, baseY + 2.9 + (height - 2.9) / 2, z, true);
	addCollision(fromX, toX, baseY, baseY + height, z - thickness / 2, z + thickness / 2);
  };
  const wallZ = (x, fromZ, toZ) => {
	const depth = toZ - fromZ, mid = (fromZ + toZ) / 2;
	box(group, thickness, 0.9, depth, cream, x, baseY + 0.45, mid, true);
	box(group, thickness, height - 0.9, depth, red, x, baseY + 0.9 + (height - 0.9) / 2, mid, true);
	addCollision(x - thickness / 2, x + thickness / 2, baseY, baseY + height, fromZ, toZ);
  };
  wallX(z0 + thickness / 2, x0, x1);
  wallX(z1 - thickness / 2, x0, x1);
  wallZ(x0 + thickness / 2, z0, z1);
  wallZ(x1 - thickness / 2, z0, cz - 1.3);
  wallZ(x1 - thickness / 2, cz + 1.3, z1);
  box(group, thickness, height - 0.9, 2.6, red, x1 - thickness / 2, baseY + 0.9 + (height - 0.9) / 2, cz, true);
  addCollision(x1 - thickness, x1, baseY + 2.9, baseY + height, cz - 1.3, cz + 1.3);
  box(group, 0.2, 2.9, 0.2, yellow, x1 - thickness / 2, baseY + 1.45, cz - 1.3);
  box(group, 0.2, 2.9, 0.2, yellow, x1 - thickness / 2, baseY + 1.45, cz + 1.3);
  box(group, x1 - x0 + 0.6, 0.3, z1 - z0 + 0.6, material(0x7f1d1d), cx, baseY + height + 0.15, cz, true);
  addCollision(x0 - 0.3, x1 + 0.3, baseY + height, baseY + height + 0.3, z0 - 0.3, z1 + 0.3, 'campero_roof');
  [-2.4, 2.4].forEach((dz) => box(group, 2.4, 0.05, 1, material(0xffffff, { emissive: 0xfff2cc, emissiveIntensity: 1.2 }), cx + 0.5, baseY + height - 0.05, cz + dz));
  const sign = new THREE.Group();
  sign.position.set(x1 + 0.12, baseY + height + 1.3, cz);
  sign.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
  createNeonSign(sign, 'POLLO CAMPERO', 0, 0, 0, '#ffc72c', '#b91c1c', 7.2, 1.5);
  group.add(sign);
  for (const z of [cz - 2.8, cz + 2.8]) box(group, 0.2, 0.9, 0.2, material(0xcbd5e1, { metalness: 0.8, roughness: 0.3 }), x1 + 0.12, baseY + height + 0.75, z);
  const chicken = new THREE.Group();
  chicken.position.set(cx + 1.5, baseY + height + 0.3, cz);
  chicken.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
  box(chicken, 0.6, 0.9, 0.6, material(0x7f1d1d), 0, 0.45, 0);
  sphere(chicken, 1, material(0xfafafa), 0, 1.9, 0, 1, 1, 1.25);
  sphere(chicken, 0.55, material(0xfafafa), 0, 2.95, 0.75);
  [-0.18, 0, 0.18].forEach((dx, i) => box(chicken, 0.15, 0.3 + (i === 1 ? 0.12 : 0), 0.15, material(0xdc2626), dx, 3.55 + (i === 1 ? 0.05 : 0), 0.75));
  box(chicken, 0.2, 0.14, 0.4, material(0xf59e0b), 0, 2.9, 1.25);
  box(chicken, 0.14, 0.3, 0.1, material(0xdc2626), 0, 2.62, 1.2);
  sphere(chicken, 0.08, material(0x0b0b0d), 0.28, 3.05, 1.15);
  sphere(chicken, 0.08, material(0x0b0b0d), -0.28, 3.05, 1.15);
  group.add(chicken);
  for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) box(group, 1.2, 0.1, 0.6, material(i % 2 ? 0xffc72c : 0xc8102e), x1 + 0.6, baseY + 3.15, cz - 1.8 + i * 0.6);
  box(group, 0.9, 1, 5.2, red, 51.3, baseY + 0.5, cz, true);
  box(group, 1.15, 0.08, 5.4, material(0xf5e6c8), 51.3, baseY + 1.04, cz);
  addCollision(50.8, 51.8, baseY, 1.5, cz - 2.6, cz + 2.6, 'campero_counter');
  createNeonSign(group, 'POLLO FRITO â€¢ COMBOS', x0 + thickness + 0.12, baseY + 2.7, cz, '#7f1d1d', '#ffd24a', 4.8, 0.9);
  const steel = material(0xcbd5e1, { metalness: 0.8, roughness: 0.3 });
  for (const [x, z] of [[55.2, -8.3], [57.8, -8.3], [55.2, -3.7], [57.8, -3.7]]) {
	cylinder(group, 0.55, 0.55, 0.06, red, x, baseY + 0.9, z, 14);
	cylinder(group, 0.07, 0.1, 0.88, steel, x, baseY + 0.44, z, 8);
	addCollision(x - 0.5, x + 0.5, baseY, baseY + 1.1, z - 0.5, z + 0.5, 'campero_table');
  }
  mapShapes.push({ x: cx, z: cz, w: x1 - x0, d: z1 - z0, color: '#facc15', border: '#b91c1c', enterable: false });
  enterableZones.push({ minX: x0 + 0.3, maxX: x1 - 0.3, minZ: z0 + 0.3, maxZ: z1 - 0.2, name: 'POLLO CAMPERO' });

  const home = (def) => addFestivalNpc({ ...def, gtHome: { x: def.x, z: def.z } }, 'campero');
  const man = (extra) => ({
	skin: choose(SKIN), hair: choose(HAIR), look: 'man', hat: choose(['cap', 'none', 'none']),
	shirt: choose([BLUE, WHITE, 0x16a34a, 0xf97316]), pants: choose([0x1e293b, 0x334155]), line: choose(CAMPERO_LINES), ...extra
  });
  const woman = (extra) => ({
	skin: choose(SKIN), hair: choose(HAIR), look: 'woman', skirt: choose([0x1d4ed8, 0x7f1d1d, 0x14532d, 0x6d28d9]),
	shirt: choose(PALETTE), pants: 0x1e293b, line: choose(CAMPERO_LINES), ...extra
  });
  home(woman({ name: 'CASHIER ROSA', x: 50.2, z: -7.4, yaw: Math.PI / 2, shirt: 0xc8102e, line: 'Welcome to Pollo Campero! What can I get you?' }));
  home(man({ name: 'FRY COOK LUIS', x: 50.2, z: -4.6, yaw: Math.PI / 2, shirt: 0xc8102e, hat: 'cap', line: 'Fresh out of the fryer! Be careful, it is hot.' }));
  home(man({ name: 'MARIO', x: 52.6, z: -7, yaw: -Math.PI / 2 }));
  home(woman({ name: 'LUCÃA', x: 52.6, z: -5.2, yaw: -Math.PI / 2 }));
  home(man({ name: 'ERICK', x: 54.25, z: -8.3, yaw: Math.PI / 2 }));
  home(woman({ name: 'CARMEN', x: 58.75, z: -8.3, yaw: -Math.PI / 2 }));
  home(man({ name: 'JOSÃ‰', x: 55.2, z: -4.65, yaw: 0 }));
  home(woman({ name: 'MIRIAM', x: 57.8, z: -4.65, yaw: 0 }));
  const queue = [[61.3, cz, -Math.PI / 2], [62.5, cz, -Math.PI / 2], [63.7, cz, -Math.PI / 2], [64.6, cz, -Math.PI / 2], [64.6, -7.2, 0], [64.6, -8.4, 0], [64.6, -9.6, 0], [64.6, -10.8, 0], [64.6, -12, 0]];
  queue.forEach(([x, z, yaw], i) => home((i % 2 ? woman : man)({ name: choose(i % 2 ? W_NAMES : M_NAMES), x, z, yaw })));
}

export function buildGuatemalanFestival() {
  if (festival.built) return;
  festival.built = true;
  for (let row = 0; row < 10; row++) for (let column = 0; column < 5; column++) {
	const woman = Math.random() < 0.45;
	const npc = addFestivalNpc({
	  name: choose(woman ? W_NAMES : M_NAMES), x: 0, z: 0, yaw: 0, scale: random(0.8, 0.89),
	  shirt: woman ? choose(PALETTE) : choose([BLUE, BLUE, WHITE, WHITE, 0xfacc15]),
	  pants: woman ? 0x1e293b : choose([0x1e293b, 0x334155, WHITE]), skin: choose(SKIN), hair: choose(HAIR),
	  look: woman ? 'woman' : 'man', skirt: choose([0x1d4ed8, 0x7f1d1d, 0x14532d, 0x6d28d9, 0x9a3412]),
	  hat: woman ? null : choose(['straw', 'cap', 'none', 'none']), sash: Math.random() < 0.3 ? BLUE : null,
	  gtRole: row % 3 === 0 && column % 2 === 0 ? 'flag' : null, line: choose(PARADE_LINES)
	}, 'parade');
	npc.gtRow = row;
	npc.gtColumn = column;
	npc.gtJitter = random(-0.35, 0.35);
	const slot = paradeSlot(npc);
	npc.x = npc.homeX = slot.x;
	npc.z = npc.homeZ = slot.z;
	npc.yaw = slot.yaw;
  }
  festival.floats.push(buildQuetzalFloat(), buildTikalFloat());
  buildBunting();
  buildCampero();
}

function placeFloat(group, distance) {
  const point = pathAt(distance);
  group.position.x = point.x;
  group.position.z = point.z;
  group.rotation.y = Math.atan2(point.hx, point.hz);
}

function busy(npc) {
  return npc.asleep || npc.hostile || npc.fleeTimer > 0 || npc.stunTimer > 0 || Math.abs(npc.kvx) + Math.abs(npc.kvz) > 0.3;
}

function animateWalk(npc, dt, fast) {
  const data = npc.mesh.userData;
  npc.walkCycle += dt * (fast ? 12 : 7.5);
  const swing = Math.sin(npc.walkCycle) * (fast ? 0.75 : 0.5);
  data.legL.rotation.x = swing;
  data.legR.rotation.x = -swing;
  data.armL.rotation.x = -swing * 0.75;
  data.armR.rotation.x = swing * 0.75;
  data.armL.rotation.z = 0;
  data.armR.rotation.z = 0;
}

export function updateGuatemalanFestival(dt, elapsed) {
  if (!festival.built) return;
  const guest = hooks.mpIsGuest();
  if (guest) {
    // Float positions derive from the host-synced parade lead (same pathAt math).
    festival.lead = hooks.mpFestivalLead();
  } else {
    festival.lead += 2 * dt;
  }
  placeFloat(festival.floats[0], festival.lead);
  placeFloat(festival.floats[1], festival.lead - 44);
  festival.musicians.forEach((musician, i) => {
    const data = musician.userData, swing = Math.sin(elapsed * 9 + i * 1.6);
    data.armL.rotation.x = -1.15 + swing * 0.45;
    data.armR.rotation.x = -1.15 - swing * 0.45;
  });
  // Parade + crowd NPC movement is host-only; guests receive it via the NPC stream.
  if (!guest) {
    for (const npc of festival.parade) {
      if (busy(npc)) continue;
      const slot = paradeSlot(npc);
      const dx = slot.x - npc.x, dz = slot.z - npc.z, distance = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
      const walking = distance > 0.35;
      if (walking) {
        const step = Math.min(distance, 6.2 * dt);
        npc.x += dx / distance * step;
        npc.z += dz / distance * step;
        npc.yaw = Math.atan2(dx, dz);
      } else {
        npc.x = slot.x;
        npc.z = slot.z;
        npc.yaw = slot.yaw;
      }
      npc.moving = walking;
      if (npc.mesh && npc.spawned) {
        npc.mesh.position.set(npc.x, 0.18, npc.z);
        npc.mesh.rotation.y = npc.yaw;
        animateWalk(npc, dt, walking);
        const data = npc.mesh.userData;
        if (data.flag) {
          data.armR.rotation.x = -0.55;
          data.flag.rotation.y = Math.sin(elapsed * 4 + npc.id) * 0.35;
        } else if (Math.sin(elapsed * 0.45 + npc.id * 1.9) > 0.82) {
          data.armL.rotation.x = -2.6 + Math.sin(elapsed * 9 + npc.id) * 0.35;
          data.armR.rotation.x = -2.6 + Math.cos(elapsed * 9 + npc.id) * 0.35;
        }
      }
    }
    for (const npc of festival.homers) {
      if (busy(npc) || !npc.mesh || !npc.spawned) continue;
      npc.x = npc.gtHome.x;
      npc.z = npc.gtHome.z;
      npc.yaw = npc.gtYaw;
      npc.mesh.position.set(npc.x, 0.18, npc.z);
      npc.mesh.rotation.y = npc.yaw;
    }
  } else {
    // Guests still wave the flags on their locally-rendered parade meshes.
    for (const npc of festival.parade) {
      if (!npc.mesh || !npc.spawned) continue;
      const data = npc.mesh.userData;
      if (data.flag) data.flag.rotation.y = Math.sin(elapsed * 4 + npc.id) * 0.35;
    }
  }
  festival.confettiTimer -= dt;
  if (festival.confettiTimer <= 0 && Math.hypot(player.x - festival.floats[0].position.x, player.z - festival.floats[0].position.z) < 90) {
    festival.confettiTimer = 0.3;
    const npc = choose(festival.parade);
    if (npc.mesh) spawnParticles(npc.x, 2.6, npc.z, choose([BLUE, WHITE, 0xfacc15, 0xdc2626, 0x16a34a, 0xf97316]), 4, 3.2);
    spawnParticles(festival.floats[0].position.x, 4.6, festival.floats[0].position.z, choose([0xfacc15, 0xdc2626, BLUE]), 3, 4);
  }
  festival.musicTimer -= dt;
  if (festival.musicTimer <= 0) {
    festival.musicTimer = 0.22;
    const distance = Math.hypot(player.x - festival.floats[0].position.x, player.z - festival.floats[0].position.z);
    if (distance < 75 && sfx.enabled && player.y > -1.2) {
      const scale = [293.66, 329.63, 369.99, 440, 493.88, 587.33];
      const pattern = [0, 2, 4, 2, 3, 2, 1, 2, 0, 2, 4, 5, 4, 3, 2, 1];
      const frequency = scale[pattern[festival.musicStep % pattern.length]];
      const volume = 0.07 * Math.pow(1 - distance / 75, 1.5);
      sfx.tone(frequency, frequency * 0.985, 0.3, 'sine', volume);
      sfx.tone(frequency * 3, frequency * 3, 0.1, 'sine', volume * 0.25);
      if (festival.musicStep % 4 === 0) sfx.tone(frequency / 2, frequency / 2.02, 0.4, 'triangle', volume * 0.7);
      festival.musicStep++;
    }
  }
}
// Net hooks: the host streams festival.lead; guests adopt it.
Object.assign(hooks, {
  mpFestivalLead: () => festival.lead,
  mpSetFestivalLead: (v) => { if (Number.isFinite(v)) festival.lead = v; },
});

export function decorateGuatemalanNpc(npc, mesh) {
  if (npc.gtFestival) decorateFestivalNpc(mesh, npc);
}

export function renderGuatemalanMap(ctx, canvas, centered) {
  if (!festival.built) return;
  const scale = centered ? 0.65 : 0.38;
  const refX = centered ? player.x : -29;
  const refZ = centered ? player.z : -190;
  const x = (wx) => canvas.width / 2 + (wx - refX) * scale;
  const y = (wz) => canvas.height / 2 + (wz - refZ) * scale;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#facc15';
  ctx.font = 'bold 9px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText('POLLO CAMPERO', x(54.5), y(-6));
  if (player.y < -1.2) return;
  for (let i = 0; i < festival.parade.length; i += 3) {
	const npc = festival.parade[i];
	ctx.fillStyle = i % 2 ? '#f8fafc' : '#4997d0';
	ctx.fillRect(x(npc.x) - 1, y(npc.z) - 1, 2.4, 2.4);
  }
  for (const group of festival.floats) {
	ctx.fillStyle = '#facc15';
	ctx.fillRect(x(group.position.x) - 2.5, y(group.position.z) - 2.5, 5, 5);
  }
}

Object.assign(hooks, {
  decorateNpc: (mesh, npc) => decorateGuatemalanNpc(npc, mesh),
  drawMapOverlay: (ctx, canvas, centered) => renderGuatemalanMap(ctx, canvas, centered)
});


==================== FILE: js\input.js ====================
// =============================================================================
// input.js â€” Keyboard, mouse look, pointer lock, weapon wheel and in-game hotkeys (call initInput() once from main.js).
// =============================================================================
import { levelData } from './levelData.js';
import { MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO, aura, climb, flashEdge, gameState, hooks, inputState, keys, manholeOpen, meteorDrop, npcs, player, pushFeed, sausageRefillZones, under } from './state.js';
import { canvas } from './engine.js';
import { sfx } from './audio.js';
import { useLeftHandItem } from './items.js';
import { enterVehicle, exitCurrentCar, getNearestVehicle, shiftVehicleGear, vehicles } from './vehicles.js';
import { findMantleTarget, getNearestElevatorPad, nearestManhole, startClimb, syncEquippedMeshes, triggerRightHandAttack } from './player.js';

export function applyLookDelta(dx, dy) {
  const mult = 0.0023 * gameState.settings.sensitivity * (gameState.isAiming ? 0.55 : 1.0);
  if (gameState.inCar && gameState.thirdPerson) {
    const vehicle = vehicles[gameState.activeCarIndex];
    if (vehicle) {
      vehicle.cameraYaw -= dx * mult;
      vehicle.cameraPitch = Math.max(-0.2, Math.min(1.05, vehicle.cameraPitch - dy * mult));
      vehicle.cameraLookIdle = 0;
    }
    return;
  }
  player.yaw -= dx * mult;
  player.pitch = Math.max(-1.42, Math.min(1.42, player.pitch - dy * mult));
}
/** Attach all DOM / window listeners. Call once from main.js (never from Studio). */
export function initInput() {

document.addEventListener('pointerlockchange', () => {
  const locked = document.pointerLockElement === canvas;
  document.getElementById('lock-hint').style.display = locked ? 'none' : 'block';
});

window.addEventListener('contextmenu', (e) => {
  if (gameState.screen === 'playing') {
    e.preventDefault();
  }
});

canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
  if (gameState.screen !== 'playing') return;
  if (gameState.inventoryOpen || gameState.mapOpen || gameState.settingsOpen) return;
  sfx.unlock();

  if (e.button === 2) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!gameState.inCar && !meteorDrop.active && gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'hotdog_shooter') {
      gameState.isAiming = !gameState.isAiming;
      sfx.uiClick();
      hooks.updateHUD();
    }
    return;
  }

  if ((!gameState.settings.mobileControls || navigator.maxTouchPoints === 0) && document.pointerLockElement !== canvas && canvas.requestPointerLock) {
    try {
      const res = canvas.requestPointerLock();
      if (res && res.catch) res.catch(() => {});
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  inputState.isDraggingLook = true;
  inputState.lastMouseX = e.clientX;
  inputState.lastMouseY = e.clientY;

  if (e.button === 0 && !meteorDrop.active) {
    inputState.isPrimaryFireHeld = true;
    if (gameState.inCar) sfx.horn();
    else triggerRightHandAttack();
  }
});

window.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
  if (gameState.screen !== 'playing') return;
  if (gameState.inventoryOpen || gameState.mapOpen || gameState.settingsOpen) return;

  if (document.pointerLockElement === canvas) {
    applyLookDelta(e.movementX || 0, e.movementY || 0);
  } else if (inputState.isDraggingLook) {
    applyLookDelta(e.clientX - inputState.lastMouseX, e.clientY - inputState.lastMouseY);
    inputState.lastMouseX = e.clientX;
    inputState.lastMouseY = e.clientY;
  }
});

window.addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => {
  if (e.button === 0) {
    inputState.isPrimaryFireHeld = false;
  }
  inputState.isDraggingLook = false;
});

window.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
  if (gameState.screen !== 'playing') return;
  if (gameState.inventoryOpen || gameState.mapOpen || gameState.settingsOpen || gameState.inCar || meteorDrop.active) return;
  hooks.cycleRightWeapon(e.deltaY > 0 ? 1 : -1);
}, { passive: true });

window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  const code = e.code;
  keys[code] = true;

  if (code === 'Tab' || code === 'Escape') {
    if (gameState.screen === 'playing') {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (gameState.settingsOpen) hooks.closeSettingsModal();
      else if (gameState.inventoryOpen) hooks.closeInventoryModal();
      else if (gameState.mapOpen) hooks.closeMapModal();
      else hooks.setScreen('paused');
      return;
    } else if (gameState.screen === 'paused') {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (gameState.settingsOpen) hooks.closeSettingsModal();
      else hooks.setScreen('playing');
      return;
    }
  }

  if (gameState.screen !== 'playing') return;
  sfx.unlock();

  if (code === 'KeyE') {
    if (gameState.mapOpen || gameState.settingsOpen) return;

    if (!gameState.inventoryOpen && !gameState.inCar && !meteorDrop.active) {
      for (const zone of sausageRefillZones) {
        if (Math.hypot(player.x - zone.x, player.z - zone.z) < 3.1 && Math.abs(player.y - zone.y) < 1.8) {
          if (!gameState.rightWeapons.includes('hotdog_shooter')) {
            gameState.rightWeapons.push('hotdog_shooter');
            gameState.activeRightWeapon = 'hotdog_shooter';
          }
          gameState.hotdogAmmo = MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO;
          sfx.pickup();
          flashEdge('rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.35)');
          pushFeed(`SAUSAGE HOPPER REFILLED (${MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO}/${MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO})!`, 'border-l-amber-400');
          syncEquippedMeshes();
          hooks.updateHUD();
          return;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!gameState.inventoryOpen && !gameState.inCar && !meteorDrop.active) {
      for (const npc of npcs) {
        if (!npc.spawned || npc.asleep) continue;
        if (Math.hypot(player.x - npc.x, player.z - npc.z) < 2.5 && Math.abs(player.y - 0.18) < 1.8) {
          const dlg = document.getElementById('npc-dialogue');
          document.getElementById('npc-dialogue-name').textContent = npc.name;
          document.getElementById('npc-dialogue-text').textContent = npc.line;
          dlg.style.display = 'block';
          gameState.dialogueTimer = 4.2;
          sfx.uiClick();
          return;
        }
      }
    }

    if (gameState.inventoryOpen) hooks.closeInventoryModal();
    else hooks.openInventoryModal();
    return;
  }

  if (code === 'KeyM') {
    if (gameState.mapOpen) hooks.closeMapModal();
    else hooks.openMapModal();
    return;
  }

  if (gameState.inventoryOpen || gameState.mapOpen || gameState.settingsOpen) return;

  if (code === 'KeyV') {
    gameState.thirdPerson = !gameState.thirdPerson;
    if (gameState.thirdPerson && !gameState.inCar) {
      gameState.isAiming = false;
    }
    sfx.uiClick();
    hooks.updateHUD();
  } else if (code === 'KeyQ') {
    hooks.cycleRightWeapon(1);
  } else if (code === 'KeyU') {
    if (!gameState.inCar && !meteorDrop.active) useLeftHandItem(gameState.activeLeftSlot);
  } else if (code === 'KeyC') {
    if (!gameState.inCar && !meteorDrop.active) {
      player.crouching = !player.crouching;
      sfx.uiClick();
    }
  } else if (code === 'Space') {
    if (gameState.inCar || player.mantle.active || meteorDrop.active) return;
    const mTarget = findMantleTarget();
    if (mTarget) {
      player.mantle.active = true;
      player.mantle.timer = 0;
      player.mantle.startX = player.x;
      player.mantle.startY = player.y;
      player.mantle.startZ = player.z;
      player.mantle.endX = mTarget.x;
      player.mantle.endY = mTarget.y;
      player.mantle.endZ = mTarget.z;
      player.vy = 0;
      player.grounded = false;
      player.crouching = false;
      sfx.mantle();
      pushFeed('MANTLED LEDGE', 'border-l-sky-400');
    } else if ((player.grounded || player.coyote > 0) && gameState.stamina >= 5) {
      player.vy = levelData.params.player.jumpForce;
      player.coyote = 0;
      player.grounded = false;
      player.peakAirY = player.y;
      gameState.stamina = Math.max(0, gameState.stamina - 5);
    }
  } else if (code === 'KeyI' && gameState.inCar) {
    shiftVehicleGear(1);
  } else if (code === 'KeyK' && gameState.inCar) {
    shiftVehicleGear(-1);
  } else if (code === 'KeyF') {
if (gameState.inCar) {
exitCurrentCar();
} else if (!climb.on) {
const u = under();
const mi = nearestManhole(u ? 3.2 : 2.4);
if (mi >= 0) {
if (u) { startClimb(1, mi); }
else if (!manholeOpen[mi]) {
manholeOpen[mi] = true; sfx.mantle();
pushFeed('MANHOLE COVER OPENED â€” [F] TO CLIMB DOWN', 'border-l-amber-400');
hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'manhole', i: mi });
}
else startClimb(-1, mi);
} else if (aura.ahF && aura.ahHint && aura.ahHint()) {
aura.ahF();
} else {
const elev = getNearestElevatorPad();
if (elev) {
player.x = elev.dest.x;
player.y = elev.dest.y;
player.z = elev.dest.z;
player.vy = 0;
player.grounded = true;
player.peakAirY = elev.dest.y;
sfx.elevatorDing();
flashEdge('rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.35)');
pushFeed(
elev.toRoof ? `ROOFTOP HELIPAD â€” ${elev.sk.name}` : `GROUND LOBBY â€” ${elev.sk.name}`,
'border-l-sky-400'
);
hooks.updateHUD();
} else {
const near = getNearestVehicle(4.2);
if (near) enterVehicle(near.idx);
}
}
}
} else if (['Digit1', 'Digit2', 'Digit3', 'Digit4', 'Digit5'].includes(code)) {
    gameState.activeLeftSlot = Number(code.replace('Digit', '')) - 1;
    syncEquippedMeshes();
    hooks.updateHUD();
  }
});

window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
  keys[e.code] = false;
});

window.addEventListener('blur', () => {
  for (const code of Object.keys(keys)) keys[code] = false;
  inputState.isDraggingLook = false;
  inputState.isPrimaryFireHeld = false;
});
}
