  cash: {
    id: 'cash',
    name: 'Cash Stack',
    stat: 'DEPOSIT +$100',
    desc: 'Strapped $100 bills. Press [U] to deposit into your cash wallet.',
    color: '#10b981',
    svg: `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" class="w-4 h-4" fill="currentColor"><path d="M3 6h18v12H3V6zm9 3a3 3 0 1 0 0 6 3 3 0 0 0 0-6z"/></svg>`
  }
};

export const crateMat = makeMat(0xc2853e, { texType: 'wood', repeatX: 1, repeatY: 1, bumpScale: 0.02 });
export const barrelMat = makeMat(0xb91c1c, { texType: 'corrugated', repeatX: 3, repeatY: 1, metalness: 0.80, roughness: 0.32 });
crateMat.userData.shared = true;
barrelMat.userData.shared = true;

export const partGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.16, 0.16, 0.16), partMats = {};

export function freeGeo(o) {
if (!o) return;
const seenGeo = new Set(), seenMat = new Set();
o.traverse((c) => {
if (c.geometry && !c.geometry.userData.shared && !seenGeo.has(c.geometry)) {
seenGeo.add(c.geometry);
c.geometry.dispose();
}
const mats = Array.isArray(c.material) ? c.material : (c.material ? [c.material] : []);
for (const m of mats) {
if (m && !m.userData.shared && !seenMat.has(m)) { seenMat.add(m); m.dispose(); }
}
});
}

export function spawnParticles(x, y, z, colorHex, count = 10, speed = 5.5) {
  const mat = partMats[colorHex] || (partMats[colorHex] = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: colorHex }));
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const m = new THREE.Mesh(partGeo, mat);
    m.position.set(x, y, z);
    worldRoot.add(m);
    particleBursts.push({
      mesh: m,
      vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * speed,
      vy: Math.random() * speed * 0.8 + 1.5,
      vz: (Math.random() - 0.5) * speed,
      life: 0.55 + Math.random() * 0.3
    });
  }
}

export function buildPickupMesh(type, id) {
  const g = new THREE.Group();

  let color = 0xf59e0b;
  let mesh;
  if (type === 'right') {
    if (id === 'bat') {
      mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.055, 0.032, 0.9, 8), makeMat(0xd97706, { texType: 'wood' }));
      mesh.rotation.z = 0.5;
      color = 0xd97706;
    } else if (id === 'crowbar') {
      mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, 0.85, 0.08), makeMat(0xef4444, { metalness: 0.85 }));
      mesh.rotation.z = 0.45;
      color = 0xef4444;
    } else if (id === 'pipe') {
      mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.05, 0.05, 0.85, 8), makeMat(0x94a3b8, { texType: 'brushedsteel', metalness: 0.9 }));
      mesh.rotation.z = 0.45;
      color = 0x94a3b8;
    } else if (id === 'hotdog_shooter') {
      const hdGun = new THREE.Group();
      const bunBody = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.11, 0.52, 6, 8), makeMat(0xd97706));
      bunBody.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
      const barrel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.07, 0.08, 0.65, 10), makeMat(0xdc2626, { metalness: 0.7 }));
      barrel.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
      barrel.position.y = 0.06;
      const tip = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.065, 8, 6), makeMat(0xb91c1c));
      tip.position.set(0, 0.06, 0.34);
      hdGun.add(bunBody, barrel, tip);
      mesh = hdGun;
      color = 0xfacc15;
    }
  } else {
    const itemDef = LEFT_ITEMS[id] || LEFT_ITEMS.cash;
    color = new THREE.Color(itemDef.color).getHex();
    mesh = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.42, 0.28, 0.32),
      makeMat(color, { emissive: color, emissiveIntensity: 0.3 })
    );
  }

  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.RingGeometry(0.45, 0.58, 16),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color, side: THREE.DoubleSide })
  );
  ring.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  ring.position.y = -0.50;

  g.add(mesh, ring);
  g.userData.pickupColor = color;
  return g;
}

// Deterministic network ids: every client builds static entities in the same
// order, so the shared counter produces identical ids everywhere. Dynamic
// (loot/drop) pickups are allocated by the HOST only; guests receive the id in
// the event (opts.netId) and never advance their counter for them. On host
// migration the digest carries the host's counter (setNetSeq).
let netSeq = 0;
export function getNetSeq() { return netSeq; }
export function setNetSeq(v) { netSeq = Math.max(netSeq, v | 0); }

export function spawnWorldPickup(type, id, x, y, z, opts = {}) {
  const g = buildPickupMesh(type, id);
  g.position.set(x, y + 0.55, z);
  if (opts.rot) g.rotation.set(opts.rot[0], opts.rot[1], opts.rot[2]);
  if (opts.scale) g.scale.set(opts.scale[0], opts.scale[1], opts.scale[2]);
  if (opts.decorate) opts.decorate(g);
  worldRoot.add(g);
  const rec = { type, id, x, y, z, group: g, baseY: y + 0.55, phase: Math.random() * 6, netId: opts.netId || ('n' + (netSeq++)) };
  worldPickups.push(rec);
  return rec;
}
// Smashable wooden crate. pos = floor point under the crate; rot/scale optional (Studio entities).
export function spawnSmashCrate(x, y, z, loot = null, opts = {}) {
  const holder = new THREE.Group();
  holder.position.set(x, y, z);
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.05, 1.05, 1.05), crateMat);
  mesh.position.y = 0.525;
  mesh.castShadow = true;
  mesh.receiveShadow = true;
  holder.add(mesh);
  if (opts.rot) holder.rotation.set(opts.rot[0], opts.rot[1], opts.rot[2]);
  if (opts.scale) holder.scale.set(opts.scale[0], opts.scale[1], opts.scale[2]);
  if (opts.decorate) opts.decorate(holder);
  worldRoot.add(holder);
  const b = uprightFootprint(holder, 0.53, 0.53, 1.05);
  const box = addWorldBox(b.minX, b.maxX, y, y + b.height, b.minZ, b.maxZ, 'crate', true);
  const rec = { x, y, z, hp: 35, mesh: holder, box, loot, netId: 'n' + (netSeq++) };
  smashableCrates.push(rec);
  return rec;
}
// Explosive hazard barrel. Same pos/rot/scale convention as crates.
export function spawnHazardBarrel(x, y, z, opts = {}) {
  const holder = new THREE.Group();
  holder.position.set(x, y, z);
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.42, 0.42, 1.15, 10), barrelMat);
  mesh.position.y = 0.575;
  mesh.castShadow = true;
  holder.add(mesh);
  if (opts.rot) holder.rotation.set(opts.rot[0], opts.rot[1], opts.rot[2]);
  if (opts.scale) holder.scale.set(opts.scale[0], opts.scale[1], opts.scale[2]);
  if (opts.decorate) opts.decorate(holder);
  worldRoot.add(holder);
  const b = uprightFootprint(holder, 0.44, 0.44, 1.15);
  const box = addWorldBox(b.minX, b.maxX, y, y + b.height, b.minZ, b.maxZ, 'barrel', true);
  const rec = { x, y, z, hp: 30, mesh: holder, box, netId: 'n' + (netSeq++) };
  hazardBarrels.push(rec);
  return rec;
}

// ---- Shared destruction helpers (used by melee, projectiles and net events) ----
export function destroyCrate(rec, spawnLoot) {
  const i = smashableCrates.indexOf(rec);
  if (i < 0) return null;
  worldRoot.remove(rec.mesh);
  freeGeo(rec.mesh);
  const bi = worldBoxes.indexOf(rec.box);
  if (bi !== -1) worldBoxes.splice(bi, 1);
  smashableCrates.splice(i, 1);
  let lootRec = null;
  if (spawnLoot && rec.loot) lootRec = spawnWorldPickup(rec.loot.type, rec.loot.id, rec.x, rec.y, rec.z);
  return lootRec;
}
export function destroyBarrel(rec) {
  const i = hazardBarrels.indexOf(rec);
  if (i < 0) return false;
  worldRoot.remove(rec.mesh);
  freeGeo(rec.mesh);
  const bi = worldBoxes.indexOf(rec.box);
  if (bi !== -1) worldBoxes.splice(bi, 1);
  hazardBarrels.splice(i, 1);
  return true;
}
export function removePickupByNetId(netId) {
  const i = worldPickups.findIndex((p) => p.netId === netId);
  if (i < 0) return null;
  const rec = worldPickups[i];
  worldRoot.remove(rec.group);
  freeGeo(rec.group);
  worldPickups.splice(i, 1);
  return rec;
}
// Visual-only networked wiener (damage is resolved by the host's own copy).
export function spawnNetWiener(x, y, z, vx, vy, vz, by) {
  const wGroup = new THREE.Group();
  wGroup.position.set(x, y, z);
  const wienerMesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.068, 0.34, 5, 8), makeMat(0xb91c1c, { roughness: 0.32 }));
  wienerMesh.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  const mustardLine = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.024, 0.02, 0.32), makeMat(0xfacc15));
  mustardLine.position.y = 0.068;
  wGroup.add(wienerMesh, mustardLine);
  worldRoot.add(wGroup);
  projectileWieners.push({ mesh: wGroup, x, y, z, vx, vy, vz, life: 3.2, by, net: true });
}

export function buildRightWeaponMesh(id) {
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  if (id === 'bat') {
    const handle = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.024, 0.032, 0.24, 8), makeMat(0x1e293b));
    handle.position.y = 0.08;
    const barrel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.048, 0.026, 0.56, 8), makeMat(0xd97706, { texType: 'wood' }));
    barrel.position.y = 0.46;
    g.add(handle, barrel);
    g.rotation.x = -0.32;
  } else if (id === 'crowbar') {
    const shaft = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.024, 0.024, 0.64, 6), makeMat(0xb91c1c, { metalness: 0.85 }));
    shaft.position.y = 0.28;
    const hook = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.05, 0.05, 0.18), makeMat(0xdbeafe, { metalness: 0.9 }));
    hook.position.set(0, 0.60, -0.07);
    g.add(shaft, hook);
    g.rotation.x = -0.32;
  } else if (id === 'pipe') {
    const pipe = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.034, 0.034, 0.66, 8), makeMat(0x94a3b8, { texType: 'brushedsteel', metalness: 0.92 }));
    pipe.position.y = 0.30;
    const flange = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.048, 0.048, 0.08, 8), makeMat(0x475569, { metalness: 0.9 }));
    flange.position.y = 0.60;
    g.add(pipe, flange);
    g.rotation.x = -0.32;
  } else if (id === 'hotdog_shooter') {
    const bunMat = makeMat(0xd97706, { roughness: 0.58 });
    const redMat = makeMat(0xdc2626, { metalness: 0.72, roughness: 0.26 });
    const yellowMat = makeMat(0xfacc15, { roughness: 0.35 });
    const steelMat = makeMat(0xe2e8f0, { metalness: 0.92, roughness: 0.18 });

    const bunL = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.068, 0.36, 6, 8), bunMat);
    bunL.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    bunL.position.set(-0.042, 0.02, -0.14);

    const bunR = bunL.clone();
    bunR.position.x = 0.042;

    const barrel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.052, 0.062, 0.54, 10), redMat);
    barrel.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    barrel.position.set(0, 0.055, -0.20);

    const mustardStripe = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.025, 0.018, 0.48), yellowMat);
    mustardStripe.position.set(0, 0.112, -0.20);

    const muzzle = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.064, 0.064, 0.06, 12), steelMat);
    muzzle.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    muzzle.position.set(0, 0.055, -0.47);

    const loadedTip = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.048, 8, 6), makeMat(0xb91c1c));
    loadedTip.position.set(0, 0.055, -0.49);

    const scope = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.024, 0.024, 0.22, 8), makeMat(0x0f172a, { metalness: 0.85 }));
    scope.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    scope.position.set(0, 0.145, -0.14);

    g.add(bunL, bunR, barrel, mustardStripe, muzzle, loadedTip, scope);
  }
  return g;
}

export function buildLeftItemMesh(id) {
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  if (id === 'medkit') {
    const box = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.18, 0.14, 0.09), makeMat(0xf8fafc));
    const c1 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, 0.026, 0.10), makeMat(0xef4444));
    const c2 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.026, 0.08, 0.10), makeMat(0xef4444));
    g.add(box, c1, c2);
  } else if (id === 'soda') {
    const can = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.042, 0.042, 0.14, 10), makeMat(0x0284c7, { metalness: 0.85 }));
    const rim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.044, 0.044, 0.02, 10), makeMat(0xe2e8f0, { metalness: 0.9 }));
    rim.position.y = 0.065;
    g.add(can, rim);
  } else if (id === 'burger') {
    const bunTop = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.055, 0.072, 0.045, 10), makeMat(0xd97706));
    bunTop.position.y = 0.032;
    const patty = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.074, 0.074, 0.028, 10), makeMat(0x451a03));
    const bunBot = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.07, 0.065, 0.03, 10), makeMat(0xd97706));
    bunBot.position.y = -0.028;
    g.add(bunTop, patty, bunBot);
  } else if (id === 'flashlight') {
    const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.028, 0.028, 0.22, 8), makeMat(0x1e293b, { metalness: 0.8 }));
    body.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.038, 0.032, 0.05, 8), makeMat(0xfef08a, { emissive: 0xfacc15, emissiveIntensity: 0.6 }));
    lens.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    lens.position.z = -0.12;
    g.add(body, lens);
  } else if (id === 'cash') {
    const stack = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.16, 0.05, 0.09), makeMat(0x10b981));
    const band = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.03, 0.054, 0.094), makeMat(0xfef3c7));
    g.add(stack, band);
  }
  return g;
}

export function fireBallisticWiener() {
  if (gameState.hotdogAmmo <= 0) {
    sfx.uiClick();
    pushFeed('OUT OF SAUSAGES! REFILL AT AURA HOTDOGS OR PLAZA CART', 'border-l-rose-500');
    return;
  }
  gameState.hotdogAmmo--;
  sfx.wienerShot();
  const dir = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -1).applyQuaternion(camera.quaternion).normalize();
  const muzzlePos = new THREE.Vector3();
  if (gameState.thirdPerson || gameState.isAiming) {
    muzzlePos.set(player.x, player.y + 1.48, player.z).addScaledVector(dir, 0.9);
  } else {
    hooks.getRightMountWorldPosition(muzzlePos);
    muzzlePos.addScaledVector(dir, 0.45);
  }
  const wGroup = new THREE.Group();
  wGroup.position.copy(muzzlePos);
  const wienerMesh = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.068, 0.34, 5, 8),
    makeMat(0xb91c1c, { roughness: 0.32 })
  );
  wienerMesh.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  const mustardLine = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.024, 0.02, 0.32),
    makeMat(0xfacc15)
  );
  mustardLine.position.y = 0.068;
  wGroup.add(wienerMesh, mustardLine);
  worldRoot.add(wGroup);
  const launchSpeed = gameState.isAiming ? 68.0 : 54.0;
  const vx = dir.x * launchSpeed;
  const vy = dir.y * launchSpeed + 2.8;
  const vz = dir.z * launchSpeed;
  projectileWieners.push({
    mesh: wGroup,
    x: muzzlePos.x, y: muzzlePos.y, z: muzzlePos.z,
    vx, vy, vz,
    life: 3.2,
    by: hooks.mpLocalId()
  });
  // Everyone sees the shot; the host's copy resolves all damage.
  hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'wiener', x: muzzlePos.x, y: muzzlePos.y, z: muzzlePos.z, vx, vy, vz });
  hooks.updateHUD();
}

export function updateProjectileWieners(dt) {
  const GRAVITY = 15.8;
  const authority = !hooks.mpIsGuest(); // host + singleplayer resolve damage; guests get FX only
  for (let i = projectileWieners.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const w = projectileWieners[i];
    w.life -= dt;
    w.vy -= GRAVITY * dt;
    w.x += w.vx * dt;
    w.y += w.vy * dt;
    w.z += w.vz * dt;
    w.mesh.position.set(w.x, w.y, w.z);
    w.mesh.lookAt(w.x + w.vx, w.y + w.vy, w.z + w.vz);
    let impacted = false;
    for (let n = 0; n < npcs.length; n++) {
      const npc = npcs[n];
      if (!npc.spawned || npc.asleep) continue;
      if (Math.hypot(w.x - npc.x, w.z - npc.z) < 0.72 && w.y >= 0.18 && w.y <= 1.95) {
        if (authority) {
          hooks.damageNpc(npc, 48, Math.atan2(w.vx, w.vz));
          if (!npc.asleep) pushFeed(`WIENER HIT ${npc.name}!`, 'border-l-amber-400');
        }
        sfx.wienerSplat();
        spawnParticles(w.x, w.y, w.z, 0xb91c1c, 8, 5.5);
        spawnParticles(w.x, w.y, w.z, 0xfacc15, 5, 4.5);
        impacted = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!impacted) {
      for (let c = smashableCrates.length - 1; c >= 0; c--) {
        const cr = smashableCrates[c];
        if (Math.abs(w.x - cr.x) < 0.72 && Math.abs(w.z - cr.z) < 0.72 && w.y >= cr.y && w.y <= cr.y + 1.15) {
          if (authority) {
            sfx.smash();
            spawnParticles(cr.x, cr.y + 0.55, cr.z, 0xd97706, 12, 6.5);
            const lootRec = destroyCrate(cr, true);
            hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'crate_dead', netId: cr.netId, x: cr.x, y: cr.y, z: cr.z,
              loot: lootRec ? { netId: lootRec.netId, type: lootRec.type, id: lootRec.id, x: lootRec.x, y: lootRec.y, z: lootRec.z } : null });
            pushFeed('CRATE SMASHED BY WIENER!', 'border-l-amber-400');
          } else {
            hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'crate_hit', netId: cr.netId, a: 999 });
            spawnParticles(w.x, w.y, w.z, 0xd97706, 6, 4.5);
          }
          impacted = true;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!impacted) {
      for (let b = hazardBarrels.length - 1; b >= 0; b--) {
        const br = hazardBarrels[b];
        if (Math.hypot(w.x - br.x, w.z - br.z) < 0.65 && w.y >= br.y && w.y <= br.y + 1.25) {
          if (authority) {
            sfx.smash();
            spawnParticles(br.x, br.y + 0.6, br.z, 0xf97316, 18, 9.0);
            destroyBarrel(br);
            hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'barrel_dead', netId: br.netId, x: br.x, y: br.y, z: br.z });
            if (Math.hypot(player.x - br.x, player.z - br.z) < 4.2) hooks.applyPlayerDamage(24, 'EXPLOSIVE BARREL');
            pushFeed('HAZARD BARREL DETONATED!', 'border-l-orange-500');
          } else {
            hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'barrel_hit', netId: br.netId, a: 999 });
            spawnParticles(w.x, w.y, w.z, 0xf97316, 8, 5);
          }
          impacted = true;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!impacted) {
      for (const lp of lampPosts) {
        if (lp.state === 'standing' && Math.hypot(w.x - lp.x, w.z - lp.z) < 0.45 && w.y >= 0.18 && w.y <= 4.6) {
          if (authority) hooks.knockOverLamppost(lp, Math.atan2(w.vx, w.vz), 8.5);
          else hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'lamp_hit', i: lampPosts.indexOf(lp), yaw: Math.atan2(w.vx, w.vz), speed: 8.5 });
          impacted = true;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!impacted && authority) {
      // Remote players (host resolves; the victim's client applies via 'hit').
      for (const t of hooks.mpTargets()) {
        if (t.inCar) continue;
        if (Math.hypot(w.x - t.x, w.z - t.z) < 0.75 && w.y >= t.y && w.y <= t.y + 1.95) {
          hooks.mpHitPlayer(t.id, 48, 'WIENER STRIKE');
          sfx.wienerSplat();
          spawnParticles(w.x, w.y, w.z, 0xb91c1c, 8, 5.5);
          impacted = true;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!impacted) {
      if (w.y <= 0.18) {
        impacted = true;
        sfx.wienerSplat();
        spawnParticles(w.x, 0.24, w.z, 0xb91c1c, 6, 4.2);
        spawnParticles(w.x, 0.24, w.z, 0xfacc15, 4, 3.5);
      } else {
        for (let k = 0; k < worldBoxes.length; k++) {
          const wb = worldBoxes[k];
          if (w.x >= wb.minX && w.x <= wb.maxX && w.z >= wb.minZ && w.z <= wb.maxZ && w.y >= wb.minY && w.y <= wb.maxY) {
            impacted = true;
            sfx.wienerSplat();
            spawnParticles(w.x, w.y, w.z, 0xb91c1c, 6, 4.2);
            spawnParticles(w.x, w.y, w.z, 0xfacc15, 4, 3.5);
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    if (impacted || w.life <= 0) {
      worldRoot.remove(w.mesh);
      freeGeo(w.mesh);
      projectileWieners.splice(i, 1);
    }
  }
}

export function useLeftHandItem(slotIndex = gameState.activeLeftSlot) {
  const slot = gameState.leftHotbar[slotIndex];
  if (!slot) return;
  const item = LEFT_ITEMS[slot.id];
  if (!item) return;

  player.leftUseTimer = 0.32;

  if (item.id === 'flashlight') {
    gameState.flashlightOn = !gameState.flashlightOn;
    sfx.uiClick();
    hooks.syncEquippedMeshes();
    pushFeed(`FLASHLIGHT ${gameState.flashlightOn ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}`, 'border-l-yellow-400');
    return;
  }

  if (item.id === 'medkit') {
    if (gameState.hp >= gameState.maxHp) {
      pushFeed('HEALTH ALREADY FULL', 'border-l-slate-400');
      return;
    }
    gameState.hp = Math.min(gameState.maxHp, gameState.hp + 40);
    sfx.heal();
    flashEdge('rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35)');
    pushFeed('USED MEDKIT (+40 HEALTH)', 'border-l-emerald-400');
  } else if (item.id === 'soda') {
    gameState.stamina = Math.min(100, gameState.stamina + 65);
    gameState.speedBoostTimer = 7.5;
    sfx.heal();
    flashEdge('rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.35)');
    pushFeed('DRANK ENERGY DRINK (+STAMINA & SPEED)', 'border-l-sky-400');
  } else if (item.id === 'burger') {
    gameState.hp = Math.min(gameState.maxHp, gameState.hp + 20);
    gameState.stamina = Math.min(100, gameState.stamina + 20);
    sfx.heal();
    flashEdge('rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.32)');
    pushFeed('ATE STREET BURGER (+20 HP / +20 STAM)', 'border-l-amber-400');
  } else if (item.id === 'cash') {
    gameState.cash += 100;
    sfx.pickup();
    flashEdge('rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.32)');
    pushFeed('DEPOSITED +$100 CASH', 'border-l-emerald-400');
  }

  slot.count -= 1;
  if (slot.count <= 0) {
    gameState.leftHotbar[slotIndex] = null;
  }
  hooks.syncEquippedMeshes();
  hooks.updateHUD();
  if (gameState.inventoryOpen) hooks.renderInventoryModal();
}

export function addLeftItemToBag(id, qty = 1) {
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    if (gameState.leftHotbar[i] && gameState.leftHotbar[i].id === id) {
      gameState.leftHotbar[i].count += qty;
      return true;
    }
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    if (!gameState.leftHotbar[i]) {
      gameState.leftHotbar[i] = { id, count: qty };
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

export function dropLeftSlot(slotIndex) {
  const slot = gameState.leftHotbar[slotIndex];
  if (!slot) return;
  const fwdX = -Math.sin(player.yaw);
  const fwdZ = -Math.cos(player.yaw);
  const dx = player.x + fwdX * 1.4, dz = player.z + fwdZ * 1.4;
  if (hooks.mpIsGuest()) {
    // Host allocates the network id and spawns it for everyone (incl. us).
    hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'pickup_drop', type: 'left', id: slot.id, x: dx, y: player.y, z: dz });
  } else {
    const rec = spawnWorldPickup('left', slot.id, dx, player.y, dz);
    hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'pickup_spawn', netId: rec.netId, type: 'left', id: slot.id, x: dx, y: player.y, z: dz });
  }
  slot.count -= 1;
  if (slot.count <= 0) gameState.leftHotbar[slotIndex] = null;
  sfx.uiClick();
  hooks.syncEquippedMeshes();
  hooks.updateHUD();
  if (gameState.inventoryOpen) hooks.renderInventoryModal();
}
// Pure visual builders (no registration) used by Studio previews and entity tabs.
export function buildCrateMesh() {
  const holder = new THREE.Group();
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.05, 1.05, 1.05), crateMat);
  mesh.position.y = 0.525;
  mesh.castShadow = true;
  mesh.receiveShadow = true;
  holder.add(mesh);
  return holder;
}

export function buildBarrelMesh() {
  const holder = new THREE.Group();
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.42, 0.42, 1.15, 10), barrelMat);
  mesh.position.y = 0.575;
  mesh.castShadow = true;
  holder.add(mesh);
  return holder;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Entity type catalogue (used by the world spawner and by Studio's Toolbox).
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
export const ENTITY_TYPES = ['vehicle', 'pickup', 'crate', 'barrel', 'ramp', 'block', 'prop', 'building', 'road'];

/** Create a fresh entity of the given prefab with a unique id. */
export function makeEntity(prefab, pos = [0, 0, 0], rot = [0, 0, 0]) {
  const base = { id: '', type: prefab.type, name: prefab.name, pos: [...pos], rot: [...rot], scale: [...(prefab.scale || [1, 1, 1])], texture: '', texRepeat: [1, 1], color: prefab.color || '' };
  const extra = structuredClone(prefab.extra || {});
  return Object.assign(base, extra);
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Load / save helpers
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
function isPlainObject(v) { return v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v); }

/** Deep-merge saved data over defaults so older saves keep working. */
function mergeDefaults(def, saved) {
  if (Array.isArray(def)) return Array.isArray(saved) ? structuredClone(saved) : structuredClone(def);
  if (!isPlainObject(def)) return saved === undefined ? def : saved;
  const out = {};
  for (const k of Object.keys(def)) out[k] = mergeDefaults(def[k], isPlainObject(saved) ? saved[k] : undefined);
  if (isPlainObject(saved)) for (const k of Object.keys(saved)) if (!(k in out)) out[k] = structuredClone(saved[k]);
  return out;
}

/** Reads localStorage override if valid JSON exists, else a clone of the defaults. */
export function loadLevelData() {
  try {
    const raw = typeof localStorage !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem(LEVEL_STORAGE_KEY) : null;
    if (raw) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (isPlainObject(parsed) && isPlainObject(parsed.params) && Array.isArray(parsed.entities)) {
        const merged = mergeDefaults(DEFAULT_LEVEL_DATA, parsed);
        merged.entities = structuredClone(parsed.entities);
        return merged;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[levelData] ignoring invalid saved level:', err);
  }
  return structuredClone(DEFAULT_LEVEL_DATA);
}

/** Live singleton shared by every module (mutated in place by Studio). */
export const levelData = loadLevelData();

const TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE = 2;

function terrainCell(heights, x, z) {
  return Number(heights[`${x},${z}`]) || 0;
}

/** Bilinearly sample the saved terrain height offset at world coordinates. */
export function terrainHeightOffset(x, z, data = levelData) {
  const heights = data.terrain && data.terrain.heights;
  if (!heights) return 0;
  const gx = x / TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE, gz = z / TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE;
  const x0 = Math.floor(gx), z0 = Math.floor(gz);
  const tx = gx - x0, tz = gz - z0;
  const a = terrainCell(heights, x0, z0), b = terrainCell(heights, x0 + 1, z0);
  const c = terrainCell(heights, x0, z0 + 1), d = terrainCell(heights, x0 + 1, z0 + 1);
  return (a + (b - a) * tx) * (1 - tz) + (c + (d - c) * tx) * tz;
}

/** Raise, lower, or smooth the saved terrain heightfield with a soft circular brush. */
export function sculptTerrain(data, centerX, centerZ, radius, strength, mode = 'raise', baseHeightAt = () => 0) {
  if (!data.terrain || typeof data.terrain !== 'object') data.terrain = { cellSize: TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE, heights: {} };
  if (!data.terrain.heights || typeof data.terrain.heights !== 'object') data.terrain.heights = {};
  const heights = data.terrain.heights;
  const minX = Math.floor((centerX - radius) / TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE);
  const maxX = Math.ceil((centerX + radius) / TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE);
  const minZ = Math.floor((centerZ - radius) / TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE);
  const maxZ = Math.ceil((centerZ + radius) / TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE);
  const before = mode === 'smooth' ? { ...heights } : heights;
  const read = (x, z) => terrainCell(before, x, z);
  for (let iz = minZ; iz <= maxZ; iz++) {
    for (let ix = minX; ix <= maxX; ix++) {
      const distance = Math.hypot(ix * TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE - centerX, iz * TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE - centerZ);
      if (distance >= radius) continue;
      const t = 1 - distance / radius;
      const falloff = t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
      const key = `${ix},${iz}`;
      const current = read(ix, iz);
      let next;
      if (mode === 'smooth') {
        let total = 0;
        for (let dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++) {
          for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
            const sx = (ix + dx) * TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE, sz = (iz + dz) * TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE;
            total += baseHeightAt(sx, sz) + read(ix + dx, iz + dz);
          }
        }
        const currentHeight = baseHeightAt(ix * TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE, iz * TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE) + current;
        next = current + (total / 9 - currentHeight) * Math.min(1, strength * falloff);
      } else {
        next = current + (mode === 'lower' ? -1 : 1) * strength * falloff;
      }
      next = Math.round(next * 1000) / 1000;
      if (Math.abs(next) < 0.001) delete heights[key];
      else heights[key] = next;
    }
  }
}

/** Replace the contents of the live singleton while keeping its identity. */
export function replaceLevelData(next) {
  for (const k of Object.keys(levelData)) delete levelData[k];
  Object.assign(levelData, structuredClone(next));
  return levelData;
}

export function saveLevelData(data = levelData) {
  localStorage.setItem(LEVEL_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
}

export function clearSavedLevelData() {
  localStorage.removeItem(LEVEL_STORAGE_KEY);
}

/** "#rrggbb" | number -> number (0xrrggbb). Empty/invalid -> fallback. */
export function colorToHex(c, fallback = 0xffffff) {
  if (typeof c === 'number') return c;
  if (typeof c === 'string' && /^#?[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(c.trim())) return parseInt(c.trim().replace('#', ''), 16);
  return fallback;
}

/** number -> "#rrggbb" */
export function hexToCss(n) {
  return '#' + (Math.max(0, Math.min(0xffffff, n | 0))).toString(16).padStart(6, '0');
}

let _idCounter = 0;
/** Unique entity id inside the given data set. */
export function nextEntityId(prefix, data = levelData) {
  const used = new Set(data.entities.map((e) => e.id));
  let id;
  do { _idCounter++; id = `${prefix}_${Date.now().toString(36)}${_idCounter}`; } while (used.has(id));
  return id;
}

import { sfx } from './audio.js';
import { gameState, hooks } from './state.js';
import { buildWorld } from './world.js';
import { initGrass, updateGrass } from './grass.js';
import { buildSewerRats } from './npcs.js';
import { syncEquippedMeshes, applyLevelParams } from './player.js';
import { initInput } from './input.js';
import { initUI, updateHUD, renderMapCanvas } from './ui.js';
import { initMultiplayer, sendMultiplayerState, updateRemotePlayers, updateRemoteWorld } from './multiplayer.js';
import { initMobileControls } from './mobile.js';
import { buildGuatemalanFestival, updateGuatemalanFestival } from './guatemala.js';
import { buildCityTraffic, updateCityTraffic } from './traffic.js';
import { initAircraft, updateAircraft } from './aircraft.js';

let lastTime = performance.now();
let minimapTimer = 0;
let minimapCanvas = null;
let windowFocused = true;

function animate(now) {
  requestAnimationFrame(animate);
  const dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - lastTime) / 1000);
  lastTime = now;
  const elapsed = now * 0.001;
  hooks.syncMobileControls();

  if (animatedWaterTex) {
    animatedWaterTex.offset.x = (elapsed * 0.08) % 1;
    animatedWaterTex.offset.y = (elapsed * 0.05) % 1;
  }

  if (gameState.screen === 'start') {
    const ang = elapsed * 0.16;
    camera.position.set(Math.cos(ang) * 76, 36 + Math.sin(elapsed * 0.4) * 3, Math.sin(ang) * 76 - 24);
    camera.lookAt(0, 6, -32);
  } else if (gameState.screen === 'playing' && windowFocused && !document.hidden) {
    updateAircraft(dt, elapsed);
    updateCityTraffic(dt);
    updateGuatemalanFestival(dt, elapsed);
    updateRemoteWorld(dt, elapsed); // guest: interpolate host cars + remote jet
    minimapTimer += dt;
    if (minimapTimer >= 0.05) {
      minimapTimer = 0;
      if (!minimapCanvas || !minimapCanvas.isConnected) {
        minimapCanvas = document.getElementById('minimap-canvas');
      }
      if (minimapCanvas) {
        renderMapCanvas(minimapCanvas, true);
      }
    }
    updateRemotePlayers(dt);
    sendMultiplayerState(performance.now());
  }

  sfx.updateMusic();
  skyGroup.position.copy(camera.position);
  if (skyGroup.children[2]?.isInstancedMesh) {
    skyGroup.children[2].rotation.y += dt * 0.0025;
  } else {
    for (let i = 2; i < skyGroup.children.length; i++) {
      const cloud = skyGroup.children[i];
      cloud.position.x += dt * 0.3;
      if (cloud.position.x > 260) cloud.position.x = -260;
    }
  }

  updateGrass(elapsed);
  updateSunLensFlare();
  updateWorldWobble(elapsed, 1.0);
  renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

async function updateLoadingProgress(percent, status) {
  const progress = document.getElementById('loading-progress');
  const percentLabel = document.getElementById('loading-percent');
  const statusLabel = document.getElementById('loading-status');
  const value = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(percent)));
  if (progress) progress.style.width = `${value}%`;
  if (percentLabel) percentLabel.textContent = `${value}%`;
  if (statusLabel) statusLabel.textContent = status;
  await new Promise((resolve) => requestAnimationFrame(resolve));
}

async function boot() {
  const loadingStarted = performance.now();
  const loadingScreen = document.getElementById('loading-screen');
  await updateLoadingProgress(5, 'Initializing graphics');
  await initEngine(); // full-screen canvas (#game-canvas)
  await updateLoadingProgress(18, 'Loading game data');
  applyLevelParams(levelData);
  preloadRegisteredTextures(levelData);
  preloadBoundAudio(levelData);
  await updateLoadingProgress(30, 'Building Sunnyvale');

  // World: static city/freeway/desert/biomes -> levelData.entities -> Aura Hills overhaul
  buildWorld(levelData, {
    register: true,
    afterStatic: () => applyGraphicsTier('medium')
  });
  initGrass();
  await updateLoadingProgress(72, 'Sunnyvale built');
  buildGuatemalanFestival();
  buildCityTraffic();
  initAircraft();
  buildSewerRats();
  await updateLoadingProgress(84, 'Preparing game systems');

  syncEquippedMeshes();
  initUI();
  initMultiplayer();
  initMobileControls();
  initInput();
  updateHUD();
  await updateLoadingProgress(96, 'Finalizing');

  window.addEventListener('blur', () => {
    windowFocused = false;
  });
  window.addEventListener('focus', () => {
    windowFocused = true;
    lastTime = performance.now();
  });

  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
    lastTime = performance.now();
    if (document.hidden && sfx.ctx && sfx.ctx.state === 'running') sfx.ctx.suspend().catch(() => {});
    else if (!document.hidden && sfx.ctx && gameState.screen === 'playing') sfx.ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
  });

  window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    resizeRenderer();
  });

  requestAnimationFrame(animate);
  const minimumLoadingTime = 500;
  const remainingLoadingTime = minimumLoadingTime - (performance.now() - loadingStarted);
  if (remainingLoadingTime > 0) {
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, remainingLoadingTime));
  }
  await updateLoadingProgress(100, 'Ready');
  loadingScreen.classList.add('is-hidden');
}

boot().catch((err) => {
  console.error(err);
  const loadingScreen = document.getElementById('loading-screen');
  if (loadingScreen) loadingScreen.classList.add('is-hidden');
  const banner = document.getElementById('error-banner');
  if (banner) {
    banner.textContent = `Initialization error: ${err.message}. Please refresh to retry.`;
    banner.style.display = 'block';
  }
});

==================== FILE: js\mobile.js ====================
// =============================================================================
// mobile.js â€” Touch controls for phones/tablets ONLY.
// Fixes vs original:
//  * Device detection: a touchscreen Chromebook (maxTouchPoints > 0 but a fine,
//    hover-capable pointer exists) is NOT a phone. Touch UI is available there
//    via the settings toggle, but never auto-enabled, and the compact
//    'mobile-device' HUD layout is only applied to phone-like devices.
//  * setHeldKey is source-aware: releasing a touch source can NEVER write
//    keys[code] = false over a physical keyboard press (the 120ms WASD stomp).
//  * syncVisibility only releases fire/look that TOUCH actually owns.
// =============================================================================
import { gameState, hooks, inputState, keys, pushFeed } from './state.js';
import { canvas } from './engine.js';
import { sfx } from './audio.js';
import { cycleRightWeapon, openInventoryModal, openMapModal, setScreen, updateHUD } from './ui.js';
import { exitCurrentCar, GEAR_LABELS, shiftVehicleGear, vehicles } from './vehicles.js';
import { syncEquippedMeshes, triggerRightHandAttack } from './player.js';
import { useLeftHandItem } from './items.js';
import { applyLookDelta } from './input.js';
import { applyJetLookDelta, boardJet, dropNuke, isJetFlying, isNearJet, leaveJet, setJetMobileKey } from './aircraft.js';

const MOBILE_PREF_KEY = 'gtaAura.mobileControls.v1';
const heldKeys = new Map();          // code -> Set of TOUCH sources only
const activeActions = new Map();
let touchCapable = false;            // controls exist (any touchscreen)
let phoneLike = false;               // compact HUD + auto-enable default
let root = null;
let lookPointer = null;
let lookX = 0, lookY = 0;
let stickPointer = null;
let lastSync = 0;
let touchFireHeld = false;           // did TOUCH press the fire button?

function setHeldKey(code, down, source) {
  let sources = heldKeys.get(code);
  if (down) {
    if (!sources) { sources = new Set(); heldKeys.set(code, sources); }
    sources.add(source);
    keys[code] = true;
    return;
  }
  // Releasing: if no touch source ever held this key, DO NOT touch keys[code] â€”
  // it may be held by the physical keyboard right now.
  if (!sources) return;
  sources.delete(source);
  if (sources.size) { keys[code] = true; return; }
  heldKeys.delete(code);
  keys[code] = false;
}

function createControls() {
  root = document.createElement('div');
  root.id = 'mobile-controls';
  root.innerHTML = `
<div id="mobile-look" aria-label="Drag to look"><span class="mobile-look-hint">DRAG TO LOOK</span></div>
<div class="mobile-tools"><button class="mobile-btn mobile-fullscreen" data-action="fullscreen" aria-label="Toggle fullscreen">FULL</button><button class="mobile-btn primary" data-action="pause">PAUSE</button></div>
<div class="mobile-stick-wrap" id="mobile-stick" aria-label="Movement joystick"><div id="mobile-stick-thumb"></div><div class="mobile-stick-label">MOVE</div></div>
<div class="mobile-actions" id="mobile-foot-actions">
<button class="mobile-btn primary" data-action="fire">FIRE</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="jump">JUMP</button><button class="mobile-btn primary wide" id="mobile-interact" data-action="interact">USE</button>
<button class="mobile-btn" data-action="more" aria-expanded="false">MORE</button>
<div class="mobile-extra-actions hidden" id="mobile-extra-actions">
<button class="mobile-btn" data-action="map">MAP</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="bag">BAG</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="pov">POV</button>
<button class="mobile-btn" data-action="run">RUN</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="crouch">CROUCH</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="aim">AIM</button>
<button class="mobile-btn" data-action="slot-prev">ITEM â—€</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="item">USE ITEM</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="slot-next">ITEM â–¶</button>
<button class="mobile-btn" data-action="weapon">WEAPON</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="strike">STRIKE</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="auto">AUTO</button>
<button class="mobile-btn" data-action="pickpocket">SNEAK</button>
</div>
</div>
<div class="mobile-car-controls" id="mobile-car-controls">
<div class="mobile-shifter"><div class="mobile-gear-caption">SHIFTER</div><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="gear-up" aria-label="Shift up">â–²</button><div id="mobile-gear-label">1</div><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="gear-down" aria-label="Shift down">â–¼</button></div>
<div class="mobile-drive-pedals"><button class="mobile-btn primary" data-action="gas">GAS</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="brake">BRAKE</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="drift">DRIFT</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="horn">HORN</button><button class="mobile-btn wide" data-action="exit">EXIT</button></div>
</div>
<div class="mobile-jet-controls" id="mobile-jet-controls"><div class="mobile-jet-buttons">
<button class="mobile-btn" data-action="jet-throttle-up">THR +</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="jet-throttle-down">THR âˆ’</button><button class="mobile-btn primary" data-action="jet-afterburner">BOOST</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="jet-cannon">GUN</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="jet-nuke">NUKE</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="jet-pitch-up">PULL UP</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="jet-pitch-down">DIVE</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="jet-rudder-left" aria-label="Yaw left">YAW â—€</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="jet-rudder-right" aria-label="Yaw right">YAW â–¶</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="jet-camera">CAM</button><button class="mobile-btn" data-action="jet-invert">INVERT</button><button class="mobile-btn wide" data-action="jet-exit">EXIT JET</button>
</div></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(root);
}

function tapKey(code) {
  window.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { code, bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
  window.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keyup', { code, bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
}
function updateFullscreenButton() {
  const button = root && root.querySelector('[data-action="fullscreen"]');
  if (button) button.textContent = document.fullscreenElement ? 'EXIT FULL' : 'FULL';
}
async function toggleFullscreen() {
  try {
    if (document.fullscreenElement) await document.exitFullscreen();
    else if (document.documentElement.requestFullscreen) await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen();
    else { pushFeed('FULLSCREEN IS NOT AVAILABLE IN THIS BROWSER', 'border-l-rose-500'); return; }
  } catch (error) {
    pushFeed(`FULLSCREEN FAILED: ${error.message || 'BROWSER BLOCKED THE REQUEST'}`, 'border-l-rose-500');
  }
}
function interact() {
  if (isJetFlying()) { leaveJet(); return; }
  if (!gameState.inCar && boardJet()) return;
  if (gameState.inCar) { exitCurrentCar(); return; }
  const prompt = document.getElementById('interact-prompt');
  if (prompt && !prompt.classList.contains('hidden')) {
    tapKey(document.getElementById('interact-key').textContent.trim() === 'F' ? 'KeyF' : 'KeyE');
  } else if (!gameState.inventoryOpen && !gameState.mapOpen && !gameState.settingsOpen) {
    openInventoryModal();
  }
}
function onFootAction(action) {
  if (gameState.screen !== 'playing' && action !== 'pause') return;
  if (action === 'fullscreen') toggleFullscreen();
  else if (action === 'more') {
    const drawer = document.getElementById('mobile-extra-actions');
    const open = drawer.classList.toggle('hidden') === false;
    root.querySelector('[data-action="more"]').setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
  } else if (action === 'map') gameState.mapOpen ? hooks.closeMapModal() : openMapModal();
  else if (action === 'bag') gameState.inventoryOpen ? hooks.closeInventoryModal() : openInventoryModal();
  else if (action === 'pov') tapKey('KeyV');
  else if (action === 'pause') setScreen(gameState.screen === 'paused' ? 'playing' : 'paused');
  else if (action === 'jump') tapKey('Space');
  else if (action === 'interact' || action === 'exit') interact();
  else if (action === 'crouch') tapKey('KeyC');
  else if (action === 'aim' && gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'hotdog_shooter' && !gameState.inCar) {
    gameState.isAiming = !gameState.isAiming;
    updateHUD(true);
  } else if (action === 'item') useLeftHandItem();
  else if (action === 'slot-prev' || action === 'slot-next') {
    gameState.activeLeftSlot = (gameState.activeLeftSlot + (action === 'slot-next' ? 1 : 4)) % 5;
    syncEquippedMeshes();
    updateHUD(true);
  } else if (action === 'weapon') cycleRightWeapon(1);
  else if (action === 'strike') tapKey('KeyB');
  else if (action === 'auto') tapKey('KeyN');
  else if (action === 'gear-up') shiftVehicleGear(1);
  else if (action === 'gear-down') shiftVehicleGear(-1);
  else if (action === 'horn') { if (gameState.inCar) sfx.horn(); }
  else if (action === 'jet-nuke') dropNuke();
  else if (action === 'jet-exit') leaveJet();
  else if (action === 'jet-camera') tapKey('KeyV');
  else if (action === 'jet-invert') tapKey('KeyY');
}
function handleAction(action, down, pointerId) {
  const source = `pointer-${pointerId}`;
  const held = {
    gas: 'KeyW', brake: 'KeyS', drift: 'Space', run: 'ShiftLeft', pickpocket: 'KeyG',
    'jet-throttle-up': 'KeyW', 'jet-throttle-down': 'KeyS', 'jet-afterburner': 'ShiftLeft',
    'jet-pitch-up': 'ArrowUp', 'jet-pitch-down': 'ArrowDown',
    'jet-rudder-left': 'KeyQ', 'jet-rudder-right': 'KeyE'
  }[action];
  if (action.startsWith('jet-') && held) { setJetMobileKey(held, down); return; }
  if (held) { setHeldKey(held, down, source); return; }
  if (action === 'jet-cannon') { setJetMobileKey('M0', down); return; }
  if (action === 'fire') {
    if (isJetFlying()) setJetMobileKey('M0', down);
    else if (gameState.inCar) { if (down) sfx.horn(); }
    else if (gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'hotdog_shooter') {
      if (down) { touchFireHeld = true; inputState.isPrimaryFireHeld = true; }
      else if (touchFireHeld) { touchFireHeld = false; inputState.isPrimaryFireHeld = false; }
    } else if (down) triggerRightHandAttack();
    return;
  }
  if (down) onFootAction(action);
}
function updateJoystick(event) {
  const bounds = document.getElementById('mobile-stick').getBoundingClientRect();
  const dx = event.clientX - (bounds.left + bounds.width / 2);
  const dy = event.clientY - (bounds.top + bounds.height / 2);
  const limit = bounds.width * 0.34;
  const length = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
  const ratio = Math.min(1, limit / length);
  const x = dx * ratio, y = dy * ratio;
  document.getElementById('mobile-stick-thumb').style.transform = `translate(${x}px, ${y}px)`;
  const setDirection = (code, down) => {
    if (isJetFlying()) { setHeldKey(code, false, 'stick'); setJetMobileKey(code, down); }
    else { setJetMobileKey(code, false); setHeldKey(code, down, 'stick'); }
  };
  setDirection('KeyA', x < -limit * 0.22);
  setDirection('KeyD', x > limit * 0.22);
  setDirection('KeyW', y < -limit * 0.22);
  setDirection('KeyS', y > limit * 0.22);
}
function clearJoystick() {
  stickPointer = null;
  for (const code of ['KeyW', 'KeyA', 'KeyS', 'KeyD']) {
    setJetMobileKey(code, false);
    setHeldKey(code, false, 'stick'); // safe: never clears physical keyboard state
  }
  const thumb = document.getElementById('mobile-stick-thumb');
  if (thumb) thumb.style.transform = '';
}
function releaseTouchFire() {
  if (touchFireHeld) { touchFireHeld = false; inputState.isPrimaryFireHeld = false; }
  setJetMobileKey('M0', false);
}
function syncVisibility(force = false) {
  if (!touchCapable || !root) return;
  const now = performance.now();
  if (!force && now - lastSync < 120) return;
  lastSync = now;
  const visible = gameState.settings.mobileControls === true && gameState.screen === 'playing'
    && !gameState.inventoryOpen && !gameState.mapOpen && !gameState.settingsOpen;
  const jetFlying = isJetFlying();
  root.classList.toggle('visible', visible);
  const lockHint = document.getElementById('lock-hint');
  if (lockHint) lockHint.style.display = visible || document.pointerLockElement === canvas ? 'none' : 'block';
  document.getElementById('mobile-foot-actions').style.display = !gameState.inCar && !jetFlying ? 'grid' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('mobile-car-controls').classList.toggle('active', gameState.inCar && !jetFlying);
  document.getElementById('mobile-jet-controls').classList.toggle('active', jetFlying);
  document.querySelector('.mobile-stick-label').textContent = jetFlying ? 'ROLL / THROTTLE' : gameState.inCar ? 'STEER' : 'MOVE';
  const interactButton = document.getElementById('mobile-interact');
  const prompt = document.getElementById('interact-prompt');
  const promptText = document.getElementById('interact-text');
  const canBoardJet = !gameState.inCar && !jetFlying && isNearJet();
  interactButton.textContent = jetFlying ? 'EXIT JET' : gameState.inCar ? 'EXIT' : canBoardJet ? 'BOARD JET'
    : prompt && !prompt.classList.contains('hidden') ? (promptText.textContent.split(' ')[0] || 'USE') : 'USE';
  interactButton.disabled = !jetFlying && !gameState.inCar && !canBoardJet && (!prompt || prompt.classList.contains('hidden'));
  const aimButton = root.querySelector('[data-action="aim"]');
  aimButton.disabled = gameState.activeRightWeapon !== 'hotdog_shooter' || gameState.inCar;
  const vehicle = vehicles[gameState.activeCarIndex];
  document.getElementById('mobile-gear-label').textContent = vehicle ? GEAR_LABELS[vehicle.gearIndex] : '1';
  if (gameState.inCar || jetFlying || gameState.screen !== 'playing') {
    document.getElementById('mobile-extra-actions').classList.add('hidden');
    root.querySelector('[data-action="more"]').setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
  }
  updateFullscreenButton();
  if (!visible) {
    clearJoystick();
    releaseTouchFire();
    lookPointer = null;
    document.getElementById('mobile-look').classList.remove('active');
    for (const [pointerId, active] of activeActions) {
      handleAction(active.action, false, pointerId);
      active.button.classList.remove('pressed');
    }
    activeActions.clear();
  }
}
export function initMobileControls() {
  const mm = (q) => window.matchMedia?.(q).matches === true;
  const hasTouch = navigator.maxTouchPoints > 0;
  const coarse = mm('(pointer: coarse)');
  const anyFine = mm('(any-pointer: fine)');
  const noHover = mm('(hover: none)');
  // Phone/tablet: coarse primary pointer, nothing that hovers, no fine pointer anywhere.
  phoneLike = coarse && noHover && !anyFine;
  // Touch-capable laptops (Chromebooks!) get optional controls, never phone treatment.
  touchCapable = hasTouch || coarse;
  let stored = null;
  try { stored = localStorage.getItem(MOBILE_PREF_KEY); } catch (err) { console.warn('[mobile] unable to load touch preference:', err); }
  gameState.settings.mobileControls = stored === null ? phoneLike : stored === '1';
  if (!touchCapable) { hooks.syncMobileControls = () => {}; return; }
  if (phoneLike) document.documentElement.classList.add('mobile-device');
  createControls();
  document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', updateFullscreenButton);
  const look = document.getElementById('mobile-look');
  const stick = document.getElementById('mobile-stick');
  root.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
    const button = event.target.closest('[data-action]');
    if (!button || !gameState.settings.mobileControls) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    event.stopPropagation();
    const action = button.dataset.action;
    activeActions.set(event.pointerId, { action, button });
    button.classList.add('pressed');
    handleAction(action, true, event.pointerId);
    try { button.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId); } catch (_) { }
  });
  const endAction = event => {
    const active = activeActions.get(event.pointerId);
    if (!active) return;
    active.button.classList.remove('pressed');
    handleAction(active.action, false, event.pointerId);
    activeActions.delete(event.pointerId);
  };
  root.addEventListener('pointerup', endAction);
  root.addEventListener('pointercancel', endAction);
  root.addEventListener('lostpointercapture', endAction);
  stick.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
    if (!gameState.settings.mobileControls || gameState.screen !== 'playing') return;
    event.preventDefault();
    stickPointer = event.pointerId;
    updateJoystick(event);
    try { stick.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId); } catch (_) { }
  });
  window.addEventListener('pointermove', event => {
    if (stickPointer === event.pointerId) { event.preventDefault(); updateJoystick(event); }
    if (lookPointer === event.pointerId) {
      event.preventDefault();
      const dx = event.clientX - lookX, dy = event.clientY - lookY;
      lookX = event.clientX; lookY = event.clientY;
      if (isJetFlying()) applyJetLookDelta(dx * gameState.settings.sensitivity, dy * gameState.settings.sensitivity);
      else applyLookDelta(dx, dy);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
  stick.addEventListener('pointerup', event => { if (stickPointer === event.pointerId) clearJoystick(); });
  stick.addEventListener('pointercancel', event => { if (stickPointer === event.pointerId) clearJoystick(); });
  look.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
    if (!gameState.settings.mobileControls || gameState.screen !== 'playing'
      || gameState.inventoryOpen || gameState.mapOpen || gameState.settingsOpen) return;
    event.preventDefault();
    lookPointer = event.pointerId; lookX = event.clientX; lookY = event.clientY;
    look.classList.add('active');
    try { look.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId); } catch (_) { }
  });
  const endLook = event => {
    if (lookPointer === event.pointerId) { lookPointer = null; look.classList.remove('active'); }
  };
  look.addEventListener('pointerup', endLook);
  look.addEventListener('pointercancel', endLook);
  window.addEventListener('blur', () => {
    clearJoystick(); releaseTouchFire(); lookPointer = null;
    for (const [pointerId, active] of activeActions) {
      handleAction(active.action, false, pointerId);
      active.button.classList.remove('pressed');
    }
    activeActions.clear();
  });
  hooks.syncMobileControls = syncVisibility;
  hooks.persistMobilePreference = () => {
    try { localStorage.setItem(MOBILE_PREF_KEY, gameState.settings.mobileControls ? '1' : '0'); }
    catch (err) { console.warn('[mobile] unable to save touch preference:', err); }
    syncVisibility(true);
  };
  syncVisibility(true);
}

==================== FILE: js\multiplayer.js ====================
// =============================================================================
// multiplayer.js â€” PROTOCOL v2: host-authoritative world + join-order migration.
//
//  * ONE simulation: the host runs NPC AI, police, rats, traffic, festival,
//    destructibles and projectile damage. Guests interpolate an 8Hz world
//    stream (rendered ~120ms behind) and send intent events.
//  * Full digest on join / migration: NPC appearance+state, destroyed world
//    objects, live dynamic pickups, lamps, manholes, gate/safes, traffic flags,
//    car locks, police, rats, jet, wanted heat, parade lead, pickup id counter.
//  * Host migration: on host loss every client elects playerOrder's first
//    surviving joiner. That client re-registers the canonical ...-HOST PeerJS
//    id (retrying while the old id drains); everyone else reconnects + resyncs.
//  * All cross-module calls go through state.js hooks (no import cycles).
// =============================================================================
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { AO, MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO, aura, gameState, hooks, keys, lampPosts, manholeOpen, npcs, player, pushFeed, smashableCrates, hazardBarrels, worldPickups } from './state.js';
import { scene, worldRoot } from './engine.js';
import { levelData } from './levelData.js';
import { sfx } from './audio.js';
import { vehicles, enterVehicle } from './vehicles.js';
import { buildPlayerRigMesh, syncEquippedMeshes } from './player.js';
import { buildNpcMeshRaw, buildSleepZzzGroup, buildCopMesh, buildCopCarMesh, lootFor, damageNpcBase, hurtCop, killRat, clearWantedLevel, rats } from './npcs.js';
import { addLeftItemToBag, buildRightWeaponMesh, destroyCrate, destroyBarrel, freeGeo, getNetSeq, removePickupByNetId, setNetSeq, spawnNetWiener, spawnParticles, spawnWorldPickup } from './items.js';
import { knockOverLamppostBase } from './world.js';

const INTERP_DELAY = 120;      // ms behind the newest snapshot
const STREAM_MS = 125;         // 8Hz world stream
const PLAYER_MS = 50;          // 20Hz player state
const CAR_MS = 80;             // guest-driven car / jet transforms

export const multiplayer = {
  mode: 'single', peer: null, hostConnection: null, connections: new Map(),
  remotePlayers: new Map(), roomCode: '', localId: '1', hostId: '1', playerCount: 1,
  playerOrder: ['1'], carOwners: new Map(),
  lastPlayerSend: 0, lastWorldSend: 0, lastVehicleSend: 0, lastJetSend: 0,
  migrating: false, grantBypass: -1, worldVersion: 0, lastDigestReq: 0,
  remoteJet: null, remoteJetBuf: null,
};
const npcBuf = new Map();      // id -> {p0,p1,t0,t1,row}
const carBuf = new Map();      // idx -> {p0,p1,t0,t1,row}
const remoteCops = new Map();  // netId -> {mesh,buf}
const remotePcars = new Map(); // netId -> {mesh,lr,lb,buf}
const ratBuf = new Map();      // i -> {p0,p1,t0,t1,dead}
const pendingPickups = new Map(); // netId -> rec (guest take requests)
const hitCooldown = new Map(); // targetId -> last hit time
let lastStreamVersion = -1;

const r2 = (v) => Math.round(v * 100) / 100;
const r3 = (v) => Math.round(v * 1000) / 1000;
const isHost = () => multiplayer.mode === 'host';
const isGuest = () => multiplayer.mode === 'guest';
const isActive = () => multiplayer.mode !== 'single';
const lerpAngle = (a, b, t) => { let d = b - a; while (d > Math.PI) d -= Math.PI * 2; while (d < -Math.PI) d += Math.PI * 2; return a + d * t; };

function roomCode() {
  const chars = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789';
  let code = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) code += chars[Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length)];
  return code;
}
function updateHud() {
  const pill = document.getElementById('multiplayer-hud');
  const startBtn = document.getElementById('btn-start-play');
  const label = startBtn && startBtn.querySelector('span');
  if (label) label.textContent = isActive() ? 'ENTER CITY (ONLINE)' : 'SINGLEPLAYER';
  if (!pill) return;
  if (!isActive()) { pill.classList.add('hidden'); return; }
  pill.classList.remove('hidden');
  document.getElementById('mp-room-label').textContent = multiplayer.roomCode;
  const role = isHost() ? 'HOST' : 'GUEST';
  document.getElementById('mp-player-count').textContent =
    `${multiplayer.playerCount} PLAYER${multiplayer.playerCount === 1 ? '' : 'S'} Â· ${role}`;
}
function clearRemoteRender() {
  for (const rc of remoteCops.values()) { worldRoot.remove(rc.mesh); freeGeo(rc.mesh); }
  remoteCops.clear();
  for (const rc of remotePcars.values()) { worldRoot.remove(rc.mesh); rc.mesh.traverse(o => { if (o.geometry) o.geometry.dispose(); if (o.material) o.material.dispose(); }); }
  remotePcars.clear();
  npcBuf.clear(); carBuf.clear(); ratBuf.clear(); pendingPickups.clear();
  multiplayer.remoteJet = null; multiplayer.remoteJetBuf = null;
}
export function mpResetConnectionState() {
  try { multiplayer.peer?.destroy(); } catch (_) {}
  multiplayer.peer = null;
  multiplayer.mode = 'single';
  multiplayer.hostConnection = null;
  multiplayer.connections.clear();
  multiplayer.carOwners.clear();
  multiplayer.playerCount = 1;
  multiplayer.roomCode = '';
  multiplayer.localId = '1';
  multiplayer.hostId = '1';
  multiplayer.playerOrder = ['1'];
  multiplayer.migrating = false;
  multiplayer.grantBypass = -1;
  multiplayer.worldVersion = 0;
  lastStreamVersion = -1;
  for (const remote of multiplayer.remotePlayers.values()) {
    if (remote.rig) { scene.remove(remote.rig); freeGeo(remote.rig); }
    if (remote.tag) scene.remove(remote.tag);
  }
  multiplayer.remotePlayers.clear();
  clearRemoteRender();
  hooks.mpJetApplyRemote(null);
  updateHud();
}
function broadcast(data, exceptId = null) {
  for (const [id, conn] of multiplayer.connections) {
    if (id !== exceptId && conn.open) conn.send(data);
  }
}
function sendHost(data) {
  if (multiplayer.hostConnection?.open) multiplayer.hostConnection.send(data);
}
function currentState() {
  const moving = Math.hypot(player.pvx || 0, player.pvz || 0) > 0.4 || !!(keys.KeyW || keys.KeyS || keys.KeyA || keys.KeyD);
  return {
    x: r2(player.x), y: r2(player.y), z: r2(player.z), yaw: r3(player.yaw), pitch: r3(player.pitch),
    crouching: player.crouching, grounded: player.grounded, moving,
    sprinting: !!(keys.ShiftLeft || keys.ShiftRight) && moving,
    weapon: gameState.activeRightWeapon, attacking: player.attackTimer > 0,
    inCar: gameState.inCar, carIdx: gameState.activeCarIndex, hp: Math.round(gameState.hp),
    bodyColor: levelData.params.player.bodyColor, accentColor: levelData.params.player.accentColor,
    name: isHost() ? 'HOST' : multiplayer.localId,
  };
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Remote player rigs (interpolated, hidden in cars, weapon + name tag)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function makeNameTag(text) {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = 256; c.height = 64;
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,11,17,.72)'; ctx.fillRect(28, 10, 200, 44);
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(245,158,11,.8)'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.strokeRect(28, 10, 200, 44);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#fcd34d'; ctx.font = 'bold 28px Rajdhani, sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ctx.fillText(text, 128, 33);
  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
  const spr = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: tex, transparent: true, depthTest: false }));
  spr.scale.set(1.6, 0.4, 1);
  return spr;
}
function ensureRemoteRig(id) {
  let remote = multiplayer.remotePlayers.get(id);
  if (!remote) { remote = { x: 0, y: 0.18, z: 0, yaw: 0, buf: null }; multiplayer.remotePlayers.set(id, remote); }
  if (!remote.rig) {
    const built = buildPlayerRigMesh({ bodyColor: remote.bodyColor || '#64748b', accentColor: remote.accentColor || '#1e293b' });
    built.tpRig.visible = true;
    scene.add(built.tpRig);
    remote.rig = built.tpRig;
    remote.limbs = built;
    remote.tag = makeNameTag(id === multiplayer.hostId ? 'HOST' : id);
    scene.add(remote.tag);
    remote.weaponId = null;
  }
  return remote;
}
function feedRemoteState(id, s) {
  if (!s || id === multiplayer.localId) return;
  const remote = ensureRemoteRig(id);
  const now = performance.now();
  const prev = remote.buf ? remote.buf.p1 : { x: s.x, y: s.y, z: s.z, yaw: s.yaw };
  remote.buf = { p0: prev, p1: { x: s.x, y: s.y, z: s.z, yaw: s.yaw }, t0: remote.buf ? remote.buf.t1 : now - 1, t1: now };
  Object.assign(remote, s);
}
export function updateRemotePlayers(dt) {
  const renderT = performance.now() - INTERP_DELAY;
  for (const [id, remote] of multiplayer.remotePlayers) {
    if (!remote.rig) continue;
    const rig = remote.rig;
    let x = remote.x ?? 0, y = remote.y ?? 0.18, z = remote.z ?? 0, yaw = remote.yaw || 0;
    if (remote.buf) {
      const { p0, p1, t0, t1 } = remote.buf;
      const t = t1 > t0 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1.35, (renderT - t0) / (t1 - t0))) : 1;
      x = p0.x + (p1.x - p0.x) * t; y = p0.y + (p1.y - p0.y) * t; z = p0.z + (p1.z - p0.z) * t;
      yaw = lerpAngle(p0.yaw, p1.yaw, Math.min(1, t));
    }
    rig.position.set(x, y, z);
    rig.rotation.y = yaw;
    rig.visible = isActive() && !remote.inCar;
    if (remote.tag) { remote.tag.visible = rig.visible; remote.tag.position.set(x, y + 2.15, z); }
    const limbs = remote.limbs;
    if (!limbs) continue;
    if (remote.moving && !remote.inCar) {
      remote.walkPhase = (remote.walkPhase || 0) + dt * (remote.sprinting ? 15 : 9);
      const s = Math.sin(remote.walkPhase);
      limbs.tpLegL.rotation.x = s * 0.55; limbs.tpLegR.rotation.x = -s * 0.55;
      limbs.tpKneeL.rotation.x = Math.max(0, -s * 0.3); limbs.tpKneeR.rotation.x = Math.max(0, s * 0.3);
      limbs.tpArmL.rotation.x = -s * 0.45; limbs.tpArmR.rotation.x = s * 0.45;
    } else {
      limbs.tpLegL.rotation.x = 0; limbs.tpLegR.rotation.x = 0;
      limbs.tpKneeL.rotation.x = 0; limbs.tpKneeR.rotation.x = 0;
      limbs.tpArmL.rotation.x = 0; limbs.tpArmR.rotation.x = 0;
    }
    limbs.tpHip.scale.y = remote.crouching ? 0.72 : 1;
    limbs.tpHip.position.y = remote.crouching ? 0.65 : 0.90;
    if (remote.weaponId !== remote.weapon) {
      remote.weaponId = remote.weapon;
      if (remote.weaponMount) { remote.limbs.tpElbowR.remove(remote.weaponMount); freeGeo(remote.weaponMount); remote.weaponMount = null; }
      if (remote.weapon && remote.weapon !== 'fists') {
        const wm = buildRightWeaponMesh(remote.weapon);
        wm.position.set(0, -0.26, 0.05);
        wm.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
        remote.limbs.tpElbowR.add(wm);
        remote.weaponMount = wm;
      }
    }
    if (remote.attacking) limbs.tpArmR.rotation.x = -1.3;
    else if (remote.weapon === 'hotdog_shooter') limbs.tpArmR.rotation.x = -1.48;
  }
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// NPC flags + digest
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const F_SLEEP = 1, F_HOSTILE = 2, F_MOVING = 4, F_FLEE = 8, F_STUN = 16, F_PICKED = 32, F_SEARCH = 64;
function npcFlags(n) {
  return (n.asleep ? F_SLEEP : 0) | (n.hostile ? F_HOSTILE : 0) | (n.moving ? F_MOVING : 0) |
    ((n.fleeTimer || 0) > 0 ? F_FLEE : 0) | ((n.stunTimer || 0) > 0 ? F_STUN : 0) |
    (n.picked ? F_PICKED : 0) | (n.alertState === 'searching' ? F_SEARCH : 0);
}
function npcFullRow(n) {
  return {
    id: n.id, name: n.name, line: n.line, role: n.role, aggro: n.aggro, clanId: n.clanId,
    homeX: n.homeX, homeZ: n.homeZ, shirt: n.shirt, pants: n.pants, bandana: n.bandana,
    age: n.age, skin: n.skin, hair: n.hair, scale: n.scale,
    gtFestival: n.gtFestival, gtLook: n.gtLook, gtHat: n.gtHat, gtSkirt: n.gtSkirt, gtSash: n.gtSash, gtRole: n.gtRole,
    x: r2(n.x), z: r2(n.z), yaw: r3(n.yaw), hp: Math.round(n.hp), maxHp: n.maxHp,
    asleep: !!n.asleep, sleepTimer: r2(n.sleepTimer || 0), hostile: !!n.hostile, alertState: n.alertState,
    picked: !!n.picked, pickCool: r2(n.pickCool || 0), wallet: n.wallet || 0, fleeTimer: r2(n.fleeTimer || 0),
  };
}
function applyNpcFull(row) {
  const n = npcs[row.id];
  if (!n) return;
  Object.assign(n, row);
  if (n.mesh) { worldRoot.remove(n.mesh); freeGeo(n.mesh); n.mesh = null; n.spawned = false; n.queued = false; }
  if (n.sleepZGroup) { worldRoot.remove(n.sleepZGroup); freeGeo(n.sleepZGroup); n.sleepZGroup = null; }
}
function buildDigest() {
  const destroyedCrates = [], destroyedBarrels = [], takenPickups = [];
  digestBaseline.crates.forEach(id => { if (!smashableCrates.some(c => c.netId === id)) destroyedCrates.push(id); });
  digestBaseline.barrels.forEach(id => { if (!hazardBarrels.some(b => b.netId === id)) destroyedBarrels.push(id); });
  digestBaseline.pickups.forEach(id => { if (!worldPickups.some(p => p.netId === id)) takenPickups.push(id); });
  const dynamicPickups = worldPickups.filter(p => !digestBaseline.pickups.includes(p.netId))
    .map(p => ({ netId: p.netId, type: p.type, id: p.id, x: r2(p.x), y: r2(p.y), z: r2(p.z) }));
  return {
    v: multiplayer.worldVersion, seq: getNetSeq(),
    heat: r2(AO.heat), lead: r2(hooks.mpFestivalLead()),
    npcsFull: npcs.map(npcFullRow),
    crates: destroyedCrates, barrels: destroyedBarrels, pickupsTaken: takenPickups, pickups: dynamicPickups,
    lamps: lampPosts.map((lp, i) => [i, lp.state, r3(lp.fallYaw || 0), r3(lp.fallAngle || 0), r2(lp.respawnTimer || 0)]),
    manholes: manholeOpen.map(Boolean),
    gate: !!aura.estates?.gate.open,
    safes: (aura.estates?.houses || []).map(h => !!h.taken),
    traffic: vehicles.map(v => !!v.isTraffic),
    owners: Array.from(multiplayer.carOwners.entries()),
    order: multiplayer.playerOrder.slice(), hostId: multiplayer.hostId,
    cops: AO.cops.map(c => [c.netId, r2(c.x), r2(c.z), r2(c.mesh.position.y), Math.round(c.hp)]),
    pcars: AO.cars.map(c => [c.netId, r2(c.x), r2(c.z), r2(c.mesh.position.y), r3(c.yaw), r2(c.sp)]),
    rats: rats.map((r, i) => [i, r2(r.x), r2(r.z), r.dead ? 1 : 0]),
    jet: multiplayer.remoteJet || hooks.mpJetDigest(),
    players: Array.from(multiplayer.remotePlayers.entries()).map(([id, r]) => [id, {
      x: r.x, y: r.y, z: r.z, yaw: r.yaw, pitch: r.pitch, crouching: r.crouching, inCar: r.inCar,
      carIdx: r.carIdx, weapon: r.weapon, hp: r.hp, moving: r.moving, bodyColor: r.bodyColor, accentColor: r.accentColor,
    }]),
  };
}
const digestBaseline = { crates: [], barrels: [], pickups: [], captured: false };
function captureBaseline() {
  if (digestBaseline.captured) return;
  digestBaseline.crates = smashableCrates.map(c => c.netId);
  digestBaseline.barrels = hazardBarrels.map(b => b.netId);
  digestBaseline.pickups = worldPickups.map(p => p.netId);
  digestBaseline.captured = true;
}
function applyLampRow([i, state, yaw, angle, timer]) {
  const lp = lampPosts[i];
  if (!lp) return;
  if (state === 'standing') {
    lp.state = 'standing'; lp.fallAngle = 0; lp.fallVel = 0;
    lp.pivot.rotation.set(0, 0, 0);
    lp.lampMat.emissiveIntensity = 0.95; lp.lampMat.color.setHex(0xfef08a);
    lp.boxRef.minY = 0.18; lp.boxRef.maxY = 4.6;
  } else {
    if (lp.state === 'standing') knockOverLamppostBase(lp, yaw, 6);
    lp.state = state; lp.fallYaw = yaw; lp.fallAngle = angle; lp.respawnTimer = timer;
    lp.pivot.rotation.order = 'YXZ'; lp.pivot.rotation.y = yaw; lp.pivot.rotation.x = angle;
  }
}
function applyDigest(d) {
  if (!d) return;
  captureBaseline();
  multiplayer.worldVersion = d.v || 0;
  lastStreamVersion = multiplayer.worldVersion;
  setNetSeq(d.seq || 0);
  AO.heat = d.heat || 0;
  hooks.mpSetFestivalLead(d.lead);
  (d.npcsFull || []).forEach(applyNpcFull);
  npcBuf.clear();
  (d.crates || []).forEach(id => { const rec = smashableCrates.find(c => c.netId === id); if (rec) destroyCrate(rec, false); });
  (d.barrels || []).forEach(id => { const rec = hazardBarrels.find(b => b.netId === id); if (rec) destroyBarrel(rec); });
  (d.pickupsTaken || []).forEach(id => removePickupByNetId(id));
  (d.pickups || []).forEach(p => { if (!worldPickups.some(q => q.netId === p.netId)) spawnWorldPickup(p.type, p.id, p.x, p.y, p.z, { netId: p.netId }); });
  (d.lamps || []).forEach(applyLampRow);
  (d.manholes || []).forEach((open, i) => { manholeOpen[i] = !!open; });
  if (aura.estates) {
    if (d.gate) aura.estates.applyGate(true);
    (d.safes || []).forEach((taken, i) => { if (taken) aura.estates.applySafe(i); });
  }
  (d.traffic || []).forEach((flag, i) => { if (vehicles[i]) vehicles[i].isTraffic = !!flag; });
  multiplayer.carOwners = new Map(d.owners || []);
  if (Array.isArray(d.order)) multiplayer.playerOrder = d.order;
  if (d.hostId) multiplayer.hostId = d.hostId;
  seedCopBuffers(d.cops || [], d.pcars || []);
  (d.rats || []).forEach(([i, x, z, dead]) => { const r = rats[i]; if (r) { r.x = x; r.z = z; r.dead = !!dead; r.mesh.visible = !dead; } });
  if (d.jet && d.jet.owner && d.jet.owner !== multiplayer.localId && (d.jet.flying || d.jet.dead)) multiplayer.remoteJet = d.jet;
  else multiplayer.remoteJet = null;
  (d.players || []).forEach(([id, s]) => { if (id !== multiplayer.localId) feedRemoteState(id, s); });
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// World stream (host -> guests, 8Hz)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function buildWorldStream() {
  const anchors = [{ x: player.x, z: player.z }];
  for (const r of multiplayer.remotePlayers.values()) anchors.push({ x: r.x || 0, z: r.z || 0 });
  const near = (x, z, rad) => anchors.some(a => Math.hypot(a.x - x, a.z - z) < rad);
  const rows = [];
  for (const n of npcs) {
    if (!n.spawned && !near(n.x, n.z, 140)) continue;
    rows.push([n.id, r2(n.x), r2(n.z), r3(n.yaw), Math.round(n.hp), r2(n.walkCycle || 0), npcFlags(n), r2(n.punchTimer || 0), n.punchSide || 0]);
  }
  return {
    type: 'wstream', v: multiplayer.worldVersion,
    npcs: rows,
    cars: vehicles.map((v, i) => [i, r2(v.x), r2(v.y), r2(v.z), r3(v.yaw), r2(v.speed), r3(v.steerAngle || 0), v.gearIndex, Math.round(v.rpm || 0), v.isTraffic ? 1 : 0]),
    cops: AO.cops.map(c => [c.netId, r2(c.x), r2(c.z), r2(c.mesh.position.y), Math.round(c.hp)]),
    pcars: AO.cars.map(c => [c.netId, r2(c.x), r2(c.z), r2(c.mesh.position.y), r3(c.yaw), r2(c.sp)]),
    rats: rats.map((r, i) => [i, r2(r.x), r2(r.z), r.dead ? 1 : 0]),
    jet: multiplayer.remoteJet || hooks.mpJetDigest(),
    heat: r2(AO.heat), lead: r2(hooks.mpFestivalLead()),
  };
}
function pushBuf(map, key, point) {
  const now = performance.now();
  let b = map.get(key);
  if (!b) { b = { p0: point, p1: point, t0: now - 1, t1: now }; map.set(key, b); }
  else { b.p0 = b.p1; b.p1 = point; b.t0 = b.t1; b.t1 = now; }
  return b;
}
function sampleBuf(b, renderT) {
  if (!b) return null;
  const t = b.t1 > b.t0 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1.5, (renderT - b.t0) / (b.t1 - b.t0))) : 1;
  return {
    x: b.p0.x + (b.p1.x - b.p0.x) * t,
    y: (b.p0.y ?? 0) + ((b.p1.y ?? 0) - (b.p0.y ?? 0)) * t,
    z: b.p0.z + (b.p1.z - b.p0.z) * t,
    yaw: lerpAngle(b.p0.yaw ?? 0, b.p1.yaw ?? 0, Math.min(1, t)),
    t,
  };
}
function applyWorldStream(s) {
  AO.heat = s.heat || 0;
  hooks.mpSetFestivalLead(s.lead);
  if (s.v !== lastStreamVersion && performance.now() - multiplayer.lastDigestReq > 1500) {
    multiplayer.lastDigestReq = performance.now();
    sendHost({ type: 'digest_req' });
  }
  lastStreamVersion = s.v;
  for (const row of s.npcs || []) {
    const [id, x, z, yaw, hp, walk, flags, punch, side] = row;
    const n = npcs[id];
    if (!n) continue;
    pushBuf(npcBuf, id, { x, z, yaw });
    n.hp = hp;
    n.asleep = !!(flags & F_SLEEP); n.hostile = !!(flags & F_HOSTILE); n.moving = !!(flags & F_MOVING);
    n.fleeTimer = (flags & F_FLEE) ? Math.max(n.fleeTimer || 0, 0.3) : 0;
    n.stunTimer = (flags & F_STUN) ? Math.max(n.stunTimer || 0, 0.2) : 0;
    n.picked = !!(flags & F_PICKED);
    n.alertState = (flags & F_HOSTILE) ? 'chasing' : (flags & F_SEARCH) ? 'searching' : 'patrol';
    n.walkCycle = walk; n.punchTimer = punch; n.punchSide = side;
  }
  for (const row of s.cars || []) {
    const [i, x, y, z, yaw, speed, steer, gear, rpm, traffic] = row;
    const v = vehicles[i];
    if (!v) continue;
    v.isTraffic = !!traffic;

    // Do not overwrite client-predicted positions of locally driven or owned cars
    const owner = multiplayer.carOwners.get(i);
    if ((gameState.inCar && gameState.activeCarIndex === i) || owner === multiplayer.localId) continue;

    pushBuf(carBuf, i, { x, y, z, yaw });
    v.speed = speed; v.steerAngle = steer; v.gearIndex = gear; v.rpm = rpm;
  }
  syncCopBuffers(s.cops || [], s.pcars || []);
  for (const [i, x, z, dead] of s.rats || []) {
    pushBuf(ratBuf, i, { x, z, yaw: 0 });
    const r = rats[i];
    if (r) { r.dead = !!dead; r.mesh.visible = !dead; }
  }
  const jet = s.jet;
  if (jet && jet.owner && jet.owner !== multiplayer.localId && (jet.flying || jet.dead)) {
    multiplayer.remoteJet = jet;
    const now = performance.now();
    const prev = multiplayer.remoteJetBuf ? multiplayer.remoteJetBuf.p1 : jet;
    multiplayer.remoteJetBuf = { p0: prev, p1: jet, t0: multiplayer.remoteJetBuf ? multiplayer.remoteJetBuf.t1 : now - 1, t1: now };
  } else {
    multiplayer.remoteJet = null;
    multiplayer.remoteJetBuf = null;
  }
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Guest NPC frame: culling + interpolation + exact pose replication
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function guestNpcFrame(dt, elapsed) {
  const renderT = performance.now() - INTERP_DELAY;
  const SPAWN = 82, DESPAWN = 130;
  for (const n of npcs) {
    const b = npcBuf.get(n.id);
    const pose = sampleBuf(b, renderT);
    if (pose) { n.x = pose.x; n.z = pose.z; n.yaw = pose.yaw; }
    const d = Math.hypot(n.x - player.x, n.z - player.z);
    if (!n.spawned && d < SPAWN && b) {
      n.mesh = buildNpcMeshRaw(n.shirt, n.pants, n.bandana, n);
      n.mesh.position.set(n.x, 0.18, n.z);
      n.mesh.rotation.y = n.yaw;
      worldRoot.add(n.mesh);
      n.spawned = true;
    } else if (n.spawned && d > DESPAWN) {
      worldRoot.remove(n.mesh); freeGeo(n.mesh); n.mesh = null; n.spawned = false;
      if (n.sleepZGroup) { worldRoot.remove(n.sleepZGroup); freeGeo(n.sleepZGroup); n.sleepZGroup = null; }
      continue;
    }
    if (!n.spawned || !n.mesh) continue;
    if (Math.abs(n.kvx || 0) + Math.abs(n.kvz || 0) > 0.05) {
      n.x += (n.kvx || 0) * dt; n.z += (n.kvz || 0) * dt;
      const dec = Math.exp(-7 * dt); n.kvx *= dec; n.kvz *= dec;
    }
    const u = n.mesh.userData;
    if (n.asleep) {
      n.mesh.position.set(n.x, 0.34, n.z);
      n.mesh.rotation.order = 'YXZ';
      n.mesh.rotation.y = n.yaw; n.mesh.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; n.mesh.rotation.z = 0;
      u.legL.rotation.x = 0.08; u.legR.rotation.x = -0.06;
      u.armL.rotation.x = 0.15; u.armR.rotation.x = -0.15;
      if (!n.sleepZGroup) {
        n.sleepZGroup = buildSleepZzzGroup();
        worldRoot.add(n.sleepZGroup);
      }
      n.sleepZGroup.position.set(n.x, 0.78 + Math.sin(elapsed * 2.4 + n.id) * 0.14, n.z);
      n.sleepZGroup.lookAt(scene.getObjectByProperty('type', 'PerspectiveCamera')?.position || n.mesh.position);
      continue;
    }
    if (n.sleepZGroup) { worldRoot.remove(n.sleepZGroup); freeGeo(n.sleepZGroup); n.sleepZGroup = null; }
    n.mesh.rotation.x = 0;
    if ((n.stunTimer || 0) > 0) {
      n.stunTimer -= dt;
      n.mesh.position.set(n.x, 0.18, n.z);
      n.mesh.rotation.y = n.yaw;
      n.mesh.rotation.z = Math.sin(elapsed * 24) * 0.22;
      continue;
    }
    n.mesh.rotation.z = 0;
    if ((n.punchTimer || 0) > 0) n.punchTimer -= dt;
    const fast = n.hostile || (n.fleeTimer || 0) > 0;
    if (n.moving) n.walkCycle += dt * (fast ? 12.5 : 7.5);
    const sw = n.moving ? Math.sin(n.walkCycle) * (fast ? 0.78 : 0.52) : 0;
    u.legL.rotation.x = sw; u.legR.rotation.x = -sw;
    u.armL.rotation.x = -sw * 0.75; u.armR.rotation.x = sw * 0.75;
    u.armL.rotation.z = 0; u.armR.rotation.z = 0; u.torso.rotation.y = 0;
    if ((n.punchTimer || 0) > 0) {
      const pProg = 1 - n.punchTimer / 0.34;
      const arc = Math.sin(pProg * Math.PI);
      if (n.punchSide === 0) { u.armR.rotation.x = -1.45 * arc; u.armR.rotation.z = -0.65 * arc; u.torso.rotation.y = 0.42 * arc; }
      else { u.armL.rotation.x = -1.45 * arc; u.armL.rotation.z = 0.65 * arc; u.torso.rotation.y = -0.42 * arc; }
    }
    n.mesh.position.set(n.x, 0.18, n.z);
    n.mesh.rotation.y = n.yaw;
  }
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Guest police + rats rendering
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function seedCopBuffers(cops, pcars) {
  for (const rc of remoteCops.values()) { worldRoot.remove(rc.mesh); freeGeo(rc.mesh); }
  remoteCops.clear();
  for (const rc of remotePcars.values()) { worldRoot.remove(rc.mesh); rc.mesh.traverse(o => { if (o.geometry) o.geometry.dispose(); if (o.material) o.material.dispose(); }); }
  remotePcars.clear();
  syncCopBuffers(cops, pcars);
}
function syncCopBuffers(cops, pcars) {
  const seenC = new Set(), seenP = new Set();
  for (const [netId, x, z, y, hp] of cops) {
    seenC.add(netId);
    let rc = remoteCops.get(netId);
    if (!rc) {
      const mesh = buildCopMesh();
      worldRoot.add(mesh);
      rc = { mesh, buf: null };
      remoteCops.set(netId, rc);
    }
    rc.hp = hp;
    rc.buf = pushBuf(remoteCops, netId, { x, z, y, yaw: 0 }) === rc.buf ? rc.buf : rc.buf;
    rc.buf = remoteCops.get(netId).buf || rc.buf;
  }
  for (const [netId, rc] of remoteCops) if (!seenC.has(netId)) { worldRoot.remove(rc.mesh); freeGeo(rc.mesh); remoteCops.delete(netId); }
  for (const [netId, x, z, y, yaw, sp] of pcars) {
    seenP.add(netId);
    let rc = remotePcars.get(netId);
    if (!rc) {
      const { group, lr, lb } = buildCopCarMesh();
      worldRoot.add(group);
      rc = { mesh: group, lr, lb, buf: null };
      remotePcars.set(netId, rc);
    }
    rc.sp = sp;
    pushBuf(remotePcars, netId, { x, z, y, yaw });
    rc.buf = remotePcars.get(netId).buf || rc.buf;
  }
  for (const [netId, rc] of remotePcars) if (!seenP.has(netId)) { worldRoot.remove(rc.mesh); rc.mesh.traverse(o => { if (o.geometry) o.geometry.dispose(); if (o.material) o.material.dispose(); }); remotePcars.delete(netId); }
}
function pushRecordBuf(rc, point) {
  const now = performance.now();
  if (!rc.buf) rc.buf = { p0: point, p1: point, t0: now - 1, t1: now };
  else { rc.buf.p0 = rc.buf.p1; rc.buf.p1 = point; rc.buf.t0 = rc.buf.t1; rc.buf.t1 = now; }
}
function guestCopFrame(dt, t) {
