  const renderT = performance.now() - INTERP_DELAY;
  for (const rc of remoteCops.values()) {
    const p = sampleBuf(rc.buf, renderT);
    if (!p) continue;
    rc.mesh.position.set(p.x, p.y, p.z);
    const dx = player.x - p.x, dz = player.z - p.z;
    rc.mesh.rotation.y = Math.atan2(dx, dz);
  }
  for (const rc of remotePcars.values()) {
    const p = sampleBuf(rc.buf, renderT);
    if (!p) continue;
    rc.mesh.position.set(p.x, p.y + 0.02, p.z);
    rc.mesh.rotation.y = p.yaw;
    const fl = Math.sin(t * 14) > 0;
    rc.lr.visible = fl; rc.lb.visible = !fl;
  }
}
function guestRatFrame(dt, t) {
  const renderT = performance.now() - INTERP_DELAY;
  for (const [i, b] of ratBuf) {
    const r = rats[i];
    if (!r) continue;
    const p = sampleBuf(b, renderT);
    if (p) { r.x = p.x; r.z = p.z; }
    r.mesh.visible = !r.dead;
    if (!r.dead) r.mesh.position.set(r.x, -9 + .1 + Math.abs(Math.sin(t * 14 + r.ph)) * .04, r.z);
  }
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Guest world interpolation (cars + jet), called from main.js
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function updateRemoteWorld(dt, elapsed) {
  if (!isGuest()) return;
  const renderT = performance.now() - INTERP_DELAY;
  for (const [i, b] of carBuf) {
    if (gameState.inCar && gameState.activeCarIndex === i) continue;
    if (multiplayer.carOwners.get(i) === multiplayer.localId) continue; // skip locally-owned cars
    const v = vehicles[i];
    if (!v) continue;
    const p = sampleBuf(b, renderT);
    if (!p) continue;
    v.x = p.x; v.y = p.y; v.z = p.z; v.yaw = p.yaw;
    v.root.position.set(v.x, v.y, v.z);
    v.root.rotation.y = v.yaw;
    if (v.wheelPivots) { v.wheelPivots[0].rotation.y = v.steerAngle || 0; v.wheelPivots[1].rotation.y = v.steerAngle || 0; }
    if (!v.isTraffic && v.wheelSpinners) v.wheelSpinners.forEach(ws => { ws.rotation.x += ((v.speed || 0) * dt) / 0.38; });
  }
  const jb = multiplayer.remoteJetBuf;
  if (jb) {
    const t = jb.t1 > jb.t0 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1.5, (renderT - jb.t0) / (jb.t1 - jb.t0))) : 1;
    const a = jb.p0, c = jb.p1;
    const qa = new THREE.Quaternion(a.qx, a.qy, a.qz, a.qw);
    const qc = new THREE.Quaternion(c.qx, c.qy, c.qz, c.qw);
    qa.slerp(qc, Math.min(1, t));
    hooks.mpJetApplyRemote({
      x: a.x + (c.x - a.x) * t, y: a.y + (c.y - a.y) * t, z: a.z + (c.z - a.z) * t,
      qx: qa.x, qy: qa.y, qz: qa.z, qw: qa.w,
      speed: c.speed, throttle: c.throttle, ground: c.ground, dead: c.dead, nukes: c.nukes, ab: c.ab,
    });
  } else {
    hooks.mpJetApplyRemote(null);
  }
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// World events: apply locally (idempotent) + route through the host
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function grantPickupRec(rec) {
  if (!rec) return;
  if (rec.type === 'right') {
    if (!gameState.rightWeapons.includes(rec.id)) gameState.rightWeapons.push(rec.id);
    gameState.activeRightWeapon = rec.id;
    if (rec.id === 'hotdog_shooter') gameState.hotdogAmmo = MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO;
    sfx.pickup();
    syncEquippedMeshes();
    pushFeed('EQUIPPED ' + rec.id.toUpperCase(), 'border-l-amber-400');
  } else if (addLeftItemToBag(rec.id, 1)) {
    sfx.pickup();
    syncEquippedMeshes();
    pushFeed('COLLECTED ' + rec.id.toUpperCase(), 'border-l-sky-400');
  }
  hooks.updateHUD();
}
function applyWorldEvent(ev) {
  const me = multiplayer.localId;
  switch (ev.t) {
    case 'wiener':
      if (ev.by !== me) spawnNetWiener(ev.x, ev.y, ev.z, ev.vx, ev.vy, ev.vz, ev.by);
      break;
    case 'car_impact': {
      const v = vehicles[ev.idx];
      if (v) {
        const owner = multiplayer.carOwners.get(ev.idx);
        // Apply impulse if this car is unowned locally or driven by this client
        if (!owner || owner === me || (gameState.inCar && gameState.activeCarIndex === ev.idx)) {
          v.velX = (v.velX || 0) + ev.ix;
          v.velZ = (v.velZ || 0) + ev.iz;
          v.yaw = (v.yaw || 0) + ev.iyaw;
          const fx = Math.sin(v.yaw), fz = Math.cos(v.yaw);
          v.speed = v.velX * fx + v.velZ * fz;
        }
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'crate_hit': {
      if (!isHost()) break;
      const rec = smashableCrates.find(c => c.netId === ev.netId);
      if (!rec) break;
      rec.hp -= ev.a;
      if (rec.hp <= 0) {
        const lootRec = destroyCrate(rec, true);
        const dead = { t: 'crate_dead', netId: rec.netId, x: rec.x, y: rec.y, z: rec.z, by: ev.by,
          loot: lootRec ? { netId: lootRec.netId, type: lootRec.type, id: lootRec.id, x: lootRec.x, y: lootRec.y, z: lootRec.z } : null };
        applyWorldEvent(dead);
        broadcast({ type: 'wevt', ev: dead });
        multiplayer.worldVersion++;
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'crate_dead': {
      const rec = smashableCrates.find(c => c.netId === ev.netId);
      if (rec) {
        destroyCrate(rec, false);
        sfx.smash();
        spawnParticles(ev.x, ev.y + 0.55, ev.z, 0x92400e, 14, 6.5);
      }
      if (ev.loot && !worldPickups.some(p => p.netId === ev.loot.netId)) {
        spawnWorldPickup(ev.loot.type, ev.loot.id, ev.loot.x, ev.loot.y, ev.loot.z, { netId: ev.loot.netId });
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'barrel_hit': {
      if (!isHost()) break;
      const rec = hazardBarrels.find(b => b.netId === ev.netId);
      if (!rec) break;
      rec.hp -= ev.a;
      if (rec.hp <= 0) {
        destroyBarrel(rec);
        const dead = { t: 'barrel_dead', netId: rec.netId, x: rec.x, y: rec.y, z: rec.z, by: ev.by };
        applyWorldEvent(dead);
        broadcast({ type: 'wevt', ev: dead });
        multiplayer.worldVersion++;
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'barrel_dead': {
      const rec = hazardBarrels.find(b => b.netId === ev.netId);
      if (rec) {
        destroyBarrel(rec);
        sfx.smash();
        spawnParticles(ev.x, ev.y + 0.6, ev.z, 0xf97316, 18, 8.5);
        if (Math.hypot(player.x - ev.x, player.z - ev.z) < 4.2) hooks.applyPlayerDamage(24, 'EXPLOSIVE BARREL');
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'lamp_hit': {
      const lp = lampPosts[ev.i];
      if (lp && lp.state === 'standing') {
        knockOverLamppostBase(lp, ev.yaw, ev.speed);
      }
      if (isHost() && ev.by !== me && ev.speed > 6) AO.heat = Math.min(5, AO.heat + 0.08);
      break;
    }
    case 'lamp_up': applyLampRow([ev.i, 'standing', 0, 0, 0]); break;
    case 'manhole': manholeOpen[ev.i] = true; break;
    case 'gate':
      if (aura.estates) aura.estates.applyGate(true);
      if (isHost() && ev.by !== me) AO.heat = Math.min(5, AO.heat + 0.35);
      break;
    case 'safe':
      if (aura.estates) aura.estates.applySafe(ev.i);
      if (isHost() && ev.by !== me) AO.heat = Math.min(5, AO.heat + 0.5);
      break;
    case 'pickup_spawn':
      if (!worldPickups.some(p => p.netId === ev.netId)) spawnWorldPickup(ev.type, ev.id, ev.x, ev.y, ev.z, { netId: ev.netId });
      break;
    case 'pickup_taken': {
      const rec = removePickupByNetId(ev.netId);
      if (rec && ev.by === me && isGuest()) { grantPickupRec(rec); pendingPickups.delete(ev.netId); }
      break;
    }
    case 'npc_looted': {
      const n = npcs[ev.id];
      if (!n) break;
      n.picked = true; n.pickCool = ev.cool;
      if (ev.by === me && isGuest() && ev.cash) {
        gameState.cash += ev.cash;
        let extra = '';
        if (ev.item) { addLeftItemToBag(ev.item, 1); extra = ' + ITEM'; }
        sfx.pickup();
        pushFeed(`PICKPOCKETED ${n.name}: +$${ev.cash}${extra}`, 'border-l-emerald-400');
        hooks.updateHUD();
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'npc_alert': {
      if (!isHost()) break;
      const n = npcs[ev.id];
      if (!n) break;
      n.picked = true; n.pickCool = 60;
      if (n.aggro === 'fighter' || n.aggro === 'clan' || n.aggro === 'thug') { n.hostile = true; n.alertState = 'chasing'; }
      else n.fleeTimer = 8;
      break;
    }
    case 'driver_eject': {
      const v = vehicles[ev.idx];
      if (v && v.isTraffic) hooks.onVehicleEnter(v);
      break;
    }
    case 'rat_dead': {
      const r = rats[ev.i];
      if (r && !r.dead) { r.dead = true; r.mesh.visible = false; spawnParticles(r.x, -9 + .3, r.z, 0x7c2d12, 6, 3); }
      break;
    }
    case 'nuke':
      if (ev.by !== me) hooks.mpSpawnBomb(ev.x, ev.y, ev.z, ev.vx, ev.vy, ev.vz);
      break;
    case 'jet_board':
      if (ev.by !== me) multiplayer.remoteJet = { owner: ev.by, flying: true, dead: false, x: -384, y: 39.6, z: -106, qx: 0, qy: 0.7071, qz: 0, qw: 0.7071, speed: 0, throttle: 0, ground: true, nukes: 4 };
      break;
    case 'jet_exit':
      if (ev.by !== me) {
        multiplayer.remoteJet = null; multiplayer.remoteJetBuf = null;
        if (ev.eject) spawnParticles(ev.x, ev.y, ev.z, 0xf97316, 12, 8);
        hooks.mpJetApplyRemote(null);
      }
      break;
    case 'jet_crash':
      if (ev.by !== me) {
        spawnParticles(ev.x, ev.y, ev.z, 0xf97316, 16, 10);
        if (multiplayer.remoteJet) multiplayer.remoteJet.dead = true;
      }
      break;
    case 'player_died':
      if (ev.by !== me) pushFeed('A PLAYER WAS WASTED â€” WANTED LEVEL CLEARED', 'border-l-sky-400');
      break;
  }
}
function mpWorldEvent(ev) {
  if (!isActive()) return;
  ev.by = multiplayer.localId;
  if (ev.t === 'jet_xform') {
    const now = performance.now();
    if (isHost()) { multiplayer.remoteJet = null; return; }
    if (now - multiplayer.lastJetSend < CAR_MS) return;
    multiplayer.lastJetSend = now;
    sendHost({ type: 'wevt', ev });
    return;
  }
  applyWorldEvent(ev);
  if (isHost()) {
    if (['crate_dead', 'barrel_dead', 'lamp_hit', 'lamp_up', 'manhole', 'gate', 'safe', 'pickup_spawn', 'pickup_taken', 'npc_looted', 'driver_eject', 'rat_dead', 'player_died'].includes(ev.t)) multiplayer.worldVersion++;
    broadcast({ type: 'wevt', ev });
  } else {
    sendHost({ type: 'wevt', ev });
  }
}
// Host receives a guest event: authority-only handlers, then rebroadcast.
function hostReceiveEvent(id, ev) {
  ev.by = id;
  switch (ev.t) {
    case 'npc_hit': {
      const n = npcs[ev.id];
      if (!n || n.asleep) return;
      if (n.hp - ev.a <= 0 && !n.picked) {
        const cash = lootFor(n, 0.5);
        n.picked = true; n.pickCool = 90;
        if (cash > 0) multiplayer.connections.get(id)?.send({ type: 'award', cash });
      }
      damageNpcBase(n, ev.a, ev.y);
      AO.heat = Math.min(5, AO.heat + (ev.a > 40 ? .45 : .25));
      AO.calm = 0;
      return;
    }
    case 'cop_hit': {
      const c = AO.cops.find(c => c.netId === ev.netId);
      if (c) hurtCop(c, ev.a, id);
      return;
    }
    case 'rat_hit': {
      const r = rats[ev.i];
      if (r && !r.dead) {
        killRat(r, id);
        const dead = { t: 'rat_dead', i: ev.i, by: id };
        broadcast({ type: 'wevt', ev: dead });
        multiplayer.worldVersion++;
      }
      return;
    }
    case 'loot_req': {
      const n = npcs[ev.id];
      if (!n || n.picked) return;
      n.picked = true; n.pickCool = 120;
      const cash = lootFor(n);
      const item = Math.random() < 0.22 ? (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'soda' : 'medkit') : null;
      const lootEv = { t: 'npc_looted', id: n.id, cool: 120, by: id, cash, item };
      broadcast({ type: 'wevt', ev: lootEv });
      multiplayer.worldVersion++;
      return;
    }
    case 'pickup_take': {
      const rec = removePickupByNetId(ev.netId);
      if (!rec) return;
      const taken = { t: 'pickup_taken', netId: ev.netId, by: id };
      broadcast({ type: 'wevt', ev: taken });
      multiplayer.worldVersion++;
      return;
    }
    case 'pickup_drop': {
      const rec = spawnWorldPickup(ev.type, ev.id, ev.x, ev.y, ev.z);
      const spawnEv = { t: 'pickup_spawn', netId: rec.netId, type: ev.type, id: ev.id, x: ev.x, y: ev.y, z: ev.z, by: id };
      broadcast({ type: 'wevt', ev: spawnEv });
      multiplayer.worldVersion++;
      return;
    }
    case 'safe_req': {
      const h = aura.estates?.houses[ev.i];
      if (!h || h.taken) return;
      aura.estates.applySafe(ev.i);
      AO.heat = Math.min(5, AO.heat + 0.5);
      multiplayer.connections.get(id)?.send({ type: 'award', cash: 600 });
      const safeEv = { t: 'safe', i: ev.i, by: id };
      broadcast({ type: 'wevt', ev: safeEv });
      multiplayer.worldVersion++;
      return;
    }
    case 'crime': AO.heat = Math.min(5, AO.heat + (Number(ev.a) || 0)); AO.calm = 0; return;
    case 'died': {
      clearWantedLevel();
      const diedEv = { t: 'player_died', by: id };
      broadcast({ type: 'wevt', ev: diedEv });
      multiplayer.worldVersion++;
      return;
    }
    case 'mugged': {
      multiplayer.connections.get(ev.targetId)?.send({ type: 'mugged', amount: ev.amount, npcName: ev.npcName });
      return;
    }
    case 'jet_xform':
      multiplayer.remoteJet = { owner: id, flying: true, ...ev };
      return;
    case 'npc_alert':
      applyWorldEvent(ev);
      broadcast({ type: 'wevt', ev });
      return;
    default:
      applyWorldEvent(ev);
      if (['wiener', 'nuke', 'jet_board', 'jet_exit', 'jet_crash', 'car_impact'].includes(ev.t)) {
        broadcast({ type: 'wevt', ev });
      } else {
        multiplayer.worldVersion++;
        broadcast({ type: 'wevt', ev });
      }
  }
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Damage / awards / targets / car impacts
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function mpHitPlayer(id, amount, source) {
  if (!isActive() || !id) return;
  const now = performance.now();
  if (now - (hitCooldown.get(id) || 0) < 600) return;
  hitCooldown.set(id, now);
  if (id === multiplayer.localId) { hooks.applyPlayerDamage(amount, source); return; }
  if (isHost()) multiplayer.connections.get(id)?.send({ type: 'hit', amount, source });
  else sendHost({ type: 'hit_req', targetId: id, a: amount, source });
}
function mpVehicleImpact(targetCarIdx, impulseX, impulseZ, yawImpulse) {
  if (!isActive() || targetCarIdx < 0) return;
  const ev = {
    t: 'car_impact',
    idx: targetCarIdx,
    ix: r2(impulseX),
    iz: r2(impulseZ),
    iyaw: r3(yawImpulse),
    by: multiplayer.localId
  };
  mpWorldEvent(ev);
}
function mpAward(id, cash, item) {
  if (!isActive()) return;
  if (id === multiplayer.localId) {
    gameState.cash += cash;
    if (item) addLeftItemToBag(item, 1);
    hooks.updateHUD();
    return;
  }
  if (isHost()) multiplayer.connections.get(id)?.send({ type: 'award', cash, item });
}
function mpTargets() {
  const out = [];
  for (const [id, r] of multiplayer.remotePlayers) {
    out.push({ id, x: r.x || 0, y: r.y ?? 0.18, z: r.z || 0, yaw: r.yaw || 0, crouching: !!r.crouching, inCar: !!r.inCar, carY: r.y, hp: r.hp ?? 100, attacking: !!r.attacking });
  }
  return out;
}
function mpMeleeCop() {
  const fx = Math.sin(player.yaw + Math.PI), fz = Math.cos(player.yaw + Math.PI);
  for (const [netId, rc] of remoteCops) {
    const dx = rc.mesh.position.x - player.x, dz = rc.mesh.position.z - player.z;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
    if (d < 2.9 && Math.abs(player.y - rc.mesh.position.y) < 2.5 && (dx * fx + dz * fz) / Math.max(.1, d) > .2) {
      sendHost({ type: 'wevt', ev: { t: 'cop_hit', netId, a: 45, by: multiplayer.localId } });
      return;
    }
  }
}
function mpMeleeRat() {
  const fx = Math.sin(player.yaw + Math.PI), fz = Math.cos(player.yaw + Math.PI);
  for (let i = 0; i < rats.length; i++) {
    const r = rats[i];
    if (r.dead) continue;
    const dx = r.x - player.x, dz = r.z - player.z;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
    if (d < 2.6 && (dx * fx + dz * fz) / Math.max(.1, d) > .2) {
      sendHost({ type: 'wevt', ev: { t: 'rat_hit', i, by: multiplayer.localId } });
      return;
    }
  }
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Car / jet locks
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function broadcastLocks() { broadcast({ type: 'locks', owners: Array.from(multiplayer.carOwners.entries()) }); }
function mpCarEnter(idx) {
  if (!isActive()) return true;
  const me = multiplayer.localId;
  if (isGuest()) {
    if (multiplayer.grantBypass === idx) { multiplayer.grantBypass = -1; return true; }
    sendHost({ type: 'car_req', idx });
    return false;
  }
  const owner = multiplayer.carOwners.get(idx);
  if (owner && owner !== me) { pushFeed('VEHICLE LOCKED BY ANOTHER PLAYER', 'border-l-rose-500'); return false; }
  multiplayer.carOwners.set(idx, me);
  broadcastLocks();
  const v = vehicles[idx];
  if (v && v.isTraffic) {
    v.isTraffic = false;
    broadcast({ type: 'wevt', ev: { t: 'driver_eject', idx, by: me } });
    multiplayer.worldVersion++;
  }
  return true;
}
function mpCarExit(idx) {
  if (!isActive() || idx < 0) return;
  if (isHost()) {
    if (multiplayer.carOwners.get(idx) === multiplayer.localId) { multiplayer.carOwners.delete(idx); broadcastLocks(); }
  } else sendHost({ type: 'car_exit', idx });
}
function mpCarLocked(idx) {
  const owner = multiplayer.carOwners.get(idx);
  return !!owner && owner !== multiplayer.localId;
}
function mpCarOwner(idx) { return multiplayer.carOwners.get(idx) || null; }
function mpNpcLoot(n) {
  if (!isActive()) return 'local';
  if (isGuest()) { sendHost({ type: 'wevt', ev: { t: 'loot_req', id: n.id, by: multiplayer.localId } }); return 'pending'; }
  mpWorldEvent({ t: 'npc_looted', id: n.id, cool: 120, cash: 0 });
  return 'local';
}
function mpJetLocked() {
  const j = multiplayer.remoteJet;
  return !!(j && j.owner && j.owner !== multiplayer.localId && (j.flying || j.dead));
}
function mpJetRenderRemote() {
  const j = multiplayer.remoteJet;
  if (!j || j.owner === multiplayer.localId) return false;
  return hooks.mpJetApplyRemote(j);
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Host peer plumbing
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function attachHostConnection(conn) {
  const id = String(conn.peer || '').split('-').pop();
  if (!id || id === 'HOST') { conn.close(); return; }
  multiplayer.connections.set(id, conn);
  conn.on('data', data => hostData(id, data));
  conn.on('close', () => guestDisconnected(id));
  conn.on('open', () => {
    if (!multiplayer.playerOrder.includes(id)) multiplayer.playerOrder.push(id);
    multiplayer.playerCount = 1 + multiplayer.connections.size;
    conn.send({ type: 'welcome', playerId: id, hostId: multiplayer.localId, roomCode: multiplayer.roomCode,
      playerCount: multiplayer.playerCount, order: multiplayer.playerOrder.slice(),
      hostState: currentState(), digest: buildDigest() });
    broadcast({ type: 'roster', order: multiplayer.playerOrder.slice(), hostId: multiplayer.localId, playerCount: multiplayer.playerCount }, id);
    pushFeed(`PLAYER ${id} JOINED THE CITY`, 'border-l-emerald-400');
    updateHud();
  });
}
function setupHostPeer(peer, onOpen) {
  peer.on('open', onOpen);
  peer.on('connection', conn => {
    if (multiplayer.peer !== peer) { conn.close(); return; }
    attachHostConnection(conn);
  });
}
export function mpStartHost(requestedCode = '') {
  if (!window.Peer) { pushFeed('PEERJS COULD NOT LOAD', 'border-l-rose-500'); return; }
  if (isActive()) mpResetConnectionState();
  captureBaseline();
  multiplayer.roomCode = (requestedCode || roomCode()).toUpperCase();
  multiplayer.mode = 'host';
  multiplayer.localId = '1';
  multiplayer.hostId = '1';
  multiplayer.playerCount = 1;
  multiplayer.playerOrder = ['1'];
  const peer = new Peer(`gta-aura-main-${multiplayer.roomCode}-HOST`);
  multiplayer.peer = peer;
  setupHostPeer(peer, () => {
    if (multiplayer.peer !== peer) return;
    const codeEl = document.getElementById('host-room-code');
    const rowEl = document.getElementById('host-room-row');
    if (codeEl) codeEl.textContent = multiplayer.roomCode;
    if (rowEl) rowEl.className = 'flex items-center gap-2';
    updateHud();
    pushFeed(`ROOM OPEN â€” CODE ${multiplayer.roomCode}. PRESS ENTER CITY TO START THE SIM.`, 'border-l-amber-400');
  });
  peer.on('error', err => {
    if (err.type === 'unavailable-id') { mpResetConnectionState(); setTimeout(() => mpStartHost(), 400); }
    else pushFeed(`MULTIPLAYER ERROR: ${err.type || 'CONNECTION'}`, 'border-l-rose-500');
  });
}
function guestDisconnected(id) {
  multiplayer.connections.delete(id);
  multiplayer.playerOrder = multiplayer.playerOrder.filter(p => p !== id);
  for (const [idx, owner] of multiplayer.carOwners) if (owner === id) multiplayer.carOwners.delete(idx);
  if (multiplayer.remoteJet?.owner === id) { multiplayer.remoteJet = null; multiplayer.remoteJetBuf = null; }
  const remote = multiplayer.remotePlayers.get(id);
  if (remote?.rig) { scene.remove(remote.rig); freeGeo(remote.rig); }
  if (remote?.tag) scene.remove(remote.tag);
  multiplayer.remotePlayers.delete(id);
  multiplayer.playerCount = 1 + multiplayer.connections.size;
  broadcast({ type: 'roster', order: multiplayer.playerOrder.slice(), hostId: multiplayer.localId, playerCount: multiplayer.playerCount });
  broadcastLocks();
  pushFeed(`PLAYER ${id} LEFT THE CITY`, 'border-l-slate-400');
  updateHud();
}
function hostData(id, data) {
  if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') return;
  switch (data.type) {
    case 'hello':
      multiplayer.connections.get(id)?.send({ type: 'welcome', playerId: id, hostId: multiplayer.localId, roomCode: multiplayer.roomCode,
        playerCount: multiplayer.playerCount, order: multiplayer.playerOrder.slice(), hostState: currentState(), digest: buildDigest() });
      break;
    case 'rejoin': {
      if (Array.isArray(data.order)) {
        for (const pid of data.order) if (!multiplayer.playerOrder.includes(pid)) multiplayer.playerOrder.push(pid);
      }
      if (!multiplayer.playerOrder.includes(multiplayer.localId)) multiplayer.playerOrder.unshift(multiplayer.localId);
      if (data.carIdx >= 0 && !multiplayer.carOwners.has(data.carIdx)) multiplayer.carOwners.set(data.carIdx, id);
      if (data.s) feedRemoteState(id, data.s);
      multiplayer.playerCount = 1 + multiplayer.connections.size;
      multiplayer.connections.get(id)?.send({ type: 'welcome', playerId: id, hostId: multiplayer.localId, roomCode: multiplayer.roomCode,
        playerCount: multiplayer.playerCount, order: multiplayer.playerOrder.slice(), hostState: currentState(), digest: buildDigest() });
      broadcast({ type: 'roster', order: multiplayer.playerOrder.slice(), hostId: multiplayer.localId, playerCount: multiplayer.playerCount }, id);
      broadcastLocks();
      updateHud();
      break;
    }
    case 'pstate':
      feedRemoteState(id, data.s);
      broadcast({ type: 'pstate', id, s: data.s }, id);
      break;
    case 'car_req': {
      const idx = Number(data.idx);
      const owner = multiplayer.carOwners.get(idx);
      if (!owner) {
        multiplayer.carOwners.set(idx, id);
        const v = vehicles[idx];
        if (v && v.isTraffic) {
          v.isTraffic = false;
          hooks.onVehicleEnter(v);
          broadcast({ type: 'wevt', ev: { t: 'driver_eject', idx, by: id } });
          multiplayer.worldVersion++;
        }
        multiplayer.connections.get(id)?.send({ type: 'car_grant', idx });
        broadcastLocks();
      } else multiplayer.connections.get(id)?.send({ type: 'car_deny', idx });
      break;
    }
    case 'car_exit':
      if (multiplayer.carOwners.get(Number(data.idx)) === id) { multiplayer.carOwners.delete(Number(data.idx)); broadcastLocks(); }
      break;
    case 'car_xform': {
      const v = vehicles[Number(data.idx)];
      if (v && multiplayer.carOwners.get(Number(data.idx)) === id) {
        Object.assign(v, data.transform);
        v.root.position.set(v.x, v.y, v.z);
        v.root.rotation.y = v.yaw;
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'hit_req': {
      const targetId = String(data.targetId);
      if (targetId === multiplayer.localId || multiplayer.connections.has(targetId)) {
        hitCooldown.delete(targetId);
        mpHitPlayer(targetId, Number(data.a) || 0, String(data.source || 'REMOTE PLAYER'));
      }
      break;
    }
    case 'digest_req':
      multiplayer.connections.get(id)?.send({ type: 'digest', digest: buildDigest() });
      break;
    case 'wevt':
      if (data.ev) hostReceiveEvent(id, data.ev);
      break;
    case 'bye':
      try { multiplayer.connections.get(id)?.close(); } catch (_) {}
      guestDisconnected(id);
      break;
  }
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Guest plumbing + migration
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function mpStartGuest(code) {
  if (!window.Peer) { pushFeed('PEERJS COULD NOT LOAD', 'border-l-rose-500'); return; }
  const roomCodeValue = String(code).trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!/^[A-Z2-9]{4}$/.test(roomCodeValue)) { pushFeed('ENTER A 4-CHARACTER ROOM CODE', 'border-l-rose-500'); return; }
  if (isActive()) mpResetConnectionState();
  captureBaseline();
  multiplayer.roomCode = roomCodeValue;
  multiplayer.mode = 'guest';
  const id = `G${Math.floor(Math.random() * 8999 + 1000)}${Math.floor(Math.random() * 10)}`;
  multiplayer.localId = id;
  multiplayer.hostId = '1';
  multiplayer.playerCount = 2;
  const peer = new Peer(`gta-aura-main-${roomCodeValue}-${id}`);
  multiplayer.peer = peer;
  peer.on('open', () => connectToHost(peer, 0));
  peer.on('error', err => {
    if (multiplayer.migrating) return;
    mpResetConnectionState();
    pushFeed(`COULD NOT JOIN ROOM: ${err.type || 'CONNECTION'}`, 'border-l-rose-500');
  });
}
function connectToHost(peer, attempt) {
  if (multiplayer.peer !== peer) return;
  const conn = peer.connect(`gta-aura-main-${multiplayer.roomCode}-HOST`, { reliable: true });
  multiplayer.hostConnection = conn;
  let opened = false;
  const timer = setTimeout(() => {
    if (!opened) {
      try { conn.close(); } catch (_) {}
      if (attempt < 12 && multiplayer.peer === peer) setTimeout(() => connectToHost(peer, attempt + 1), 1200);
      else if (multiplayer.migrating) { pushFeed('MIGRATION FAILED â€” ROOM CLOSED', 'border-l-rose-500'); mpResetConnectionState(); }
    }
  }, 2600);
  conn.on('open', () => {
    opened = true;
    clearTimeout(timer);
    multiplayer.migrating = false;
    if (attempt === 0 && multiplayer.playerOrder.length <= 1) conn.send({ type: 'hello', id: multiplayer.localId });
    else conn.send({ type: 'rejoin', id: multiplayer.localId, order: multiplayer.playerOrder.slice(), s: currentState(), carIdx: gameState.inCar ? gameState.activeCarIndex : -1 });
    updateHud();
  });
  conn.on('data', guestData);
  conn.on('close', () => {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    if (multiplayer.mode === 'guest' && !multiplayer.migrating && multiplayer.hostConnection === conn) handleHostLost();
  });
}
function guestData(data) {
  if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') return;
  switch (data.type) {
    case 'welcome':
      multiplayer.localId = data.playerId || multiplayer.localId;
      multiplayer.hostId = data.hostId || '1';
      multiplayer.playerCount = Number(data.playerCount) || 2;
      if (Array.isArray(data.order)) multiplayer.playerOrder = data.order;
      if (data.hostState) feedRemoteState(multiplayer.hostId, data.hostState);
      applyDigest(data.digest);
      pushFeed('SYNCED WITH HOST â€” SAME CITY, SAME TIME', 'border-l-emerald-400');
      updateHud();
      break;
    case 'roster':
      if (Array.isArray(data.order)) multiplayer.playerOrder = data.order;
      multiplayer.hostId = data.hostId || multiplayer.hostId;
      multiplayer.playerCount = Number(data.playerCount) || multiplayer.playerCount;
      for (const [id, r] of multiplayer.remotePlayers) {
        if (!multiplayer.playerOrder.includes(id)) {
          if (r.rig) { scene.remove(r.rig); freeGeo(r.rig); }
          if (r.tag) scene.remove(r.tag);
          multiplayer.remotePlayers.delete(id);
        }
      }
      updateHud();
      break;
    case 'pstate':
      feedRemoteState(data.id, data.s);
      break;
    case 'wstream':
      applyWorldStream(data);
      break;
    case 'wevt':
      if (data.ev) applyWorldEvent(data.ev);
      break;
    case 'digest':
      applyDigest(data.digest);
      break;
    case 'hit':
      hooks.applyPlayerDamage(Number(data.amount) || 0, String(data.source || 'REMOTE'));
      break;
    case 'award':
      gameState.cash += Number(data.cash) || 0;
      if (data.item) addLeftItemToBag(data.item, 1);
      if (data.cash) pushFeed(`+$${data.cash} (KILL CREDIT)`, 'border-l-emerald-400');
      hooks.updateHUD();
      break;
    case 'mugged':
      gameState.cash = Math.max(0, gameState.cash - (Number(data.amount) || 0));
      pushFeed(`${data.npcName || 'THUG'} MUGGED YOU: -$${data.amount}`, 'border-l-rose-500');
      hooks.updateHUD();
      break;
    case 'locks':
      multiplayer.carOwners = new Map(data.owners || []);
      break;
    case 'car_grant':
      multiplayer.grantBypass = Number(data.idx);
      enterVehicle(Number(data.idx));
      break;
    case 'car_deny':
      pushFeed('VEHICLE LOCKED BY ANOTHER PLAYER', 'border-l-rose-500');
      break;
    case 'host_bye':
      handleHostLost();
      break;
  }
}
function handleHostLost() {
  if (multiplayer.mode !== 'guest' || multiplayer.migrating) return;
  multiplayer.migrating = true;
  const oldHost = multiplayer.hostId;
  const order = multiplayer.playerOrder.filter(id => id !== oldHost);
  const next = order[0];
  try { multiplayer.peer?.destroy(); } catch (_) {}
  multiplayer.peer = null;
  multiplayer.hostConnection = null;
  const hr = multiplayer.remotePlayers.get(oldHost);
  if (hr?.rig) { scene.remove(hr.rig); freeGeo(hr.rig); }
  if (hr?.tag) scene.remove(hr.tag);
  multiplayer.remotePlayers.delete(oldHost);
  if (multiplayer.remoteJet?.owner === oldHost) { multiplayer.remoteJet = null; multiplayer.remoteJetBuf = null; hooks.mpJetApplyRemote(null); }
  if (!next) { pushFeed('HOST LEFT â€” LOBBY CLOSED', 'border-l-rose-500'); mpResetConnectionState(); return; }
  pushFeed(next === multiplayer.localId ? 'HOST LEFT â€” YOU ARE THE NEW HOST' : `HOST LEFT â€” MIGRATING TO ${next}`, 'border-l-amber-400');
  if (next === multiplayer.localId) becomeHost(order);
  else {
    multiplayer.hostId = next;
    multiplayer.playerOrder = order;
    const peer = new Peer(`gta-aura-main-${multiplayer.roomCode}-${multiplayer.localId}`);
    multiplayer.peer = peer;
    peer.on('open', () => setTimeout(() => connectToHost(peer, 0), 900));
    peer.on('error', () => { if (multiplayer.migrating) setTimeout(() => { if (multiplayer.peer === peer) connectToHost(peer, 0); }, 1500); });
  }
}
function becomeHost(order) {
  multiplayer.mode = 'host';
  multiplayer.hostId = multiplayer.localId;
  multiplayer.playerOrder = order.slice();
  multiplayer.connections.clear();
  if (gameState.inCar) multiplayer.carOwners.set(gameState.activeCarIndex, multiplayer.localId);
  for (const rc of remoteCops.values()) { worldRoot.remove(rc.mesh); freeGeo(rc.mesh); }
  remoteCops.clear();
  for (const rc of remotePcars.values()) { worldRoot.remove(rc.mesh); rc.mesh.traverse(o => { if (o.geometry) o.geometry.dispose(); if (o.material) o.material.dispose(); }); }
  remotePcars.clear();
  hooks.mpAdoptTraffic();
  multiplayer.worldVersion++;
  const tryHost = (attempt) => {
    if (multiplayer.mode !== 'host') return;
    const peer = new Peer(`gta-aura-main-${multiplayer.roomCode}-HOST`);
    multiplayer.peer = peer;
    setupHostPeer(peer, () => {
      if (multiplayer.peer !== peer) return;
      multiplayer.migrating = false;
      multiplayer.playerCount = 1 + multiplayer.connections.size;
      broadcast({ type: 'roster', order: multiplayer.playerOrder.slice(), hostId: multiplayer.localId, playerCount: multiplayer.playerCount });
      broadcastLocks();
      pushFeed(`YOU ARE NOW THE HOST â€” ROOM ${multiplayer.roomCode} LIVES ON`, 'border-l-emerald-400');
      updateHud();
    });
    peer.on('error', err => {
      if (err.type === 'unavailable-id' && attempt < 12) {
        try { peer.destroy(); } catch (_) {}
        setTimeout(() => tryHost(attempt + 1), 900);
      } else if (attempt >= 12) {
        pushFeed('MIGRATION FAILED â€” GOING OFFLINE', 'border-l-rose-500');
        mpResetConnectionState();
      }
    });
  };
  tryHost(0);
}
function mpShutdown() {
  if (!isActive()) return;
  if (isHost()) {
    broadcast({ type: 'host_bye' });
    setTimeout(() => mpResetConnectionState(), 150);
  } else {
    sendHost({ type: 'bye' });
    setTimeout(() => mpResetConnectionState(), 100);
  }
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Per-frame send loop
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function sendMultiplayerState(now) {
  if (!isActive()) return;
  if (isHost()) {
    if (now - multiplayer.lastPlayerSend > PLAYER_MS) {
      multiplayer.lastPlayerSend = now;
      broadcast({ type: 'pstate', id: multiplayer.localId, s: currentState() });
    }
    if (now - multiplayer.lastWorldSend > STREAM_MS && gameState.screen === 'playing') {
      multiplayer.lastWorldSend = now;
      broadcast(buildWorldStream());
    }
  } else if (multiplayer.hostConnection?.open) {
    if (now - multiplayer.lastPlayerSend > PLAYER_MS) {
      multiplayer.lastPlayerSend = now;
      sendHost({ type: 'pstate', s: currentState() });
    }
    if (gameState.inCar && now - multiplayer.lastVehicleSend > CAR_MS) {
      multiplayer.lastVehicleSend = now;
      const v = vehicles[gameState.activeCarIndex];
      if (v) sendHost({ type: 'car_xform', idx: gameState.activeCarIndex,
        transform: { x: r2(v.x), y: r2(v.y), z: r2(v.z), yaw: r3(v.yaw), speed: r2(v.speed), steerAngle: r3(v.steerAngle), gearIndex: v.gearIndex, rpm: Math.round(v.rpm) } });
    }
  }
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Map dots for remote players
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function mpDrawMap(ctx, X, Y) {
  if (!isActive()) return;
  for (const [id, r] of multiplayer.remotePlayers) {
    if (r.x === undefined) continue;
    ctx.fillStyle = id === multiplayer.hostId ? '#f59e0b' : '#a855f7';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(X(r.x), Y(r.z), 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
    ctx.stroke();
  }
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Boot + hook registration
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function initMultiplayer() {
  const hostBtn = document.getElementById('btn-host-multiplayer');
  const joinBtn = document.getElementById('btn-join-multiplayer');
  const copyBtn = document.getElementById('btn-copy-room');
  hostBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => mpStartHost());
  joinBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => mpStartGuest(document.getElementById('join-room-code')?.value || ''));
  copyBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const code = document.getElementById('host-room-code')?.textContent || '';
    if (code) navigator.clipboard?.writeText(code).catch(() => {});
  });
  updateHud();
}
Object.assign(hooks, {
  mpActive: isActive,
  mpIsHost: isHost,
  mpIsGuest: isGuest,
  mpLocalId: () => multiplayer.localId,
  mpTargets,
  mpHitPlayer,
  mpVehicleImpact,
  mpAward,
  mpWorldEvent,
  mpCarEnter,
  mpCarExit,
  mpCarLocked,
  mpCarOwner,
  mpNpcLoot,
  mpGuestNpcFrame: guestNpcFrame,
  mpGuestCopFrame: guestCopFrame,
  mpGuestRatFrame: guestRatFrame,
  mpMeleeCop,
  mpMeleeRat,
  mpJetLocked,
  mpJetRenderRemote,
  mpDrawMap,
  mpShutdown,
});
window.__mp = multiplayer;

  { name: 'OUTPOST ATTENDANT', role: 'stationary', aggro: 'fighter', x: 254.0, z: 81.5, yaw: 0, shirt: 0xd97706, pants: 0x1e293b, line: '"Watch out for the Redrock Scorpions around the desert outpost!"' },

  { name: 'SCORPION SCOUT DRAKE', role: 'clan', clanId: 'redrock_scorpions', x: 274.0, z: 96.0, yaw: 0, line: '"SCORPION: Sound the alarmâ€”outsider on Route 66!"' },

  { name: 'JACKAL SENTRY FANG', role: 'clan', clanId: 'iron_jackals', x: 42.0, z: -38.0, yaw: -1.57, line: '"IRON JACKAL: Brickwood is Jackal territory! Take him out!"' }
];

export function buildNpcMeshLegacy(shirtColor, pantsColor, bandanaColor = null) {
  const root = new THREE.Group();
  const hip = new THREE.Group();
  hip.position.y = 0.90;
  root.add(hip);

  const torso = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.44, 0.54, 0.22), makeMat(shirtColor));
  torso.position.y = 0.27;
  torso.castShadow = true;

  const head = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.24, 0.26, 0.24), makeMat(0xd99b72));
  head.position.y = 0.68;
  head.castShadow = true;

  const hair = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.26, 0.09, 0.26), makeMat(0x1c1917));
  hair.position.y = 0.80;

  if (bandanaColor !== null) {
    const band = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.27, 0.06, 0.27), makeMat(bandanaColor));
    band.position.y = 0.75;
    hip.add(band);
  }

  const armGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.11, 0.50, 0.11);
  const legGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.14, 0.72, 0.14);

  const armL = new THREE.Mesh(armGeo, makeMat(shirtColor));
  armL.position.set(-0.29, 0.26, 0);
  const armR = armL.clone();
  armR.position.x = 0.29;

  const legL = new THREE.Mesh(legGeo, makeMat(pantsColor));
  legL.position.set(-0.12, -0.36, 0);
  const legR = legL.clone();
  legR.position.x = 0.12;

  hip.add(torso, head, hair, armL, armR, legL, legR);
  root.userData = { hip, torso, head, armL, armR, legL, legR };
  return root;
}

NPC_ROSTER.push(...makeExtraRoster());

NPC_ROSTER.forEach((def, idx) => {
  const clan = def.clanId ? CLAN_DEFS[def.clanId] : null;
  npcs.push({
    id: idx,
    name: def.name,
    role: def.role,
    aggro: clan ? 'clan' : (def.aggro || 'passive'),
    clanId: def.clanId || null,
    homeX: def.x,
    homeZ: def.z,
    x: def.x,
    z: def.z,
    yaw: def.yaw,
    shirt: clan ? clan.shirt : def.shirt,
    pants: clan ? clan.pants : def.pants,
    bandana: clan ? clan.bandana : null,
    line: def.line,
    age: def.age || 'adult', skin: def.skin, hair: def.hair, wallet: 0, picked: false, pickCool: 0, fleeTimer: 0, kvx: 0, kvz: 0,
    mesh: null,
    spawned: false,
    walkCycle: Math.random() * 6,
    turnTimer: 2 + Math.random() * 4,
    stunTimer: 0,
    hostile: false,
    alertState: 'patrol',
    searchX: def.x,
    searchZ: def.z,
    searchTimer: 0,
    attackCooldown: 0,
    punchTimer: 0,
    punchSide: 0,
    hp: npcHpFor(def, clan),
    maxHp: npcHpFor(def, clan),
    asleep: false,
    sleepTimer: 0,
    sleepZGroup: null,
    isKnockedElder: false
  });
});

export function buildSleepZzzGroup() {
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  const zMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x38bdf8 });
  const z1 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.18, 0.05, 0.05), zMat);
  z1.position.set(-0.14, 0, 0);
  const z2 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.24, 0.06, 0.05), zMat);
  z2.position.set(0.02, 0.22, 0);
  const z3 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.30, 0.07, 0.05), zMat);
  z3.position.set(0.18, 0.48, 0);
  g.add(z1, z2, z3);
  return g;
}

export function damageNpcBase(npc, amount, knockYaw) {
  if (!npc || npc.asleep) return;

  sfx.hit();
  spawnParticles(npc.x, 1.2, npc.z, 0xef4444, 6, 4);

  // Elder NPCs knock to the ground immediately & shake head in confusion
  if (npc.age === 'elder') {
    npc.hp = 0;
    npc.asleep = true;
    npc.hostile = false;
    npc.alertState = 'patrol';
    npc.sleepTimer = 18.0;
    npc.stunTimer = 0;
    npc.isKnockedElder = true;
    pushFeed(`${npc.name} WAS KNOCKED TO THE GROUND!`, 'border-l-amber-500');
    if (!npc.picked) {
      const lt = lootFor(npc, 0.5);
      npc.picked = true;
      npc.pickCool = 90;
      if (lt > 0) {
        gameState.cash += lt;
        pushFeed(`+$${lt} DROPPED FROM ${npc.name}`, 'border-l-emerald-400');
      }
    }
    return;
  }

  npc.hp = Math.max(0, npc.hp - amount);
  npc.stunTimer = 0.45;
  const kf = Math.min(16, 5 + amount * 0.09);
  npc.kvx = (npc.kvx || 0) + Math.sin(knockYaw) * kf;
  npc.kvz = (npc.kvz || 0) + Math.cos(knockYaw) * kf;

  if (npc.hp <= 0) {
    npc.asleep = true;
    npc.hostile = false;
    npc.alertState = 'patrol';
    npc.sleepTimer = 22.0;
    npc.stunTimer = 0;
    if (npc.mesh && !npc.sleepZGroup) {
      const zzz = buildSleepZzzGroup();
      zzz.position.set(npc.x, 0.85, npc.z);
      worldRoot.add(zzz);
      npc.sleepZGroup = zzz;
    }
    if (!npc.picked) { const lt = lootFor(npc, 0.5); npc.picked = true; npc.pickCool = 90; if (lt > 0) { gameState.cash += lt; pushFeed(`+$${lt} FROM ${npc.name}'S POCKETS`, 'border-l-emerald-400'); } }
    sfx.sleepChime();
    pushFeed(`${npc.name} WENT TO SLEEP! (Zzz)`, 'border-l-sky-400');
    return;
  }

  // Passive NPCs square up & fight back when punched!
  if (npc.aggro === 'passive') {
    if (rnd() < 0.85) {
      npc.aggro = 'fighter';
      npc.hostile = true;
      npc.alertState = 'chasing';
      pushFeed(`${npc.name} SQUARED UP! "YOU WANT SOME?!"`, 'border-l-rose-500');
    } else {
      npc.fleeTimer = 8;
    }
  } else if (npc.aggro === 'fighter' || npc.aggro === 'clan' || npc.aggro === 'thug') {
    if (!npc.hostile) {
      npc.hostile = true;
      npc.alertState = 'chasing';
      pushFeed(`${npc.name} IS FIGHTING BACK!`, 'border-l-rose-500');
    }
    if (npc.clanId) {
      for (const ally of npcs) {
        if (ally.clanId === npc.clanId && !ally.asleep && Math.hypot(ally.x - npc.x, ally.z - npc.z) < 34) {
          ally.hostile = true;
          ally.alertState = 'chasing';
          ally.searchX = player.x;
          ally.searchZ = player.z;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

export let activeClanAlertText = '';
const npcSpawnQueue = [];
export function updateNPCs(dt, elapsed) {
  // Guests never simulate NPC AI â€” the host stream drives everything.
  if (hooks.mpIsGuest()) { hooks.mpGuestNpcFrame(dt, elapsed); return; }
  const SPAWN_DIST = 82;
  const DESPAWN_DIST = 130;
  // Host AI targets the NEAREST player (host or any guest). Singleplayer: just local.
  const targets = [{ id: hooks.mpLocalId(), x: player.x, y: player.y, z: player.z, crouching: player.crouching, inCar: gameState.inCar, local: true }];
  if (hooks.mpIsHost()) for (const t of hooks.mpTargets()) targets.push({ ...t, local: false });
  const nearest = (x, z) => {
    let best = targets[0], bd = Infinity;
    for (const t of targets) { const d = Math.hypot(t.x - x, t.z - z); if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = t; } }
    return { tgt: best, dist: bd };
  };

  // --- PLAYER MELEE SWING HIT DETECTION AGAINST REGULAR NPCs ---
  if (player.attackTimer <= 0) {
    player._npcHit = false;
  } else if (!player._npcHit && (1 - player.attackTimer / player.attackDuration) >= 0.35) {
    player._npcHit = true;
    const fx = Math.sin(player.yaw + Math.PI), fz = Math.cos(player.yaw + Math.PI);
    const wDef = RIGHT_WEAPONS[gameState.activeRightWeapon] || { damage: 18 };
    for (let i = 0; i < npcs.length; i++) {
      const npc = npcs[i];
      if (!npc.spawned || npc.asleep) continue;
      const dx = npc.x - player.x, dz = npc.z - player.z, d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
      if (d < 2.5 && Math.abs(player.y - 0.18) < 2.2 && (dx * fx + dz * fz) / Math.max(0.1, d) > 0.2) {
        damageNpc(npc, wDef.damage, player.yaw);
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  let currentClanAlert = '';
  for (const clan of Object.values(CLAN_DEFS)) {
    if (clan.inTurf(player.x, player.z)) {
      currentClanAlert = `${clan.name} TERRITORY â€” THEY ARE LOOKING FOR INTRUDERS!`;
      break;
    }
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < npcs.length; i++) {
    const npc = npcs[i];
    const { tgt, dist: distToPlayer } = nearest(npc.x, npc.z);
    if (!npc.spawned && distToPlayer < SPAWN_DIST) {
      if (!npc.queued) { npc.queued = true; npcSpawnQueue.push(npc); }
      continue;
    } else if (npc.spawned && distToPlayer > DESPAWN_DIST) {
      worldRoot.remove(npc.mesh);
      freeGeo(npc.mesh);
      npc.mesh = null;
      if (npc.sleepZGroup) {
        worldRoot.remove(npc.sleepZGroup);
        freeGeo(npc.sleepZGroup);
        npc.sleepZGroup = null;
      }
      npc.spawned = false;
      continue;
    }
    if (!npc.spawned || !npc.mesh) continue;
    if (Math.abs(npc.kvx) + Math.abs(npc.kvz) > 0.05) {
      const kx = npc.x + npc.kvx * dt, kz = npc.z + npc.kvz * dt;
      if (!npcBlocked(kx, kz, i)) { npc.x = kx; npc.z = kz; } else { npc.kvx = 0; npc.kvz = 0; }
      const dec = Math.exp(-7 * dt);
      npc.kvx *= dec; npc.kvz *= dec;
    }

    const u = npc.mesh.userData;

    if (npc.asleep) {
      npc.sleepTimer -= dt;
      npc.mesh.position.set(npc.x, 0.24, npc.z);
      npc.mesh.rotation.order = 'YXZ';
      npc.mesh.rotation.y = npc.yaw;
      npc.mesh.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
      npc.mesh.rotation.z = 0;
      u.legL.rotation.x = 0.08;
      u.legR.rotation.x = -0.06;
      u.armL.rotation.x = 0.15;
      u.armR.rotation.x = -0.15;

      // Elder NPCs shake their head while knocked down on the floor
      if (npc.isKnockedElder && u.head) {
        u.head.rotation.y = Math.sin(elapsed * 11.0 + npc.id) * 0.52;
        u.head.rotation.z = Math.cos(elapsed * 7.0 + npc.id) * 0.15;
      }

      if (npc.sleepZGroup) {
        npc.sleepZGroup.position.set(npc.x, 0.78 + Math.sin(elapsed * 2.4 + npc.id) * 0.14, npc.z);
        npc.sleepZGroup.lookAt(camera.position);
      }
      if (npc.sleepTimer <= 0) {
        npc.asleep = false;
        npc.isKnockedElder = false;
        npc.hostile = false;
        npc.alertState = 'patrol';
        npc.hp = npc.maxHp;
        npc.mesh.rotation.x = 0;
        npc.mesh.position.y = 0.18;
        if (u.head) { u.head.rotation.y = 0; u.head.rotation.z = 0; }
        if (npc.sleepZGroup) {
          worldRoot.remove(npc.sleepZGroup);
          npc.sleepZGroup = null;
        }
      }
      continue;
    }

    npc.mesh.rotation.x = 0;
    if (npc.attackCooldown > 0) npc.attackCooldown -= dt;
    if (npc.punchTimer > 0) npc.punchTimer -= dt;
    if (npc.stunTimer > 0) {
      npc.stunTimer -= dt;
      npc.mesh.rotation.z = Math.sin(elapsed * 24) * 0.22;
      continue;
    }
    npc.mesh.rotation.z = 0;
    if (npc.aggro === 'clan' && !meteorDrop.active) {
      const clan = CLAN_DEFS[npc.clanId];
      const playerInMyTurf = clan && clan.inTurf(tgt.x, tgt.z);
      const detectRadius = tgt.crouching ? 11.5 : 23.0;
      if (playerInMyTurf && distToPlayer < detectRadius && Math.abs(tgt.y - 0.18) < 4.5) {
        if (npc.alertState !== 'chasing') {
          npc.alertState = 'chasing';
          npc.hostile = true;
          if (tgt.local) pushFeed(`${npc.line.replace(/"/g, '')}`, 'border-l-rose-500');
        }
        npc.searchX = tgt.x;
        npc.searchZ = tgt.z;
        npc.searchTimer = 7.5;
      } else if (playerInMyTurf && distToPlayer < 44.0 && npc.alertState === 'patrol') {
        npc.alertState = 'searching';
        npc.searchX = tgt.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 14;
        npc.searchZ = tgt.z + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 14;
        npc.searchTimer = 6.0;
      } else if (npc.searchTimer > 0) {
        npc.searchTimer -= dt;
        if (npc.searchTimer <= 0) {
          npc.alertState = 'patrol';
          npc.hostile = false;
        }
      }
      if (tgt.local) {
        if (npc.alertState === 'chasing') {
          currentClanAlert = `âš  ${clan.name} HUNTING YOU! CROUCH [C] OR ESCAPE THEIR TURF`;
        } else if (npc.alertState === 'searching' && !currentClanAlert.startsWith('âš ')) {
          currentClanAlert = `ðŸ‘ ${clan.name} SEARCHING THE AREA...`;
        }
      }
    }
    if (npc.aggro === 'thug' && !meteorDrop.active && !npc.hostile && !tgt.inCar && distToPlayer < (tgt.crouching ? 8 : 16) && Math.abs(tgt.y - 0.18) < 3) {
      npc.hostile = true; npc.alertState = 'chasing';
      if (tgt.local) pushFeed(`${npc.name}: ${npc.line.replace(/"/g, '')}`, 'border-l-rose-500');
    }

    let moveSpeed = 0;
    if (npc.fleeTimer > 0 && !npc.hostile) {
      npc.fleeTimer -= dt;
      npc.yaw = Math.atan2(npc.x - tgt.x, npc.z - tgt.z);
      moveSpeed = 5.6;
    } else if ((npc.hostile || npc.alertState === 'chasing') && !meteorDrop.active) {
      const dx = tgt.x - npc.x;
      const dz = tgt.z - npc.z;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
      npc.yaw = Math.atan2(dx, dz);
      if (d > 48 || Math.abs(tgt.y - 0.18) > 6.5) {
        npc.hostile = false;
        npc.alertState = 'patrol';
      } else if (d > 1.45) {
        moveSpeed = npc.aggro === 'clan' ? 5.1 : 4.5;
      } else {
        if (npc.attackCooldown <= 0 && !tgt.inCar) {
          npc.attackCooldown = 1.05;
          npc.punchTimer = 0.34;
          npc.punchSide = (npc.punchSide + 1) % 2;
          sfx.swing();
          const dmg = (npc.aggro === 'clan' ? 11 : npc.aggro === 'thug' ? 9 : 8) * (npc.age === 'elder' ? 0.5 : npc.age === 'child' ? 0.3 : npc.age === 'teen' ? 0.8 : 1);
          if (tgt.local) hooks.applyPlayerDamage(dmg, npc.name);
          else hooks.mpHitPlayer(tgt.id, dmg, npc.name);
          if (npc.aggro === 'thug' && Math.random() < 0.4) {
            if (tgt.local && gameState.cash > 0) {
              const stolen = Math.min(gameState.cash, 10 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30));
              gameState.cash -= stolen; npc.wallet = (npc.wallet || 0) + stolen;
              pushFeed(`${npc.name} MUGGED YOU: -$${stolen} (KNOCK THEM OUT TO GET IT BACK)`, 'border-l-rose-500');
            } else if (!tgt.local) {
              const stolen = 10 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30);
              npc.wallet = (npc.wallet || 0) + stolen;
              hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'mugged', targetId: tgt.id, amount: stolen, npcName: npc.name });
            }
          }
        }
      }
    } else if (npc.alertState === 'searching') {
      const dx = npc.searchX - npc.x;
      const dz = npc.searchZ - npc.z;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
      if (d > 1.6) {
        npc.yaw = Math.atan2(dx, dz);
        moveSpeed = 3.2;
      } else {
        npc.yaw += Math.sin(elapsed * 3.5 + npc.id) * 1.8 * dt;
      }
    } else if (npc.role === 'patrol' || npc.role === 'clan') {
      npc.turnTimer -= dt;
      if (npc.turnTimer <= 0) {
        if (npc.role === 'clan' && Math.hypot(npc.x - npc.homeX, npc.z - npc.homeZ) > 22) {
          npc.yaw = Math.atan2(npc.homeX - npc.x, npc.homeZ - npc.z);
        } else {
          npc.yaw += (Math.random() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1) * (Math.PI / 2);
        }
        npc.turnTimer = 3.0 + Math.random() * 4.0;
      }
      moveSpeed = 2.15;
    }

    if (npc.age === 'elder') moveSpeed *= 0.6; else if (npc.age === 'child') moveSpeed *= 1.1;
    npc.moving = moveSpeed > 0;
    if (moveSpeed > 0) {
      const nx = npc.x + Math.sin(npc.yaw) * moveSpeed * dt;
      const nz = npc.z + Math.cos(npc.yaw) * moveSpeed * dt;
      let blocked = npcBlocked(nx, nz, i);
      if (blocked) {
        npc.yaw += Math.PI * 0.65;
      } else {
        npc.x = nx;
        npc.z = nz;
      }
      npc.walkCycle += dt * (moveSpeed > 3.5 ? 12.5 : 7.5);
      const sw = Math.sin(npc.walkCycle) * (moveSpeed > 3.5 ? 0.78 : 0.52);
      u.legL.rotation.x = sw;
      u.legR.rotation.x = -sw;
      if (npc.punchTimer <= 0) {
        if (npc.hostile) {
          // Boxing Guard stance when squared up
          u.armL.rotation.x = -1.25; u.armL.rotation.z = 0.38;
          u.armR.rotation.x = -1.25; u.armR.rotation.z = -0.38;
          u.torso.rotation.y = Math.sin(elapsed * 7.0 + npc.id) * 0.18;
        } else {
          u.armL.rotation.x = -sw * 0.75; u.armR.rotation.x = sw * 0.75;
          u.armL.rotation.z = 0; u.armR.rotation.z = 0; u.torso.rotation.y = 0;
        }
      }
    } else {
      u.legL.rotation.x = 0;
      u.legR.rotation.x = 0;
      if (npc.punchTimer <= 0) {
        if (npc.hostile) {
          // Boxing Guard stance when squared up idling
          u.armL.rotation.x = -1.25; u.armL.rotation.z = 0.38;
          u.armR.rotation.x = -1.25; u.armR.rotation.z = -0.38;
          u.torso.rotation.y = Math.sin(elapsed * 7.0 + npc.id) * 0.18;
        } else {
          u.armL.rotation.x = 0; u.armR.rotation.x = 0;
          u.armL.rotation.z = 0; u.armR.rotation.z = 0; u.torso.rotation.y = 0;
        }
      }
    }

    if (npc.punchTimer > 0) {
      const pProg = 1 - npc.punchTimer / 0.34;
      const swingArc = Math.sin(pProg * Math.PI);
      if (npc.punchSide === 0) {
        u.armR.rotation.x = -1.45 * swingArc;
        u.armR.rotation.z = -0.65 * swingArc;
        u.torso.rotation.y = 0.42 * swingArc;
      } else {
        u.armL.rotation.x = -1.45 * swingArc;
        u.armL.rotation.z = 0.65 * swingArc;
        u.torso.rotation.y = -0.42 * swingArc;
      }
    }

    npc.mesh.position.set(npc.x, 0.18, npc.z);
    npc.mesh.rotation.y = npc.yaw;
  }

  for (let k = 0; k < 2 && npcSpawnQueue.length; k++) {
    const npc = npcSpawnQueue.shift();
    npc.queued = false;
    if (npc.spawned) continue;
    const { dist } = nearest(npc.x, npc.z);
    if (dist > DESPAWN_DIST) continue;
    npc.mesh = buildNpcMeshRaw(npc.shirt, npc.pants, npc.bandana, npc);
    npc.mesh.position.set(npc.x, 0.18, npc.z);
    npc.mesh.rotation.y = npc.yaw;
    worldRoot.add(npc.mesh);
    npc.spawned = true;
    if (npc.asleep && !npc.sleepZGroup) {
      const zzz = buildSleepZzzGroup();
      zzz.position.set(npc.x, 0.85, npc.z);
      worldRoot.add(zzz);
      npc.sleepZGroup = zzz;
    }
  }
  activeClanAlertText = currentClanAlert;
}

export const SKINS = [0xf1c8a5, 0xe2a87e, 0xd99b72, 0xb97a57, 0x8d5a3c, 0x6b4129];

export const HAIRS = [0x1c1917, 0x3b2a1a, 0x6b3f1d, 0xb8860b, 0xc2410c, 0x0f172a];

export function npcHpFor(def, clan) {
  const base = clan ? 120 : 100;
  return def.age === 'child' ? 40 : def.age === 'elder' ? 70 : def.age === 'teen' ? 85 : base;
}

// NPC collision detection with terrain, world boxes, and OTHER NPCs
export function npcBlocked(nx, nz, selfIdx = -1) {
  if (Math.abs(nx) > 136 || nz < -172 || nz > 202) return true;

  // Static geometry box collision
  for (let b = 0; b < worldBoxes.length; b++) {
    const wb = worldBoxes[b];
    if (wb.maxY <= 0.45 || wb.minY >= 1.8) continue;
    if (nx + 0.4 > wb.minX && nx - 0.4 < wb.maxX && nz + 0.4 > wb.minZ && nz - 0.4 < wb.maxZ) return true;
  }

  // NPC vs NPC collision prevents walking through each other
  for (let j = 0; j < npcs.length; j++) {
    if (j === selfIdx) continue;
    const other = npcs[j];
    if (!other.spawned || other.asleep) continue;
    if (Math.hypot(nx - other.x, nz - other.z) < 0.72) return true;
  }

  return false;
}

// Attach seated NPC Driver inside traffic or civilian vehicles
export function attachDriverToVehicle(carGroup, shirtColor = 0x2563eb, pantsColor = 0x0f172a) {
  const driver = buildNpcMeshRaw(shirtColor, pantsColor, null, { age: 'adult' });
  driver.scale.setScalar(0.72);
  driver.position.set(-0.4, 0.42, -0.15);
  
  if (driver.userData) {
    const u = driver.userData;
    u.legL.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2.2;
    u.legR.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2.2;
    u.armL.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 3;
    u.armR.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 3;
  }
  carGroup.add(driver);
  return driver;
}

// ---------- FACES ----------
const faceBasicCache = new Map();
function faceBasic(hex, transparent = false, opacity = 1) {
  const key = hex + '|' + transparent + '|' + opacity;
  let m = faceBasicCache.get(key);
  if (!m) { m = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: hex, transparent, opacity }); m.userData.shared = true; faceBasicCache.set(key, m); }
  return m;
}

export function makeFace(w, h, d, dir, o = {}) {
  const C = makeFace.c || (makeFace.c = (() => {
    const cache = {
      white: new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffffff }),
      mouth: new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x7f1d1d }),
      lens: new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x1e293b, transparent: true, opacity: 0.45 }),
      rim: new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x111827 })
    };
    Object.values(cache).forEach(m => { m.userData.shared = true; });
    return cache;
  })());
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  g.position.z = dir * (d / 2 + 0.004);
  const B = (sx, sy, sz, m, x, y, z = 0) => {
    const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(sx, sy, sz), m);
    b.position.set(x, y, z * dir);
    g.add(b);
    return b;
  };
  const ex = w * 0.22, ey = h * 0.06, es = w * 0.2, by = ey + h * 0.17, my = -h * 0.27;
  const irisM = faceBasic(o.iris || 0x3b2a1a);
  const browM = faceBasic(o.brow || 0x1c1917);
  const eyeL = B(es, es * 0.8, 0.008, C.white, -ex, ey), eyeR = B(es, es * 0.8, 0.008, C.white, ex, ey);
  const pupL = B(es * 0.5, es * 0.62, 0.01, irisM, -ex, ey, 0.004), pupR = B(es * 0.5, es * 0.62, 0.01, irisM, ex, ey, 0.004);
  const browL = B(w * 0.27, h * 0.055, 0.01, browM, -ex, by), browR = B(w * 0.27, h * 0.055, 0.01, browM, ex, by);
  const skinHex = new THREE.Color(o.skin || 0xd99b72).multiplyScalar(0.82).getHex();
  B(w * 0.1, h * 0.17, 0.035, makeSharedMat(skinHex), 0, -h * 0.05, 0.012);
  const mouth = B(w * 0.3, h * 0.055, 0.01, C.mouth, 0, my);
  const smileL = B(w * 0.06, h * 0.05, 0.01, C.mouth, -w * 0.17, my + h * 0.04);
  const smileR = B(w * 0.06, h * 0.05, 0.01, C.mouth, w * 0.17, my + h * 0.04);
  smileL.visible = smileR.visible = false;
  if (o.age === 'elder') {
    B(w * 0.5, h * 0.025, 0.006, faceBasic(skinHex), 0, h * 0.34, 0);
    B(w * 0.4, h * 0.025, 0.006, faceBasic(skinHex), 0, h * 0.30, 0);
  }
  if (o.glasses) {
    B(es * 1.5, es * 1.25, 0.006, C.lens, -ex, ey, 0.008);
    B(es * 1.5, es * 1.25, 0.006, C.lens, ex, ey, 0.008);
    B(w * 0.1, h * 0.03, 0.006, C.rim, 0, ey + 0.004, 0.008);
  }
  g.userData = { eyeL, eyeR, pupL, pupR, browL, browR, mouth, smileL, smileR, by, my, h, mood: '', blink: null };
  return g;
}

export function setFaceMood(f, mood, blink) {
  const u = f.userData;
  if (u.mood === mood && u.blink === blink) return;
  u.mood = mood; u.blink = blink;
  const a = mood === 'angry', s = mood === 'scared';
  u.browL.rotation.z = a ? -0.42 : s ? 0.2 : 0;
  u.browR.rotation.z = a ? 0.42 : s ? -0.2 : 0;
  u.browL.position.y = u.browR.position.y = u.by + (s ? u.h * 0.05 : a ? -u.h * 0.03 : 0);
  const ey = blink ? 0.12 : (s ? 1.35 : a ? 0.8 : 1);
  u.eyeL.scale.y = u.eyeR.scale.y = ey;
  u.pupL.scale.y = u.pupR.scale.y = blink ? 0.1 : 1;
  u.mouth.scale.set(a ? 0.9 : s ? 0.5 : 1, a ? 1.8 : s ? 3.4 : 1, 1);
  u.mouth.position.y = u.my - (a ? u.h * 0.015 : 0);
  u.smileL.visible = u.smileR.visible = (mood === 'happy');
}

export function addHair(head, w, h, d, dir, style, hex) {
  const m = makeSharedMat(hex);
  const put = (sx, sy, sz, x, y, z) => {
    const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(sx, sy, sz), m);
    b.position.set(x, y, z);
    head.add(b);
  };
  if (style === 'bald') {
    put(w * 1.04, h * 0.12, d * 0.3, 0, h * 0.18, -dir * d * 0.42);
    return;
  }
  put(w * 1.08, h * 0.3, d * 1.08, 0, h * 0.42, 0);
  if (style === 'long' || style === 'pigtails') {
    put(w * 1.08, h * 0.95, d * 0.28, 0, -h * 0.02, -dir * d * 0.46);
    put(w * 0.14, h * 0.8, d * 0.9, -w * 0.52, 0, 0);
    put(w * 0.14, h * 0.8, d * 0.9, w * 0.52, 0, 0);
  }
  if (style === 'pigtails') {
    put(w * 0.2, h * 0.5, w * 0.2, -w * 0.62, -h * 0.3, 0);
    put(w * 0.2, h * 0.5, w * 0.2, w * 0.62, -h * 0.3, 0);
  }
  if (style === 'mohawk') put(w * 0.18, h * 0.3, d * 1.1, 0, h * 0.68, 0);
  if (style === 'cap') {
    const cap = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w * 1.12, h * 0.22, d * 1.12), makeSharedMat(0xdc2626));
    cap.position.y = h * 0.5;
    head.add(cap);
    const brim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w * 1.0, h * 0.06, d * 0.5), makeSharedMat(0xb91c1c));
    brim.position.set(0, h * 0.42, dir * d * 0.62);
    head.add(brim);
  }
}

export function buildNpcMeshRaw(shirtC, pantsC, bandC, o = {}) {
  const age = o.age || 'adult';
  const S = { child: 0.62, teen: 0.88, adult: 1, elder: 0.95 }[age] || 1;
  const bigHead = age === 'child' ? 1.42 : age === 'teen' ? 1.1 : 1;
  const id = o.id || 0;
  const skin = o.skin || SKINS[id % SKINS.length];
  const hairHex = age === 'elder' ? 0xd1d5db : (o.hair || HAIRS[(id * 3 + 1) % HAIRS.length]);
  const root = new THREE.Group();
  const hip = new THREE.Group();
  hip.position.y = 0.90;
  root.add(hip);
  const skinM = makeSharedMat(skin);
  const shirtM = makeSharedMat(shirtC);
  const pantsM = makeSharedMat(pantsC);
  const shoeM = makeSharedMat(0x111827);
  const bx = (sx, sy, sz, m) => new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(sx, sy, sz), m);
  const torso = bx(0.44, 0.54, 0.22, shirtM);
  torso.position.y = 0.27;
  torso.castShadow = true;
  const belt = bx(0.46, 0.06, 0.24, makeSharedMat(0x1c1917));
  torso.add(belt);
  belt.position.y = -0.22;
  const collar = bx(0.2, 0.05, 0.24, skinM);
  collar.position.y = 0.28;
  torso.add(collar);
  const hw = 0.24, hh = 0.26, hd = 0.24;
  const head = bx(hw, hh, hd, skinM);
  head.castShadow = true;
  head.scale.setScalar(bigHead);
  head.position.y = 0.55 + (hh / 2) * bigHead;
  const style = age === 'child' ? (id % 2 ? 'pigtails' : 'short') : age === 'elder' ? (id % 3 === 0 ? 'bald' : 'short')
    : age === 'teen' ? pick(['cap', 'mohawk', 'long', 'short']) : pick(['short', 'short', 'long', 'bald']);
  addHair(head, hw, hh, hd, 1, style, hairHex);

  // Add stylish beards to selected adult and elder male NPCs
  const hasBeard = age !== 'child' && (o.beard || (id % 3 === 1 || id % 7 === 0));
  if (hasBeard) {
    const beardM = makeSharedMat(hairHex);
    const chinBeard = bx(hw * 1.04, hh * 0.35, hd * 0.65, beardM);
    chinBeard.position.set(0, -hh * 0.3, hd * 0.22);
    head.add(chinBeard);
    const mustache = bx(hw * 0.65, hh * 0.12, hd * 0.35, beardM);
    mustache.position.set(0, -hh * 0.14, hd * 0.42);
    head.add(mustache);
  }

  const face = makeFace(hw, hh, hd, 1, { age, skin, brow: hairHex, glasses: age === 'elder' && id % 2 === 0, iris: pick([0x3b2a1a, 0x1e40af, 0x166534, 0x44403c]) });
  head.add(face);
  if (bandC !== null && bandC !== undefined) {
    const band = bx(hw * 1.1, hh * 0.2, hd * 1.1, makeSharedMat(bandC));
    band.position.y = hh * 0.22;
    head.add(band);
  }
  const armGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.11, 0.50, 0.11);
  const legGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.14, 0.72, 0.14);
  const mkArm = (x) => {
    const a = new THREE.Mesh(armGeo, shirtM);
    a.position.set(x, 0.26, 0);
    a.castShadow = true;
    const hand = bx(0.1, 0.1, 0.1, skinM);
    hand.position.y = -0.28;
    a.add(hand);
    return a;
  };
  const mkLeg = (x) => {
    const l = new THREE.Mesh(legGeo, pantsM);
    l.position.set(x, -0.36, 0);
    l.castShadow = true;
    const shoe = bx(0.16, 0.08, 0.26, shoeM);
    shoe.position.set(0, -0.34, 0.05);
    l.add(shoe);
    return l;
  };
  const armL = mkArm(-0.29), armR = mkArm(0.29), legL = mkLeg(-0.12), legR = mkLeg(0.12);
  if (age === 'elder') {
    hip.rotation.x = 0.16;
    const cane = bx(0.035, 0.85, 0.035, makeSharedMat(0x78350f));
    cane.position.set(0, -0.42, 0.12);
    armR.add(cane);
  }
  if (age === 'child' || age === 'teen') {
    const pack = bx(0.3, 0.34, 0.12, makeSharedMat(pick([0x16a34a, 0xf59e0b, 0x2563eb, 0xdb2777])));
    pack.position.set(0, 0.3, -0.17);
    hip.add(pack);
  }
  hip.add(torso, head, armL, armR, legL, legR);
  root.scale.setScalar(S * (o.scale || 1));
  root.userData = { hip, torso, head, armL, armR, legL, legR, face };
  if (o.gtFestival) hooks.decorateNpc(root, o);
  return root;
}

export function buildNpcMesh(config = {}) {
  const toHex = (c, d) => (c === undefined || c === null || c === '') ? d : (typeof c === 'number' ? c : new THREE.Color(c).getHex());
  const band = config.bandColor ? toHex(config.bandColor, null) : null;
  return buildNpcMeshRaw(toHex(config.shirtColor, 0x1d4ed8), toHex(config.pantsColor, 0x0b1220), band, {
    age: config.age || 'adult',
    id: config.id === undefined ? 1 : config.id,
    skin: config.skin === undefined ? undefined : toHex(config.skin, undefined),
    hair: config.hair === undefined ? undefined : toHex(config.hair, undefined)
  });
}
