// ---------- PICKPOCKETING ----------
export const pp = { npc: null, prog: 0, aware: 0, el: null, hinted: false };

export function ppEnsureUI() {
  if (pp.el) return;
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:104px;transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:25;display:none;pointer-events:none;min-width:250px;padding:8px 12px;border-radius:10px;background:rgba(10,13,18,.9);border:1px solid rgba(245,158,11,.5);color:#fff;font:700 11px Rajdhani,Inter,sans-serif;letter-spacing:.06em;text-align:center;text-transform:uppercase';
  el.innerHTML = '<div id="pp-label"></div><div style="height:6px;margin-top:6px;background:#1e293b;border-radius:9px;overflow:hidden"><div id="pp-prog" style="height:100%;width:0;background:#34d399"></div></div><div style="height:4px;margin-top:4px;background:#1e293b;border-radius:9px;overflow:hidden"><div id="pp-aware" style="height:100%;width:0;background:#f43f5e"></div></div><div style="font-size:9px;color:#94a3b8;margin-top:3px">GREEN: LIFT &nbsp;Â·&nbsp; RED: SUSPICION (STAY BEHIND, CROUCH)</div>';
  document.body.appendChild(el);
  pp.el = el;
}

export function lootFor(n, mult = 1) {
  const r = (a, b) => Math.floor(a + rnd() * (b - a));
  let c = n.age === 'child' ? r(1, 7) : n.age === 'teen' ? r(4, 26) : n.age === 'elder' ? r(18, 70) : r(12, 90);
  if (n.clanId) c = r(30, 120);
  if (n.aggro === 'thug') c = r(25, 110);
  if (/TELLER/.test(n.name)) c = r(120, 300);
  return Math.floor(c * mult) + (n.wallet || 0);
}

export function updatePickpocket(dt) {
  ppEnsureUI();
  const isGuest = hooks.mpIsGuest();
  // Guests: picked/pickCool are host-authoritative (arriving via npc_looted events).
  if (!isGuest) for (const n of npcs) if (n.picked) { n.pickCool = (n.pickCool || 0) - dt; if (n.pickCool <= 0) n.picked = false; }
  const blocked = gameState.inCar || gameState.inventoryOpen || gameState.mapOpen || gameState.settingsOpen || meteorDrop.active || player.mantle.active;
  let best = null, bd = 1.95;
  if (!blocked) {
    for (const n of npcs) {
      if (!n.mesh || n.hostile || n.picked || n.stunTimer > 0 || n.fleeTimer > 0) continue;
      const d = Math.hypot(n.x - player.x, n.z - player.z);
      if (d < bd && Math.abs(player.y - 0.18) < 1.6) { bd = d; best = n; }
    }
  }
  if (best !== pp.npc) { pp.npc = best; pp.prog = 0; pp.aware = 0; }
  if (!best) { pp.el.style.display = 'none'; return; }
  const n = best;
  const tx = player.x - n.x, tz = player.z - n.z, L = Math.hypot(tx, tz) || 1;
  const facing = n.asleep ? -1 : (Math.sin(n.yaw) * tx + Math.cos(n.yaw) * tz) / L;
  const held = !!keys['KeyG'];
  const moving = keys['KeyW'] || keys['KeyA'] || keys['KeyS'] || keys['KeyD'];
  const sprinting = (keys['ShiftLeft'] || keys['ShiftRight']) && moving;
  if (held) {
    const dur = n.asleep ? 0.5 : n.age === 'elder' ? 0.9 : n.age === 'child' ? 0.8 : n.clanId ? 1.6 : 1.2;
    pp.prog += (dt / dur) * (player.crouching ? 1.25 : 1);
    if (!n.asleep) {
      let rate = 0.2 + (player.crouching ? -0.15 : 0.25);
      if (facing > 0.25) rate += 1.1;
      if (sprinting) rate += 0.8;
      rate *= n.age === 'elder' ? 0.6 : n.age === 'child' ? 1.4 : (n.clanId || n.aggro === 'thug') ? 1.4 : 1;
      pp.aware += Math.max(0, rate) * dt;
    }
    if (pp.aware >= 1) {
      sfx.hit();
      flashEdge('rgba(244, 63, 94, 0.35)');
      if (isGuest) {
        hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'npc_alert', id: n.id });
      } else {
        n.picked = true; n.pickCool = 60;
        if (n.aggro === 'fighter' || n.aggro === 'clan' || n.aggro === 'thug') {
          n.hostile = true; n.alertState = 'chasing';
        } else {
          n.fleeTimer = 8;
        }
        hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'npc_alert', id: n.id });
      }
      if (n.aggro === 'fighter' || n.aggro === 'clan' || n.aggro === 'thug') {
        pushFeed(`${n.name} CAUGHT YOU! "HEY!"`, 'border-l-rose-500');
      } else {
        pushFeed(`${n.name} NOTICED AND RAN OFF!`, 'border-l-rose-500');
      }
      pp.prog = 0; pp.aware = 0;
    } else if (pp.prog >= 1) {
      const mode = hooks.mpNpcLoot(n); // 'local' (single/host) or 'pending' (guest request)
      if (mode === 'pending') {
        pp.prog = 0; pp.aware = 0; // host grant arrives as npc_looted {by: me, cash, item}
      } else {
        const cash = lootFor(n);
        gameState.cash += cash;
        let extra = '';
        if (rnd() < 0.22) { addLeftItemToBag(rnd() < 0.5 ? 'soda' : 'medkit', 1); extra = ' + ITEM'; }
        n.picked = true; n.pickCool = 120;
        sfx.pickup();
        flashEdge('rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.3)');
        pushFeed(`PICKPOCKETED ${n.name}: +$${cash}${extra}`, 'border-l-emerald-400');
        pp.prog = 0; pp.aware = 0;
        hooks.updateHUD();
      }
    }
  } else {
    pp.prog = Math.max(0, pp.prog - dt * 1.5);
    pp.aware = Math.max(0, pp.aware - dt * 0.6);
  }
  pp.el.style.display = 'block';
  const tag = n.asleep ? 'SLEEPING' : facing > 0.25 ? 'FACING YOU!' : 'BACK TURNED';
  document.getElementById('pp-label').textContent = `HOLD [G] Â· PICKPOCKET ${n.name} (${n.age}) Â· ${tag}`;
  document.getElementById('pp-prog').style.width = `${Math.min(100, pp.prog * 100)}%`;
  document.getElementById('pp-aware').style.width = `${Math.min(100, pp.aware * 100)}%`;
}

// ---- damage routing: guests request, host applies (kill credit goes to the killer) ----
export function damageNpc(n, a, y) {
  if (!n) return;
  if (hooks.mpIsGuest()) {
    hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'npc_hit', id: n.id, a, y });
    n.stunTimer = 0.45; // cosmetic feedback; host stream owns hp/state
    const kf = Math.min(16, 5 + a * 0.09);
    n.kvx = (n.kvx || 0) + Math.sin(y) * kf * 0.5;
    n.kvz = (n.kvz || 0) + Math.cos(y) * kf * 0.5;
    return;
  }
  damageNpcBase(n, a, y);
  if (Math.hypot(player.x - n.x, player.z - n.z) < 14) crime(a > 40 ? .45 : .25);
}
export const tmpV = new THREE.Vector3();
let netCopSeq = 0, netPcarSeq = 0;

// Pure visual builders (shared by host sim + guest remote rendering).
export function buildCopMesh() {
  const gp_ = levelData.params.models.npc_guard;
  const m = buildNpcMeshRaw(colorToHex(gp_.shirtColor, 0x1d4ed8), colorToHex(gp_.pantsColor, 0x0b1220), null, { id: 900 + (netCopSeq % 50) });
  applyModelPreset('npc_guard', m);
  const cap = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.34, .1, .4), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x0b1220 }));
  cap.position.set(0, 1.78, .04); m.add(cap);
  const bd = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.08, .1, .02), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfacc15 }));
  bd.position.set(.12, 1.3, .2); m.add(bd);

  // Equip handgun onto the police officer's right arm
  if (m.userData && m.userData.armR) {
    const gunGroup = new THREE.Group();
    gunGroup.name = 'copPistol';
    const barrel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.06, 0.08, 0.24), makeSharedMat(0x1e293b));
    barrel.position.set(0, -0.26, 0.10);
    const grip = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.05, 0.12, 0.06), makeSharedMat(0x0f172a));
    grip.position.set(0, -0.30, 0.03);
    gunGroup.add(barrel, grip);
    m.userData.armR.add(gunGroup);
  }

  return m;
}

export function makeCop(x, z) {
  const m = buildCopMesh();
  worldRoot.add(m);
  const c = { mesh: m, x, z, hp: 60, atk: 0, shoot: Math.random(), netId: 'cop' + (netCopSeq++) };
  m.position.set(x, computeSupportFloor(x, z, 2, .4), z);
  AO.cops.push(c);
}

export function buildCopCarMesh() {
  const g = new THREE.Group(), dark = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x0b1220, metalness: .7, roughness: .3 }), wh = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xf1f5f9, roughness: .4 });
  const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2, .75, 4.5), dark); b.position.y = .65; g.add(b);
  const d = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.04, .4, 2.2), wh); d.position.set(0, .6, 0); g.add(d);
  const cab = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.7, .6, 2.1), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x111827, metalness: .9, roughness: .1 })); cab.position.set(0, 1.2, -.2); g.add(cab);
  const lr = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.8, .18, .35), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xff1f2f })), lb = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.8, .18, .35), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x2f6bff }));
  lr.position.set(.45, 1.58, -.2); lb.position.set(-.45, 1.58, -.2); g.add(lr, lb);
  for (const sx of [-1, 1]) for (const sz of [-1.4, 1.4]) { const w = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.38, .38, .28, 14), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x111 })); w.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2; w.position.set(sx * 1.02, .38, sz); g.add(w); }

  // Seated Cop Driver in Police Cruiser
  const driver = buildCopMesh();
  driver.scale.setScalar(0.72);
  driver.position.set(-0.4, 0.42, -0.15);
  if (driver.userData) {
    driver.userData.legL.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2.2;
    driver.userData.legR.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2.2;
    driver.userData.armL.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 3;
    driver.userData.armR.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 3;
  }
  g.add(driver);

  g.rotation.order = 'YXZ';
  return { group: g, lr, lb };
}

export function makeCar(x, z) {
  const { group: g, lr, lb } = buildCopCarMesh();
  worldRoot.add(g);
  AO.cars.push({ mesh: g, x, z, yaw: 0, sp: 0, hp: 220, lr, lb, stuck: 0, netId: 'pcar' + (netPcarSeq++) });
}

export function removeCop(c) { worldRoot.remove(c.mesh); freeGeo(c.mesh); AO.cops.splice(AO.cops.indexOf(c), 1); }

export function clearWantedLevel() {
  if (hooks.mpIsGuest()) return; // heat is host-authoritative; guests receive it via the stream
  AO.heat = 0;
  AO.calm = 0;
  AO.spawnT = 4;
  for (const cop of AO.cops.slice()) removeCop(cop);
  for (const car of AO.cars.slice()) {
    worldRoot.remove(car.mesh);
    car.mesh.traverse((object) => {
      if (object.geometry) object.geometry.dispose();
      if (object.material && object.material.dispose) object.material.dispose();
    });
    AO.cars.splice(AO.cars.indexOf(car), 1);
  }
  const starsElement = document.getElementById('ao-stars');
  if (starsElement) starsElement.style.display = 'none';
  pushFeed('WANTED LEVEL CLEARED', 'border-l-sky-400');
}

export const tracerMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfff2a8 });
export function tracer(x1, y1, z1, x2, y2, z2) { const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([new THREE.Vector3(x1, y1, z1), new THREE.Vector3(x2, y2, z2)]); const l = new THREE.Line(g, tracerMat); worldRoot.add(l); setTimeout(() => { worldRoot.remove(l); g.dispose(); }, 70); }

export function hurtCop(c, a, creditId) {
  c.hp -= a;
  spawnParticles(c.x, 1.2, c.z, 0xdc2626, 6, 4);
  crime(.15);
  if (c.hp <= 0) {
    const me = hooks.mpLocalId();
    if (!creditId || creditId === me) {
      gameState.cash += 40;
      pushFeed('OFFICER DOWN (+$40)', 'border-l-amber-400');
    } else {
      hooks.mpAward(creditId, 40);
    }
    crime(.5);
    removeCop(c);
  }
}

// Shared wanted-stars HUD (host sim + guest stream both call this).
export function updateWantedHud(t) {
  const st = document.getElementById('ao-stars');
  if (st) {
    const n = stars();
    st.style.display = n > 0 ? 'flex' : 'none';
    st.innerHTML = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map(i => `<span style="color:${i <= n ? (Math.sin(t * 10) > 0 ? '#ef4444' : '#3b82f6') : '#475569'}">â˜…</span>`).join('');
  }
}

export function copFrame(dt, t) {
  // Guests render host police from the world stream; they never simulate.
  if (hooks.mpIsGuest()) { hooks.mpGuestCopFrame(dt, t); updateWantedHud(t); return; }
  const s = stars(), pu = under();
  const targets = [{ id: hooks.mpLocalId(), x: player.x, y: player.y, z: player.z, crouching: player.crouching, inCar: gameState.inCar, local: true }];
  if (hooks.mpIsHost()) for (const tp of hooks.mpTargets()) targets.push({ ...tp, local: false });
  const nearest = (x, z) => {
    let best = targets[0], bd = Infinity;
    for (const tp of targets) { const d = Math.hypot(tp.x - x, tp.z - z); if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = tp; } }
    return { tgt: best, d: bd };
  };
  const hitTarget = (tp, amount, label) => { if (tp.local) hooks.applyPlayerDamage(amount, label); else hooks.mpHitPlayer(tp.id, amount, label); };

  if (s > 0 && !pu) {
    AO.spawnT -= dt;
    // Nerfed police spawns: max 4 foot cops, max 2 cars
    const wantF = Math.min(4, s + 1), wantC = s >= 2 ? Math.min(2, Math.floor(s / 2)) : 0;
    if (AO.spawnT <= 0) {
      AO.spawnT = 3.2; // Slower wave timer (was 1.6s)
      const anchor = targets[Math.floor(Math.random() * targets.length)];
      // Spawn at a comfortable distance so player has time to react & escape
      const a = Math.random() * 6.28, r = 65 + Math.random() * 30, x = anchor.x + Math.cos(a) * r, z = anchor.z + Math.sin(a) * r;
      if (x > -130 && x < 360 && z > -165 && z < 200 && !collidesHorizontally(x, z, .2, 1.8, 1.5)) {
        if (AO.cops.length < wantF) makeCop(x, z);
        else if (AO.cars.length < wantC) makeCar(x, z);
      }
    }
  }

  // Calm-down & Hiding mechanics: player can outrun and hide from cops
  AO.calm += dt;
  let nearestAny = 99;
  for (const c of AO.cops) nearestAny = Math.min(nearestAny, nearest(c.x, c.z).d);
  for (const c of AO.cars) nearestAny = Math.min(nearestAny, nearest(c.x, c.z).d);

  const localTgt = targets[0];
  const isHiding = localTgt && localTgt.crouching && nearestAny > 14;
  const calmThreshold = isHiding ? 3.0 : 5.5;

  if (AO.calm > calmThreshold && nearestAny > 22) {
    const hideMult = isHiding ? 2.5 : 1.0;
    AO.heat = Math.max(0, AO.heat - dt * (nearestAny > 45 ? 0.35 : 0.18) * hideMult);
  }
  if (pu) AO.heat = Math.max(0, AO.heat - dt * .25);

  // local melee & projectile damage (guest melee arrives via mpMeleeCop -> cop_hit)
  if (player.attackTimer > 0 && !player._copHit && (1 - player.attackTimer / player.attackDuration) >= 0.42) {
    player._copHit = true; const fx = Math.sin(player.yaw + Math.PI), fz = Math.cos(player.yaw + Math.PI);
    for (const c of AO.cops.slice()) { const dx = c.x - player.x, dz = c.z - player.z, d = Math.hypot(dx, dz); if (d < 2.9 && Math.abs(player.y - c.mesh.position.y) < 2.5 && (dx * fx + dz * fz) / Math.max(.1, d) > .2) hurtCop(c, (RIGHT_WEAPONS[gameState.activeRightWeapon] || { damage: 18 }).damage); }
  }
  AO.lastAtk -= dt;
  for (const w of projectileWieners) for (const c of AO.cops.slice()) if (!w.copHit && Math.hypot(w.x - c.x, w.z - c.z) < 1 && w.y < c.mesh.position.y + 2 && w.y > c.mesh.position.y - .2) { w.copHit = 1; hurtCop(c, RIGHT_WEAPONS.hotdog_shooter.damage, w.by); }

  // Foot police logic (chasing, aiming guns toward player chest, outrunnable)
  for (const c of AO.cops.slice()) {
    const { tgt, d } = nearest(c.x, c.z);
    const dx = tgt.x - c.x, dz = tgt.z - c.z, dd = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1;
    const py = tgt.inCar ? (tgt.carY ?? tgt.y) : tgt.y;
    if (pu || s === 0) { c.mesh.visible = true; if (s === 0 && d > 60) { removeCop(c); continue; } }

    // Nerfed foot cop speed (4.2 vs 5.6) so player can outrun them
    const isSearching = tgt.crouching && d > 12;
    const want = isSearching ? 2.2 : (d > (tgt.inCar ? 6 : 8) ? 4.2 : 0);
    const vx = dx / dd * want * dt, vz = dz / dd * want * dt;
    if (!collidesHorizontally(c.x + vx, c.z, .2, 1.7, .4)) c.x += vx;
    if (!collidesHorizontally(c.x, c.z + vz, .2, 1.7, .4)) c.z += vz;
    c.mesh.position.set(c.x, computeSupportFloor(c.x, c.z, c.mesh.position.y + .6, .4) + Math.abs(Math.sin(t * 9 + c.x)) * .04 * (want > 0), c.z);
    c.mesh.rotation.y = Math.atan2(dx, dz);

    // Precise Gun Aiming Pose (Pitches right arm directly at player chest)
    const u = c.mesh.userData;
    if (u && u.armR && u.armL) {
      if (d < 28 && !pu && s > 0 && !isSearching) {
        const copY = c.mesh.position.y;
        const dy = (py + 1.1) - (copY + 1.4);
        const pitchAngle = Math.atan2(dy, Math.max(0.1, dd));

        u.armR.rotation.order = 'YXZ';
        u.armR.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2 + pitchAngle;
        u.armR.rotation.y = 0;
        u.armR.rotation.z = -0.08;

        u.armL.rotation.order = 'YXZ';
        u.armL.rotation.x = -1.1 + pitchAngle * 0.5;
        u.armL.rotation.y = 0.25;
        u.armL.rotation.z = 0.35;
      } else {
        const sw = want > 0 ? Math.sin(t * 8 + c.x) * 0.5 : 0;
        u.armR.rotation.order = 'XYZ';
        u.armR.rotation.x = -sw;
        u.armR.rotation.y = 0;
        u.armR.rotation.z = 0;
        u.armL.rotation.order = 'XYZ';
        u.armL.rotation.x = sw;
        u.armL.rotation.y = 0;
        u.armL.rotation.z = 0;
      }
    }

    c.shoot -= dt; c.atk -= dt;
    if (d < 2.0 && c.atk <= 0 && Math.abs(py - c.mesh.position.y) < 2.5) {
      c.atk = 1.3;
      hitTarget(tgt, 6, 'OFFICER BATON');
    } else if (d < 18 && d > 2.0 && c.shoot <= 0 && Math.abs(py - c.mesh.position.y) < 4 && !isSearching) {
      c.shoot = 1.6 + Math.random() * 0.8;
      tracer(c.x, 1.3, c.z, tgt.x + (Math.random() - .5) * 1.2, tgt.y + 1, tgt.z + (Math.random() - .5) * 1.2);
      if (Math.random() < 0.28) hitTarget(tgt, Math.max(2, 3 + s * 0.6), 'POLICE FIRE');
    }
  }

  // Police cars logic
  for (const c of AO.cars.slice()) {
    const { tgt, d } = nearest(c.x, c.z);
    const dx = tgt.x - c.x, dz = tgt.z - c.z, dd = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1, want = Math.atan2(dx, dz);
    let dy = want - c.yaw; dy = Math.atan2(Math.sin(dy), Math.cos(dy));
    c.yaw += Math.max(-2.2 * dt, Math.min(2.2 * dt, dy * 3 * dt)) * (c.sp > 3 ? 1 : 2);
    const tgtSp = d > 14 ? (22 + s * 2) : (d > 7 ? 8 : 0); c.sp += (tgtSp - c.sp) * Math.min(1, dt * 1.2);
    const sl = (terrainH(c.x + Math.sin(c.yaw) * 2, c.z + Math.cos(c.yaw) * 2) - terrainH(c.x, c.z)) / 2; if (sl > 0 && sl < 5) c.sp -= 9.8 * sl * dt * .8;
    const nx = c.x + Math.sin(c.yaw) * c.sp * dt, nz = c.z + Math.cos(c.yaw) * c.sp * dt;
    if (collidesHorizontally(nx, nz, .3, 1.2, 1.5)) { c.sp *= -.3; c.yaw += .6; c.stuck += dt; } else { c.x = nx; c.z = nz; c.stuck = Math.max(0, c.stuck - dt); }
    const gy = computeSupportFloor(c.x, c.z, 1.4, 1.2);
    c.mesh.position.set(c.x, gy + .02, c.z); c.mesh.rotation.y = c.yaw;
    c.mesh.rotation.x = -Math.atan2(terrainH(c.x + Math.sin(c.yaw) * 2, c.z + Math.cos(c.yaw) * 2) - terrainH(c.x - Math.sin(c.yaw) * 2, c.z - Math.cos(c.yaw) * 2), 4) * (c.z < -170 ? 1 : 0);
    const fl = Math.sin(t * 14) > 0; c.lr.visible = fl; c.lb.visible = !fl;
    if (d < 3.3 && !pu && Math.abs(c.sp) > 5) {
      hitTarget(tgt, 8, 'POLICE RAM'); c.sp *= .4;
      if (tgt.local && gameState.inCar) { const v = vehicles[gameState.activeCarIndex]; v.speed *= .7; }
    }
    // Any player-driven car (local or remote) can wreck a cruiser
    for (let vi = 0; vi < vehicles.length; vi++) {
      const v = vehicles[vi];
      const drivenLocal = gameState.inCar && gameState.activeCarIndex === vi;
      const owner = hooks.mpCarOwner(vi);
      if (!drivenLocal && !(owner && owner !== hooks.mpLocalId())) continue;
      if (Math.hypot(v.x - c.x, v.z - c.z) < 3 && Math.abs(v.speed) > 7) { c.hp -= Math.abs(v.speed) * 5; v.speed *= .8; spawnParticles(c.x, 1, c.z, 0xf59e0b, 8, 6); }
    }
    if (c.hp <= 0 || c.stuck > 6 || (s === 0 && d > 90)) { spawnParticles(c.x, 1, c.z, 0xf97316, 16, 8); worldRoot.remove(c.mesh); c.mesh.traverse(o => { if (o.geometry) o.geometry.dispose(); if (o.material) o.material.dispose(); }); AO.cars.splice(AO.cars.indexOf(c), 1); }
  }
  updateWantedHud(t);
}

export const rats = [], ratG = new THREE.BoxGeometry(.22, .16, .5), ratTG = new THREE.BoxGeometry(.03, .03, .35), ratM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x3b332c, roughness: 1 });
export let ratHit = 0;
export function buildSewerRats() {
  [[-110, -30], [-70, -30], [-20, -30], [34, -30], [100, -30], [20, -50], [20, -10], [20, 50], [20, 85], [0, 24]].forEach((q, i) => {
    if (!sewerOK(q[0], q[1], .3)) return;
    const m = new THREE.Mesh(ratG, ratM), tl = new THREE.Mesh(ratTG, ratM); tl.position.z = -.38; m.add(tl); m.position.set(q[0], SFY + .1, q[1]); worldRoot.add(m);
    rats.push({ mesh: m, x: q[0], z: q[1], hx: q[0], hz: q[1], bite: 0, ph: i * 1.7, dead: false });
  });
}
export function reviveRats() { for (const r of rats) { r.dead = false; r.x = r.hx; r.z = r.hz; r.mesh.visible = true; } }
export function killRat(r, creditId) {
  if (r.dead) return;
  r.dead = true; r.mesh.visible = false;
  spawnParticles(r.x, SFY + .3, r.z, 0x7c2d12, 6, 3);
  const me = hooks.mpLocalId();
  if (!creditId || creditId === me) gameState.cash += 5;
  else hooks.mpAward(creditId, 5);
  sfx.hit();
}
export function ratFrame(dt, t) {
  if (hooks.mpIsGuest()) { hooks.mpGuestRatFrame(dt, t); return; }
  if (!under() || climb.on) return;
  if (Math.random() < dt * .5) sfx.tone(1500 + Math.random() * 500, 800, .06, 'sine', .025);
  const swing = player.attackTimer > 0 && ratHit <= 0; if (swing) ratHit = .4; ratHit -= dt;
  const fx = Math.sin(player.yaw + Math.PI), fz = Math.cos(player.yaw + Math.PI);
  const targets = [{ id: hooks.mpLocalId(), x: player.x, y: player.y, z: player.z, local: true }];
  if (hooks.mpIsHost()) for (const tp of hooks.mpTargets()) if (tp.y < -1.2) targets.push({ ...tp, local: false });
  for (const r of rats) {
    if (r.dead) continue;
    let tgt = targets[0], bd = Infinity;
    for (const tp of targets) { const d = Math.hypot(tp.x - r.x, tp.z - r.z); if (d < bd) { bd = d; tgt = tp; } }
    const dx = tgt.x - r.x, dz = tgt.z - r.z, d = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1; let tx, tz, sp;
    if (d < 14) { tx = dx / d; tz = dz / d; sp = d > .9 ? 4.6 : 0; } else { tx = Math.sin(t * .7 + r.ph); tz = Math.cos(t * .5 + r.ph); sp = 1.2; }
    const nx = r.x + tx * sp * dt, nz = r.z + tz * sp * dt; if (sewerOK(nx, nz, .2)) { r.x = nx; r.z = nz; }
    r.mesh.position.set(r.x, SFY + .1 + Math.abs(Math.sin(t * 14 + r.ph)) * .04, r.z); r.mesh.rotation.y = Math.atan2(tx, tz);
    r.bite -= dt;
    if (d < 1 && r.bite <= 0 && Math.abs(tgt.y - SFY) < 2) {
      r.bite = 1;
      if (tgt.local) hooks.applyPlayerDamage(3, 'SEWER RAT');
      else hooks.mpHitPlayer(tgt.id, 3, 'SEWER RAT');
      sfx.tone(1800, 1200, .05, 'square', .03);
    }
    const localHit = tgt.local && swing && d < 2.6 && (dx * fx + dz * fz) / d > .2;
    const w = projectileWieners.find(w => Math.hypot(w.x - r.x, w.z - r.z) < .8 && Math.abs(w.y - (SFY + .2)) < 1.2);
    if (localHit || w) {
      killRat(r, w ? w.by : undefined);
      hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'rat_dead', i: rats.indexOf(r) });
    }
  }
}

// Register late-bound implementations for lower layers (see state.js).
Object.assign(hooks, { damageNpc });

function clampMeteorPosition(x, z) {
  return {
    x: Math.max(WORLD_BOUNDS.minX + player.radius, Math.min(WORLD_BOUNDS.maxX - player.radius, x)),
    z: Math.max(WORLD_BOUNDS.minZ + player.radius, Math.min(WORLD_BOUNDS.maxZ - player.radius, z))
  };
}

export const fpRoot = new THREE.Group();

camera.add(fpRoot);

export const skinMat = makeMat(0xe2a87e);

export const sleeveMat = makeMat(0x1e293b);

export function createFirstPersonArm(side) {
  const pivot = new THREE.Group();
  pivot.position.set(side * 0.32, -0.26, -0.34);

  const sleeve = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.11, 0.11, 0.30), sleeveMat);
  sleeve.position.set(0, -0.02, 0.12);

  const forearm = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.095, 0.095, 0.24), skinMat);
  forearm.position.set(0, 0, -0.06);

  const fist = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.105, 0.105, 0.11), skinMat);
  fist.position.set(0, 0.005, -0.19);

  const itemMount = new THREE.Group();
  itemMount.position.set(0, 0.02, -0.20);

  pivot.add(sleeve, forearm, fist, itemMount);
  fpRoot.add(pivot);
  return { pivot, fist, itemMount };
}

export const fpRight = createFirstPersonArm(1);

export const fpLeft = createFirstPersonArm(-1);

export const spotLight = new THREE.SpotLight(0xfef9c3, 3.6, 36, Math.PI / 5.5, 0.42, 1.1);

spotLight.position.set(0, 0, -0.1);

spotLight.target.position.set(0, 0, -10);

spotLight.visible = false;

fpLeft.itemMount.add(spotLight);

fpLeft.itemMount.add(spotLight.target);

// 3rd-Person Articulated Character Rig (Olympic Sprinter & Jump Ready)
export function buildPlayerRigMesh(params = {}) {
  const toHex = (c, d) => (c === undefined || c === null || c === '') ? d : (typeof c === 'number' ? c : new THREE.Color(c).getHex());
  const bodyColor = toHex(params.bodyColor, 0x1e293b);
  const accentColor = toHex(params.accentColor, 0x0f172a);
  const sleeveMat = makeMat(bodyColor);
  const tpRig = new THREE.Group();
  const tpHip = new THREE.Group();
  tpHip.position.y = 0.90;
  tpRig.add(tpHip);

  const tpTorso = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.46, 0.56, 0.24), makeMat(bodyColor));
  tpTorso.position.y = 0.28;
  tpTorso.castShadow = true;

  const tpHead = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.26, 0.28, 0.26), skinMat);
  tpHead.position.y = 0.72;
  tpHead.castShadow = true;

  const tpHair = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.28, 0.10, 0.28), makeMat(accentColor));
  tpHair.position.y = 0.85;
  tpHead.add(makeFace(0.26, 0.28, 0.26, -1, { age: 'adult', skin: 0xe2a87e, brow: 0x0f172a, iris: 0x1e40af }));

  function createTpArticulatedArm(side) {
    const shoulderPivot = new THREE.Group();
    shoulderPivot.position.set(side * 0.31, 0.50, 0);

    const upperArm = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.12, 0.27, 0.12), sleeveMat);
    upperArm.position.y = -0.135;
    upperArm.castShadow = true;

    const elbowPivot = new THREE.Group();
    elbowPivot.position.set(0, -0.26, 0);

    const forearm = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.105, 0.26, 0.105), skinMat);
    forearm.position.y = -0.13;
    forearm.castShadow = true;

    elbowPivot.add(forearm);
    shoulderPivot.add(upperArm, elbowPivot);
    return { shoulderPivot, elbowPivot };
  }

  const tpArmLeftRig = createTpArticulatedArm(-1);
  const tpArmRightRig = createTpArticulatedArm(1);
  const tpArmL = tpArmLeftRig.shoulderPivot;
  const tpArmR = tpArmRightRig.shoulderPivot;
  const tpElbowL = tpArmLeftRig.elbowPivot;
  const tpElbowR = tpArmRightRig.elbowPivot;

  function createTpArticulatedLeg(side) {
    const hipJoint = new THREE.Group();
    hipJoint.position.set(side * 0.13, 0.0, 0);

    const thigh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.15, 0.38, 0.15), makeMat(accentColor));
    thigh.position.y = -0.19;
    thigh.castShadow = true;

    const kneeJoint = new THREE.Group();
    kneeJoint.position.set(0, -0.37, 0);

    const shin = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.135, 0.37, 0.135), makeMat(accentColor));
    shin.position.y = -0.185;
    shin.castShadow = true;

    kneeJoint.add(shin);
    hipJoint.add(thigh, kneeJoint);
    return { hipJoint, kneeJoint };
  }

  const tpLegLeftRig = createTpArticulatedLeg(-1);
  const tpLegRightRig = createTpArticulatedLeg(1);
  const tpLegL = tpLegLeftRig.hipJoint;
  const tpLegR = tpLegRightRig.hipJoint;
  const tpKneeL = tpLegLeftRig.kneeJoint;
  const tpKneeR = tpLegRightRig.kneeJoint;

  tpHip.add(tpTorso, tpHead, tpHair, tpArmL, tpArmR, tpLegL, tpLegR);
  tpRig.visible = false;
  return { tpRig, tpHip, tpTorso, tpHead, tpHair, tpArmL, tpArmR, tpElbowL, tpElbowR, tpLegL, tpLegR, tpKneeL, tpKneeR };
}

export const { tpRig, tpHip, tpTorso, tpHead, tpHair, tpArmL, tpArmR, tpElbowL, tpElbowR, tpLegL, tpLegR, tpKneeL, tpKneeR } = buildPlayerRigMesh(levelData.params.player);

worldRoot.add(tpRig);

export function syncEquippedMeshes() {
  while (fpRight.itemMount.children.length > 0) {
    fpRight.itemMount.remove(fpRight.itemMount.children[0]);
  }
  const rId = gameState.activeRightWeapon;
  if (rId !== 'fists') {
    { const wm = buildRightWeaponMesh(rId); applyModelPreset(rId, wm); fpRight.itemMount.add(wm); }
  }

  const keptLight = spotLight;
  const keptTarget = spotLight.target;
  while (fpLeft.itemMount.children.length > 0) {
    fpLeft.itemMount.remove(fpLeft.itemMount.children[0]);
  }
  fpLeft.itemMount.add(keptLight, keptTarget);

  const lSlot = gameState.leftHotbar[gameState.activeLeftSlot];
  if (lSlot && LEFT_ITEMS[lSlot.id]) {
    fpLeft.itemMount.add(buildLeftItemMesh(lSlot.id));
  }
  spotLight.visible = Boolean(gameState.flashlightOn && lSlot && lSlot.id === 'flashlight');
}

export function findMantleTarget() {
  if (gameState.inCar || player.mantle.active || meteorDrop.active) return null;
  const fwdX = -Math.sin(player.yaw);
  const fwdZ = -Math.cos(player.yaw);
  const probeX = player.x + fwdX * 0.95;
  const probeZ = player.z + fwdZ * 0.95;
  const landX = player.x + fwdX * 1.48;
  const landZ = player.z + fwdZ * 1.48;

  let bestTop = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < worldBoxes.length; i++) {
    const b = worldBoxes[i];
    if (!b.climbable) continue;
    const nearX = probeX >= b.minX - 0.35 && probeX <= b.maxX + 0.35;
    const nearZ = probeZ >= b.minZ - 0.35 && probeZ <= b.maxZ + 0.35;
    if (!nearX || !nearZ) continue;

    const rise = b.maxY - player.y;
    if (rise >= 0.45 && rise <= 2.38) {
      let blockedAbove = false;
      for (let j = 0; j < worldBoxes.length; j++) {
        if (i === j) continue;
        const ob = worldBoxes[j];
        if (landX + 0.3 > ob.minX && landX - 0.3 < ob.maxX && landZ + 0.3 > ob.minZ && landZ - 0.3 < ob.maxZ) {
          if (ob.maxY > b.maxY + 0.1 && ob.minY < b.maxY + 1.55) {
            blockedAbove = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      if (!blockedAbove && (bestTop === null || b.maxY > bestTop)) {
        bestTop = b.maxY;
      }
    }
  }
  if (bestTop === null) return null;
  return { x: landX, y: bestTop, z: landZ };
}

export function getNearestElevatorPad() {
  if (gameState.inCar || meteorDrop.active) return null;
  for (let i = 0; i < skyscrapers.length; i++) {
    const sk = skyscrapers[i];
    if (Math.hypot(player.x - sk.lobbyPad.x, player.z - sk.lobbyPad.z) < 2.2 && Math.abs(player.y - sk.lobbyPad.y) < 1.5) {
      return { sk, toRoof: true, dest: sk.roofPad };
    }
    if (Math.hypot(player.x - sk.roofPad.x, player.z - sk.roofPad.z) < 2.4 && Math.abs(player.y - sk.roofPad.y) < 1.8) {
      return { sk, toRoof: false, dest: sk.lobbyPad };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

export function triggerRightHandAttack() {
  if (gameState.inCar || player.attackTimer > 0 || meteorDrop.active) return;
  const w = RIGHT_WEAPONS[gameState.activeRightWeapon] || RIGHT_WEAPONS.fists;
  player.attackDuration = w.cooldown;
  player.attackTimer = w.cooldown;
  player.hitApplied = false;
  player._copHit = false;

  if (w.isRanged && w.id === 'hotdog_shooter') {
    fireBallisticWiener();
    player.hitApplied = true;
  } else {
    if (w.id === 'fists') {
      player.punchCombo = (player.punchCombo + 1) % 2;
    }
    sfx.swing();
  }
}

export function evaluateMeleeHit() {
  const w = RIGHT_WEAPONS[gameState.activeRightWeapon] || RIGHT_WEAPONS.fists;
  if (w.isRanged) return;
  const fwdX = -Math.sin(player.yaw);
  const fwdZ = -Math.cos(player.yaw);
  const reach = w.reach;
  const guest = hooks.mpIsGuest();
  for (let i = smashableCrates.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const c = smashableCrates[i];
    const dx = c.x - player.x;
    const dz = c.z - player.z;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
    if (dist <= reach && Math.abs(c.y - player.y) < 1.6) {
      const dot = dist > 0.01 ? (dx * fwdX + dz * fwdZ) / dist : 1;
      if (dot > 0.45) {
        sfx.hit();
        spawnParticles(c.x, c.y + 0.55, c.z, 0xd97706, 8, 4.5);
        if (guest) {
          hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'crate_hit', netId: c.netId, a: w.damage });
        } else {
          c.hp -= w.damage;
          if (c.hp <= 0) {
            sfx.smash();
            spawnParticles(c.x, c.y + 0.55, c.z, 0x92400e, 14, 6.5);
            const lootRec = destroyCrate(c, true);
            hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'crate_dead', netId: c.netId, x: c.x, y: c.y, z: c.z,
              loot: lootRec ? { netId: lootRec.netId, type: lootRec.type, id: lootRec.id, x: lootRec.x, y: lootRec.y, z: lootRec.z } : null });
            pushFeed('CRATE SMASHED!', 'border-l-amber-400');
          }
        }
        return;
      }
    }
  }
}

export function applyPlayerDamage(amount, sourceLabel) {
  if (gameState.damageCooldown > 0) return;
  gameState.damageCooldown = 0.45;
  gameState.hp = Math.max(0, gameState.hp - amount);
  sfx.damage();
  flashEdge('rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.48)');
  pushFeed(`-${Math.round(amount)} HP (${sourceLabel})`, 'border-l-rose-500');
  if (gameState.hp <= 0) {
    if (gameState.inCar) exitCurrentCar(true);
    if (hooks.mpIsGuest()) {
      hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'died' }); // host clears the shared wanted level
    } else {
      clearWantedLevel();
      hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'player_died' });
    }
    gameState.hp = gameState.maxHp;
    gameState.stamina = 100;
    gameState.cash = Math.max(0, gameState.cash - 50);
    climb.on = false;
    player.x = aura.hospitalSpawn ? aura.hospitalSpawn.x : -11.5;
    player.y = 0.18;
    player.z = aura.hospitalSpawn ? aura.hospitalSpawn.z : 18.5;
    player.vy = 0;
    player.grounded = true;
    player.peakAirY = 0.18;
    player.slideTimer = 0;
    player.airSlideReady = false;
    const wasted = document.getElementById('wasted-screen');
    if (wasted) {
      const cause = document.getElementById('wasted-cause');
      if (cause) cause.textContent = sourceLabel;
      wasted.style.display = 'flex';
      clearTimeout(wasted._t);
      wasted._t = setTimeout(() => { wasted.style.display = 'none'; }, 2600);
    }
    pushFeed(aura.hospitalSpawn ? 'REVIVED AT AURA GENERAL HOSPITAL (-$50 MEDICAL FEE)' : 'REVIVED AT CENTRAL PLAZA (-$50 MEDICAL FEE)', 'border-l-rose-500');
  }
  hooks.updateHUD();
}

// Menu Button Listeners
export function triggerMeteorLanding() {
  meteorDrop.active = true;
  meteorDrop.timer = 0;
  meteorDrop.x = -12;
  meteorDrop.y = 148;
  meteorDrop.z = -18;
  meteorDrop.vx = 0;
  meteorDrop.vy = -34;
  meteorDrop.vz = 0;
  meteorDrop.cameraShake = 0;

  if (meteorDrop.craterMesh) {
    worldRoot.remove(meteorDrop.craterMesh);
    meteorDrop.craterMesh = null;
  }
  if (meteorDrop.shockwaveMesh) {
    worldRoot.remove(meteorDrop.shockwaveMesh);
    meteorDrop.shockwaveMesh = null;
  }

  player.x = meteorDrop.x;
  player.y = meteorDrop.y;
  player.z = meteorDrop.z;
  player.vy = 0;
  player.yaw = 0.0;
  player.pitch = -0.68;
  gameState.isAiming = false;
  inputState.isPrimaryFireHeld = false;

  meteorGroup.visible = true;
  meteorGroup.position.set(player.x, player.y, player.z);
  meteorReticle.visible = true;
  document.getElementById('meteor-aim-banner').style.display = 'block';
  fpRoot.visible = false;
  tpRig.visible = false;

  sfx.meteorDive();
  pushFeed('AIM YOUR METEOR WITH MOUSE OR WASD!', 'border-l-amber-400');
}

let _promptT = 0;
const _pTxt = new WeakMap(), _pCls = new WeakMap();
function pText(el, v) { if (el && _pTxt.get(el) !== v) { _pTxt.set(el, v); el.textContent = v; } }
function pShow(el, on) { if (el && _pCls.get(el) !== on) { _pCls.set(el, on); el.classList.toggle('hidden', !on); } }
export function updateZoneAndPrompts(force = false) {
  const now = performance.now();
  if (!force && now - _promptT < 100) return;
  _promptT = now;
  let zone = 'SUNNYVALE CITY';
  for (const ez of enterableZones) {
    if (player.x >= ez.minX && player.x <= ez.maxX && player.z >= ez.minZ && player.z <= ez.maxZ && player.y < 5.0) {
      zone = ez.name;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (zone === 'SUNNYVALE CITY') {
    if (player.x >= 164) zone = 'REDROCK DESERT';
    else if ((player.x >= 140 && player.x <= 164) || (player.z >= 87 && player.z <= 109 && player.y > 2.2)) zone = 'I-88 INTERSTATE FREEWAY';
    else if (biomeAt(player.x, player.z) !== 'city') zone = { alpine: 'FROSTPINE ALPINE', woods: 'EMERALD WOODS', cove: 'SUNSET COVE' }[biomeAt(player.x, player.z)];
    else if (Math.abs(player.x) < 17 && Math.abs(player.z) < 17) zone = 'CENTRAL PLAZA';
    else if (player.z < -72) zone = 'DOWNTOWN SKYSCRAPER DISTRICT';
    else if (player.x < -22 && player.z < -22) zone = 'COMMERCIAL DISTRICT';
    else if (player.x > 22 && player.z < -22) zone = 'BRICKWOOD APARTMENTS';
    else if (player.x > 22 && player.z > 22) zone = 'HARBOR CONTAINER YARD';
    else zone = 'SUBURBAN NEIGHBORHOOD';
  }
  gameState.zoneName = zone;
  const promptEl = document.getElementById('interact-prompt');
  const mantleEl = document.getElementById('mantle-prompt');
  const keyEl = document.getElementById('interact-key');
  const textEl = document.getElementById('interact-text');
  if (meteorDrop.active) { pShow(promptEl, false); pShow(mantleEl, false); return; }
  if (gameState.inCar) { pShow(promptEl, true); pText(keyEl, 'F'); pText(textEl, 'EXIT VEHICLE'); pShow(mantleEl, false); return; }
  let nearRefill = null;
  for (const zoneObj of sausageRefillZones) {
    if (Math.hypot(player.x - zoneObj.x, player.z - zoneObj.z) < 3.1 && Math.abs(player.y - zoneObj.y) < 1.8) { nearRefill = zoneObj; break; }
  }
  if (nearRefill) {
    pShow(promptEl, true); pText(keyEl, 'E'); pText(textEl, `REFILL SAUSAGES (${gameState.hotdogAmmo}/${MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO})`);
  } else {
    const elev = getNearestElevatorPad();
    if (elev) {
      pShow(promptEl, true); pText(keyEl, 'F');
      pText(textEl, elev.toRoof ? `RIDE ELEVATOR TO ROOF (${elev.sk.name})` : 'RIDE ELEVATOR TO LOBBY');
    } else {
      const nearCar = getNearestVehicle(4.2);
      let nearNpc = null;
      for (const npc of npcs) {
        if (!npc.spawned || npc.asleep) continue;
        if (Math.hypot(player.x - npc.x, player.z - npc.z) < 2.5 && Math.abs(player.y - 0.18) < 1.8) { nearNpc = npc; break; }
      }
      if (nearCar) { pShow(promptEl, true); pText(keyEl, 'F'); pText(textEl, `DRIVE ${nearCar.v.name}`); }
      else if (nearNpc) { pShow(promptEl, true); pText(keyEl, 'E'); pText(textEl, `TALK TO ${nearNpc.name}`); }
      else { pShow(promptEl, false); }
    }
  }
  pShow(mantleEl, !!findMantleTarget());
}

function stepParticles(dt) {
  for (let i = particleBursts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const pt = particleBursts[i];
    pt.life -= dt;
    pt.vy -= 14 * dt;
    pt.mesh.position.x += pt.vx * dt;
    pt.mesh.position.y += pt.vy * dt;
    pt.mesh.position.z += pt.vz * dt;
    pt.mesh.scale.setScalar(Math.max(0.01, pt.life));
    if (pt.life <= 0) {
      worldRoot.remove(pt.mesh);
      particleBursts.splice(i, 1);
    }
  }
}

export function stepSimulation(dt, elapsed) {
  if (gameState.damageCooldown > 0) gameState.damageCooldown -= dt;
  if (gameState.speedBoostTimer > 0) gameState.speedBoostTimer -= dt;

  // Health Regeneration
  if (gameState.hp > 0 && gameState.hp < gameState.maxHp) {
    const regenRate = gameState.damageCooldown > 0 ? 0 : 3.5;
    if (regenRate > 0) {
      gameState.hp = Math.min(gameState.maxHp, gameState.hp + regenRate * dt);
    }
  }

  if (meteorDrop.active) {
    meteorDrop.timer += dt;

    const moveFwd = (keys['KeyW'] || keys['ArrowUp'] ? 1 : 0) - (keys['KeyS'] || keys['ArrowDown'] ? 1 : 0);
    const moveRight = (keys['KeyD'] || keys['ArrowRight'] ? 1 : 0) - (keys['KeyA'] || keys['ArrowLeft'] ? 1 : 0);
    const fastDive = keys['ShiftLeft'] || keys['ShiftRight'] || keys['Space'];

    const fwdX = -Math.sin(player.yaw);
    const fwdZ = -Math.cos(player.yaw);
    const rightX = Math.cos(player.yaw);
    const rightZ = -Math.sin(player.yaw);

    const aimHorizMag = Math.max(0, Math.cos(player.pitch)) * 46;
    const targetVx = (fwdX * moveFwd + rightX * moveRight) * 68 + fwdX * aimHorizMag * 0.72;
    const targetVz = (fwdZ * moveFwd + rightZ * moveRight) * 68 + fwdZ * aimHorizMag * 0.72;
    const targetVy = fastDive ? -74 : -31;

    meteorDrop.vx += (targetVx - meteorDrop.vx) * Math.min(1, dt * 6.5);
    meteorDrop.vz += (targetVz - meteorDrop.vz) * Math.min(1, dt * 6.5);
    meteorDrop.vy += (targetVy - meteorDrop.vy) * Math.min(1, dt * 6.5);

    const meteorPosition = clampMeteorPosition(meteorDrop.x + meteorDrop.vx * dt, meteorDrop.z + meteorDrop.vz * dt);
    meteorDrop.x = meteorPosition.x;
    meteorDrop.z = meteorPosition.z;
    meteorDrop.y += meteorDrop.vy * dt;

    player.x = meteorDrop.x;
    player.y = meteorDrop.y;
    player.z = meteorDrop.z;

    const timeToImpact = Math.max(0.05, (meteorDrop.y - 0.18) / Math.max(12, Math.abs(meteorDrop.vy)));
    const predictedPosition = clampMeteorPosition(
      meteorDrop.x + meteorDrop.vx * timeToImpact * 0.55,
      meteorDrop.z + meteorDrop.vz * timeToImpact * 0.55
    );
    const predX = predictedPosition.x;
    const predZ = predictedPosition.z;
    const predSurfaceY = computeSupportFloor(predX, predZ, 90, 0.5);

    meteorReticle.visible = true;
    meteorReticle.position.set(predX, predSurfaceY + 0.08, predZ);
    meteorReticle.rotation.y += dt * 2.5;
    const pulseScale = 1 + Math.sin(elapsed * 10) * 0.12;
    meteorReticle.children[0].scale.setScalar(pulseScale);

    const targetLabelEl = document.getElementById('meteor-target-label');
    if (targetLabelEl) {
      const locTag = predX >= 164 ? 'REDROCK DESERT'
        : predZ < -72 ? 'DOWNTOWN SKYSCRAPERS'
        : (predX >= 140 || (predZ >= 87 && predZ <= 109)) ? 'I-88 FREEWAY'
        : 'SUNNYVALE CITY';
      targetLabelEl.textContent = `TARGET: ${locTag} (${Math.round(predX)}, ${Math.round(predZ)}) â€¢ ALT ${Math.max(0, Math.round(meteorDrop.y))}M`;
    }

    meteorGroup.visible = true;
    meteorGroup.position.set(player.x, player.y + 0.9, player.z);
    meteorGroup.lookAt(
      player.x + meteorDrop.vx * 0.25,
      player.y + 0.9 + meteorDrop.vy * 0.25,
      player.z + meteorDrop.vz * 0.25
    );
    meteorCore.rotation.x += dt * 9.5;
    meteorCore.rotation.y += dt * 7.2;

    spawnParticles(player.x, player.y + 1.1, player.z, 0xf97316, 3, 4.5);
    spawnParticles(player.x, player.y + 1.4, player.z, 0xfacc15, 2, 3.2);

    const diveFov = fastDive ? 92 : 78;
    if (Math.abs(camera.fov - diveFov) > 0.2) {
      camera.fov += (diveFov - camera.fov) * Math.min(1, dt * 8);
      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    }

    const camBackDist = 9.5;
    const camOffsetX = Math.sin(player.yaw) * Math.cos(player.pitch) * camBackDist;
    const camOffsetY = -Math.sin(player.pitch) * camBackDist + 2.4;
    const camOffsetZ = Math.cos(player.yaw) * Math.cos(player.pitch) * camBackDist;
    camera.position.set(player.x + camOffsetX, player.y + camOffsetY, player.z + camOffsetZ);
    camera.lookAt(
      player.x - Math.sin(player.yaw) * 14,
      Math.max(0.2, player.y + Math.sin(player.pitch) * 14 - 4),
      player.z - Math.cos(player.yaw) * 14
    );

    const surfaceAtMeteor = computeSupportFloor(player.x, player.z, player.y + 1.2, 0.5);
    if (player.y <= surfaceAtMeteor + 0.2) {
      meteorDrop.active = false;
      meteorGroup.visible = false;
      meteorReticle.visible = false;
      document.getElementById('meteor-aim-banner').style.display = 'none';

      player.y = surfaceAtMeteor;
      player.vy = 0;
      player.grounded = true;
      player.peakAirY = surfaceAtMeteor;
      player.pitch = -0.04;
      camera.fov = 72;
      camera.updateProjectionMatrix();

      sfx.meteorImpact();
      meteorDrop.cameraShake = 0.72;
      flashEdge('rgba(249, 115, 22, 0.65)');
      spawnParticles(player.x, player.y + 0.4, player.z, 0xf97316, 26, 14.0);
      spawnParticles(player.x, player.y + 0.4, player.z, 0xfacc15, 20, 12.0);
      spawnParticles(player.x, player.y + 0.4, player.z, 0x44403c, 16, 10.0);

      const crater = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.CircleGeometry(3.4, 24),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x1c1917 })
      );
      crater.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
      crater.position.set(player.x, player.y + 0.035, player.z);
      worldRoot.add(crater);
      meteorDrop.craterMesh = crater;

      const shock = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.RingGeometry(0.8, 1.4, 28),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfacc15, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.9 })
      );
      shock.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
      shock.position.set(player.x, player.y + 0.06, player.z);
      worldRoot.add(shock);
      meteorDrop.shockwaveMesh = shock;
      meteorDrop.shockwaveLife = 0.65;

      for (const lp of lampPosts) {
        if (lp.state === 'standing' && Math.hypot(lp.x - player.x, lp.z - player.z) < 11.5) {
          knockOverLamppost(lp, Math.atan2(lp.x - player.x, lp.z - player.z), 4.2);
        }
      }

      pushFeed('METEOR TOUCHDOWN â€” LANDED AT YOUR TARGET!', 'border-l-amber-400');
    }

    stepParticles(dt);
    updateZoneAndPrompts();
    hooks.updateHUD();
    return;
  }

  if (meteorDrop.shockwaveMesh) {
    meteorDrop.shockwaveLife -= dt;
    const sc = 1 + (0.65 - meteorDrop.shockwaveLife) * 26;
    meteorDrop.shockwaveMesh.scale.set(sc, sc, 1);
    meteorDrop.shockwaveMesh.material.opacity = Math.max(0, meteorDrop.shockwaveLife / 0.65);
    if (meteorDrop.shockwaveLife <= 0) {
      worldRoot.remove(meteorDrop.shockwaveMesh);
      meteorDrop.shockwaveMesh = null;
    }
  }
  if (meteorDrop.cameraShake > 0) {
    meteorDrop.cameraShake = Math.max(0, meteorDrop.cameraShake - dt);
  }

  if (gameState.wheelTimer > 0) {
    gameState.wheelTimer -= dt;
    if (gameState.wheelTimer <= 0) document.getElementById('radial-menu').style.display = 'none';
  }
  if (gameState.dialogueTimer > 0) {
    gameState.dialogueTimer -= dt;
    if (gameState.dialogueTimer <= 0) document.getElementById('npc-dialogue').style.display = 'none';
  }

  // Rapid-fire trigger while Left-Mouse Button is held down!
  if (
    inputState.isPrimaryFireHeld &&
    !gameState.inCar &&
    player.attackTimer <= 0 &&
    gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'hotdog_shooter' &&
    !gameState.inventoryOpen &&
    !gameState.mapOpen &&
    !gameState.settingsOpen
  ) {
    if (gameState.hotdogAmmo > 0) {
      triggerRightHandAttack();
    } else {
      inputState.isPrimaryFireHeld = false;
      sfx.uiClick();
      pushFeed('OUT OF SAUSAGES! REFILL AT AURA HOTDOGS OR PLAZA CART', 'border-l-rose-500');
    }
  }

  if (player.attackTimer > 0) {
    player.attackTimer = Math.max(0, player.attackTimer - dt);
    const progress = 1 - player.attackTimer / player.attackDuration;
    if (!player.hitApplied && progress >= 0.42) {
      player.hitApplied = true;
      evaluateMeleeHit();
    }
  }
  if (player.leftUseTimer > 0) {
    player.leftUseTimer = Math.max(0, player.leftUseTimer - dt);
  }

  const targetFov = (!gameState.inCar && gameState.isAiming && gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'hotdog_shooter')
    ? 34
    : (gameState.inCar ? 76 : 72);
  if (Math.abs(camera.fov - targetFov) > 0.15) {
    camera.fov += (targetFov - camera.fov) * Math.min(1, dt * 12);
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  }

  if (gameState.inCar) {
    fpRoot.visible = false;
    tpRig.visible = false;

    stepVehicleDriving(dt, elapsed);

  } else {
    sfx.updateEngine(false, 0, false);

    if (player.mantle.active) {
      player.mantle.timer += dt;
      const t = Math.min(1, player.mantle.timer / player.mantle.duration);
      const ease = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
      player.x = player.mantle.startX + (player.mantle.endX - player.mantle.startX) * ease;
      player.z = player.mantle.startZ + (player.mantle.endZ - player.mantle.startZ) * ease;
      player.y = player.mantle.startY + (player.mantle.endY - player.mantle.startY) * ease + Math.sin(t * Math.PI) * 0.34;
      if (t >= 1) {
        player.mantle.active = false;
        player.y = player.mantle.endY;
        player.grounded = true;
        player.peakAirY = player.y;
      }
    } else {
      // Input key checks for Crouch & Jump
      const crouchInput = Boolean(keys['KeyC'] || keys['ControlLeft']);
      if (!crouchInput) {
        player.crouchJumped = false;
      }

      // Jump input while crouching -> jump & uncrouch immediately
      if (keys['Space'] && (player.grounded || player.coyote > 0) && !player.mantle?.active) {
        player.vy = 7.5;
        player.grounded = false;
        player.coyote = 0;
        if (player.crouching || player.slideTimer > 0) {
          player.crouching = false;
          player.crouchJumped = true;
          player.slideTimer = 0;
        }
      } else if (crouchInput && !player.crouchJumped) {
        player.crouching = true;
      } else if (!crouchInput) {
        player.crouching = false;
      }

      const moveFwd = (keys['KeyW'] || keys['ArrowUp'] ? 1 : 0) - (keys['KeyS'] || keys['ArrowDown'] ? 1 : 0);
      const moveRight = (keys['KeyD'] || keys['ArrowRight'] ? 1 : 0) - (keys['KeyA'] || keys['ArrowLeft'] ? 1 : 0);

      // Sprinting and Stamina Latch
      const shiftHeld = Boolean(keys['ShiftLeft'] || keys['ShiftRight']);
      if (!shiftHeld) {
        player.sprintExhausted = false;
      }

      const wantsSprint = shiftHeld && !player.sprintExhausted && moveFwd > 0 && !player.crouching && !gameState.isAiming && gameState.stamina > 0;
      if (wantsSprint) {
        gameState.stamina = Math.max(0, gameState.stamina - 8.5 * dt);
        if (gameState.stamina <= 0) {
          player.sprintExhausted = true;
        }
        player.sprintCycle += dt * 15.5;
      } else {
        // Crouching replenishes stamina 3x quicker!
        const regenRate = player.crouching ? 48.0 : 16.0;
        gameState.stamina = Math.min(100, gameState.stamina + regenRate * dt);
        player.sprintCycle = 0;
      }

      const boost = gameState.speedBoostTimer > 0 ? 1.25 : 1.0;
      const pp_ = levelData.params.player;
      const msScale = pp_.moveSpeed / 4.7;
      const speed = (player.crouching ? 2.6 * msScale : gameState.isAiming ? 3.2 * msScale : wantsSprint ? pp_.moveSpeed * pp_.sprintMultiplier : pp_.moveSpeed) * boost * (isInWater(player.x, player.z) ? 0.55 : 1);

      const fwdX = -Math.sin(player.yaw);
      const fwdZ = -Math.cos(player.yaw);
      const rightX = Math.cos(player.yaw);
      const rightZ = -Math.sin(player.yaw);

      let vx = fwdX * moveFwd + rightX * moveRight;
      let vz = fwdZ * moveFwd + rightZ * moveRight;
      const len = Math.hypot(vx, vz);
      let tvx = 0, tvz = 0;
      if (len > 0.001) {
        tvx = (vx / len) * speed;
        tvz = (vz / len) * speed;
        player.walkCycle += dt * (wantsSprint ? 15.5 : 8.5);
      }
      const grip = player.grounded ? surfaceGrip(player.x, player.z) * (len > 0.001 ? 1 : 1.25) : (len > 0.001 ? 2.2 : 0.4);
      player.pvx = (player.pvx || 0) + (tvx - (player.pvx || 0)) * Math.min(1, grip * dt);
      player.pvz = (player.pvz || 0) + (tvz - (player.pvz || 0)) * Math.min(1, grip * dt);

      // Crouch Slide from Air (Jump/Cliff) vs Flat Ground
      const horizSpeed = Math.hypot(player.pvx || 0, player.pvz || 0);

      if (!player.grounded) {
        if (player.crouching && horizSpeed > 1.8) {
          player.airSlideReady = true;
          player.airSlideSpeed = Math.max(horizSpeed * 1.3, 7.5);
          player.airSlideDirX = (player.pvx || 0) / horizSpeed;
          player.airSlideDirZ = (player.pvz || 0) / horizSpeed;
        }
      } else {
        if (player.crouching && player.airSlideReady) {
          player.slideTimer = 0.65;
          player.slideVx = player.airSlideDirX * player.airSlideSpeed;
          player.slideVz = player.airSlideDirZ * player.airSlideSpeed;
          player.airSlideReady = false;
          sfx.mantle();
          spawnParticles(player.x, player.y + 0.1, player.z, 0xd1d5db, 10, 4.0);
        } else if (!player.crouching) {
          player.airSlideReady = false;
        }
      }

      if (player.slideTimer > 0) {
        player.slideTimer -= dt;
        player.pvx = player.slideVx;
        player.pvz = player.slideVz;
        player.slideVx *= Math.max(0, 1 - dt * 2.2);
        player.slideVz *= Math.max(0, 1 - dt * 2.2);
        if (player.grounded && Math.random() < 0.35) {
          spawnParticles(player.x, player.y + 0.05, player.z, 0x9ca3af, 2, 1.5);
        }
      }

      vx = player.pvx; vz = player.pvz;

      const nextX = player.x + vx * dt;
      const nextZ = player.z + vz * dt;
      const bodyH = player.crouching ? 1.22 : player.height;

      if (!collidesHorizontally(nextX, player.z, player.y, bodyH, player.radius)) player.x = nextX; else player.pvx = 0;
      if (!collidesHorizontally(player.x, nextZ, player.y, bodyH, player.radius)) player.z = nextZ; else player.pvz = 0;

      const previousY = player.y;
      player.vy -= 19.5 * dt * (player.vy < 0 ? 1.28 : (keys['Space'] ? 1 : 1.9));
      player.y += player.vy * dt;
      if (!player.grounded && player.y > player.peakAirY) player.peakAirY = player.y;

      const supportY = computeSupportFloor(player.x, player.z, previousY - 0.46, player.radius);
      if (player.y <= supportY && player.vy <= 0) {
        if (!player.grounded) {
          const fallDist = player.peakAirY - supportY;
          if (fallDist > 3.6) {
            const fallDmg = Math.min(65, (fallDist - 3.6) * 8.5);
            applyPlayerDamage(fallDmg, 'FALL IMPACT');
          }
          player.landingDip = Math.min(0.24, Math.max(0.06, fallDist * 0.04));
        }
        player.y = supportY;
        player.vy = 0;
        player.grounded = true;
        player.peakAirY = supportY;
      } else {
        player.grounded = false;
      }
    }

    if (player.landingDip > 0) player.landingDip = Math.max(0, player.landingDip - dt * 1.4);
    player.coyote = player.grounded ? 0.12 : Math.max(0, (player.coyote || 0) - dt);

    const targetEye = player.crouching ? 1.12 : 1.62;
    player.eyeHeight += (targetEye - player.eyeHeight) * Math.min(1, dt * 14);
    const movingSpeed = (keys['KeyW'] || keys['KeyS'] || keys['KeyA'] || keys['KeyD']) && player.grounded;
    const isSprintingNow = (keys['ShiftLeft'] || keys['ShiftRight']) && (keys['KeyW'] || keys['ArrowUp']) && !player.crouching && !gameState.isAiming && gameState.stamina > 0 && movingSpeed && !player.sprintExhausted;
    const bobY = movingSpeed ? Math.abs(Math.sin(player.walkCycle)) * (isSprintingNow ? 0.085 : 0.045) : 0;
    const isStepping = movingSpeed && player.slideTimer <= 0 && !player.mantle.active;
    const biome = biomeAt(player.x, player.z);
    const stepSurface = under() ? 'metal'
      : (player.x >= 164 || biome === 'cove') ? 'sand'
      : (biome === 'woods' || biome === 'alpine') ? 'grass'
      : 'concrete';
    sfx.updateFootsteps(dt, isStepping, isSprintingNow, player.grounded, stepSurface);


    let swingPitch = 0;
    let punchZ = 0;
    let punchSwingX = 0;
    let punchYaw = 0;
    let punchRoll = 0;
    let recoilZ = 0;
    if (player.attackTimer > 0) {
      const t = 1 - player.attackTimer / player.attackDuration;
      const curve = Math.sin(t * Math.PI);
      if (gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'hotdog_shooter') {
        recoilZ = curve * 0.11;
        swingPitch = curve * 0.10;
      } else if (gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'fists') {
        punchZ = curve * 0.34;
        punchSwingX = curve * 0.22;
        punchYaw = curve * 0.68;
        punchRoll = curve * 0.45;
        swingPitch = curve * 0.18;
      } else {
        swingPitch = -curve * 1.15;
      }
    }
    const leftRaise = player.leftUseTimer > 0 ? Math.sin((1 - player.leftUseTimer / 0.32) * Math.PI) * 0.22 : 0;

    if (gameState.isAiming && gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'hotdog_shooter') {
      fpRight.pivot.visible = false;
      fpLeft.pivot.visible = false;
    } else {
      fpRight.pivot.visible = true;
      fpLeft.pivot.visible = true;
      const isRightHook = gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'fists' && player.punchCombo === 0 && player.attackTimer > 0;
      const isLeftHook = gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'fists' && player.punchCombo === 1 && player.attackTimer > 0;

      fpRight.pivot.position.set(
        0.32 - (isRightHook ? punchSwingX : 0),
        -0.26 - bobY * 0.5 + (isRightHook ? 0.05 : 0),
        -0.34 - (isRightHook ? punchZ : 0) + recoilZ
      );
      fpRight.pivot.rotation.set(
        swingPitch,
        isRightHook ? punchYaw : 0,
        isRightHook ? punchRoll : 0
      );

      fpLeft.pivot.position.set(
        -0.32 + (isLeftHook ? punchSwingX : 0),
        -0.26 - bobY * 0.5 + leftRaise + (isLeftHook ? 0.05 : 0),
        -0.34 - (isLeftHook ? punchZ : 0)
      );
      fpLeft.pivot.rotation.set(
        isLeftHook ? swingPitch : 0,
        isLeftHook ? -punchYaw : 0,
        isLeftHook ? -punchRoll : 0
      );
    }

    if (gameState.thirdPerson) {
      fpRoot.visible = false;
      tpRig.visible = true;
      tpRig.position.set(player.x, player.y + bobY - player.landingDip, player.z);

      // Rotate playerMesh.rotation.y by + Math.PI (180 degrees) so character faces forward
      tpRig.rotation.y = player.yaw;

      tpHip.rotation.x = 0;
      tpTorso.rotation.y = 0;
      tpArmL.rotation.set(0, 0, 0);
      tpArmR.rotation.set(0, 0, 0);
      tpElbowL.rotation.x = 0;
      tpElbowR.rotation.x = 0;
      tpLegL.rotation.set(0, 0, 0);
      tpLegR.rotation.set(0, 0, 0);
      tpKneeL.rotation.x = 0;
      tpKneeR.rotation.x = 0;

      if (player.slideTimer > 0) {
        // Sliding posture animation
        tpHip.rotation.x = -0.48;
        tpLegL.rotation.x = 0.85;
        tpKneeL.rotation.x = 0.12;
        tpLegR.rotation.x = -0.65;
        tpKneeR.rotation.x = 0.95;
        tpArmL.rotation.x = -0.45;
        tpElbowL.rotation.x = 0.22;
        tpArmR.rotation.x = -0.45;
        tpElbowR.rotation.x = 0.22;
      } else if (!player.grounded) {
        // 3RD-PERSON JUMP ANIMATION (Rising Leap vs Mid-Air Landing Prep)
        const airBlend = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, player.vy / 6.5));
        if (airBlend > 0.1) {
          tpHip.rotation.x = -0.14 * airBlend;
          tpLegL.rotation.x = -0.82 * airBlend;
          tpKneeL.rotation.x = 0.95 * airBlend;
          tpLegR.rotation.x = 0.42 * airBlend;
          tpKneeR.rotation.x = 0.25 * airBlend;
          tpArmL.rotation.x = 0.95 * airBlend;
          tpArmL.rotation.z = -0.22 * airBlend;
          tpElbowL.rotation.x = 0.45 * airBlend;
          tpArmR.rotation.x = -0.52 * airBlend;
          tpArmR.rotation.z = 0.22 * airBlend;
          tpElbowR.rotation.x = 0.45 * airBlend;
        } else {
          tpHip.rotation.x = -0.08;
          tpLegL.rotation.x = -0.34;
          tpKneeL.rotation.x = 0.42;
          tpLegR.rotation.x = -0.18;
          tpKneeR.rotation.x = 0.30;
          tpArmL.rotation.x = 0.48;
          tpArmL.rotation.z = -0.38;
          tpElbowL.rotation.x = 0.35;
          tpArmR.rotation.x = 0.48;
          tpArmR.rotation.z = 0.38;
          tpElbowR.rotation.x = 0.35;
        }
      } else if (isSprintingNow) {
        // 3RD-PERSON OLYMPIC SPRINTER ANIMATION
        const sPhase = player.walkCycle;
        const stride = Math.sin(sPhase);
        const oppStride = Math.sin(sPhase + Math.PI);

        tpHip.rotation.x = -0.26;
        tpTorso.rotation.y = -stride * 0.18;

        tpLegL.rotation.x = stride * 1.12;
        tpKneeL.rotation.x = Math.max(0.12, -stride * 1.15 + 0.35);

        tpLegR.rotation.x = oppStride * 1.12;
        tpKneeR.rotation.x = Math.max(0.12, -oppStride * 1.15 + 0.35);

        tpArmL.rotation.x = -stride * 1.18 + 0.15;
        tpArmL.rotation.z = -0.08;
        tpElbowL.rotation.x = 1.52;

        tpArmR.rotation.x = -oppStride * 1.18 + 0.15;
        tpArmR.rotation.z = 0.08;
        tpElbowR.rotation.x = 1.52;
      } else {
        const legSwing = movingSpeed ? Math.sin(player.walkCycle) * 0.58 : 0;
        tpLegL.rotation.x = legSwing;
        tpKneeL.rotation.x = movingSpeed ? Math.max(0, -legSwing * 0.55) : 0;
        tpLegR.rotation.x = -legSwing;
        tpKneeR.rotation.x = movingSpeed ? Math.max(0, legSwing * 0.55) : 0;

        tpArmL.rotation.x = -legSwing * 0.65 + leftRaise * 3.5;
        tpElbowL.rotation.x = movingSpeed ? 0.22 : 0.08;
        tpArmR.rotation.x = legSwing * 0.65;
        tpElbowR.rotation.x = movingSpeed ? 0.22 : 0.08;
      }

      if (gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'hotdog_shooter') {
        tpArmR.rotation.set(1.48 + swingPitch, 0, 0);
        tpElbowR.rotation.x = 0.18;
      } else if (player.attackTimer > 0) {
        const t = 1 - player.attackTimer / player.attackDuration;
        const hookArc = Math.sin(t * Math.PI);
        if (gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'fists') {
          if (player.punchCombo === 0) {
            tpArmR.rotation.set(1.35 * hookArc, 0, -0.78 * hookArc);
            tpElbowR.rotation.x = 0.85 * hookArc;
            tpTorso.rotation.y = 0.48 * hookArc;
          } else {
            tpArmL.rotation.set(1.35 * hookArc, 0, 0.78 * hookArc);
            tpElbowL.rotation.x = 0.85 * hookArc;
            tpTorso.rotation.y = -0.48 * hookArc;
          }
        } else {
          tpArmR.rotation.x = -swingPitch * 1.4;
          tpElbowR.rotation.x = 0.35;
        }
      }

      tpHip.scale.y = player.crouching ? 0.72 : 1.0;
      tpHip.position.y = player.crouching ? 0.65 : 0.90;

      const pivotY = player.y + (player.crouching ? 1.15 : 1.56);
      const camDist = 3.65;
      const idealX = player.x + Math.sin(player.yaw) * Math.cos(player.pitch) * camDist;
      const idealY = pivotY - Math.sin(player.pitch) * camDist + 0.35;
      const idealZ = player.z + Math.cos(player.yaw) * Math.cos(player.pitch) * camDist;

      let safeDist = camDist;
      for (let s = 0.25; s <= 1.0; s += 0.25) {
        const tx = player.x + (idealX - player.x) * s;
        const tz = player.z + (idealZ - player.z) * s;
        const ty = pivotY + (idealY - pivotY) * s;
        if (collidesHorizontally(tx, tz, ty - 0.3, 0.5, 0.25)) {
          safeDist = Math.max(0.75, camDist * (s - 0.22));
          break;
        }
      }
      const ratio = safeDist / camDist;
      camera.position.set(
        player.x + (idealX - player.x) * ratio,
        Math.max(0.55, terrainH(player.x + (idealX - player.x) * ratio, player.z + (idealZ - player.z) * ratio) + 0.7, pivotY + (idealY - pivotY) * ratio),
        player.z + (idealZ - player.z) * ratio
      );
      camera.lookAt(player.x - Math.sin(player.yaw) * 4, pivotY + Math.sin(player.pitch) * 4, player.z - Math.cos(player.yaw) * 4);
    } else {
      fpRoot.visible = true;
      tpRig.visible = false;
      const shakeX = meteorDrop.cameraShake > 0 ? (Math.random() - 0.5) * meteorDrop.cameraShake * 0.45 : 0;
      const shakeY = meteorDrop.cameraShake > 0 ? (Math.random() - 0.5) * meteorDrop.cameraShake * 0.45 : 0;
      camera.position.set(player.x + shakeX, player.y + player.eyeHeight + bobY - player.landingDip + shakeY, player.z);
      camera.rotation.y = player.yaw;
      camera.rotation.x = player.pitch;
    }
  }

  for (let i = worldPickups.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const p = worldPickups[i];
    p.phase += dt * 2.8;
    p.group.rotation.y += dt * 1.8;
    p.group.position.y = p.baseY + Math.sin(p.phase) * 0.12;
    if (Math.hypot(player.x - p.x, player.z - p.z) < 1.35 && Math.abs(player.y - p.y) < 1.8) {
      if (hooks.mpIsGuest()) {
        if (!p.asked) { p.asked = true; hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'pickup_take', netId: p.netId }); }
        continue;
      }
      if (p.type === 'right') {
        if (!gameState.rightWeapons.includes(p.id)) gameState.rightWeapons.push(p.id);
        gameState.activeRightWeapon = p.id;
        if (p.id === 'hotdog_shooter') gameState.hotdogAmmo = MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO;
        sfx.pickup();
        syncEquippedMeshes();
        pushFeed(`EQUIPPED ${RIGHT_WEAPONS[p.id].name.toUpperCase()}`, 'border-l-amber-400');
        hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'pickup_taken', netId: p.netId });
        worldRoot.remove(p.group);
        freeGeo(p.group);
        worldPickups.splice(i, 1);
      } else if (addLeftItemToBag(p.id, 1)) {
        sfx.pickup();
        syncEquippedMeshes();
        pushFeed(`COLLECTED ${LEFT_ITEMS[p.id].name.toUpperCase()}`, 'border-l-sky-400');
        hooks.mpWorldEvent({ t: 'pickup_taken', netId: p.netId });
        worldRoot.remove(p.group);
        freeGeo(p.group);
        worldPickups.splice(i, 1);
      }
    }
  }

  stepParticles(dt);

  for (let i = 0; i < aviationBeacons.length; i++) {
    const pulse = Math.sin(elapsed * 4.5 + i * 1.4) > 0.25;
    aviationBeacons[i].visible = pulse;
  }

  updateProjectileWieners(dt);
  updateLampposts(dt, elapsed);
  updateBuildingLods();
  updateSkyscraperInteriors();
  updateNPCs(dt, elapsed);

  const sunY = Math.max(0, player.y);
  const shadowMoved = !aura._shadowAt
    || Math.hypot(player.x - aura._shadowAt.x, player.z - aura._shadowAt.z) > 8
    || Math.abs(sunY - aura._shadowAt.y) > 6;
  const shadowStale = performance.now() - (aura._shadowT || 0) > 200;
  if (shadowMoved || shadowStale) {
    sunLight.position.set(player.x + 78, sunY + 130, player.z + 52);
    sunLight.target.position.set(player.x, sunY, player.z);
    sunLight.target.updateMatrixWorld();
    renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate = true;
    aura._shadowAt = { x: player.x, y: sunY, z: player.z };
    aura._shadowT = performance.now();
  }

  updateZoneAndPrompts();
  hooks.updateHUD();
}

export function enhancePerFrame(dt, elapsed) {
  for (const n of npcs) {
    if (n.mesh && n.mesh.userData.face) {
      const bl = n.asleep || ((elapsed * 0.8 + n.id * 1.37) % 3.6) < 0.13;
      setFaceMood(n.mesh.userData.face, n.asleep ? 'neutral' : n.hostile ? 'angry' : n.fleeTimer > 0 ? 'scared' : (n.age === 'child' ? 'happy' : 'neutral'), bl);
    }
  }
  updatePickpocket(dt);
  updateBiomeAtmosphere(dt, elapsed);
  auraFrame(dt, elapsed);
  if (!pp.hinted && elapsed > 6) { pp.hinted = true; pushFeed('TIP: SNEAK BEHIND A CITIZEN & HOLD [G] TO PICKPOCKET', 'border-l-emerald-400'); }
}

export function startClimb(dir,i){climb.on=true;climb.dir=dir;if(dir<0)reviveRats();climb.t=0;climb.i=i;sfx.mantle();
  pushFeed(dir<0?'CLIMBING DOWN INTO THE SEWERS':'CLIMBING OUT',dir<0?'border-l-emerald-400':'border-l-sky-400');}

export function climbFrame(dt){
  if(!climb.on)return;
  climb.t=Math.min(1,climb.t+dt/2.2);
  const e=climb.t*climb.t*(3-2*climb.t),top=.18,bot=SFY+.02,m=manholes[climb.i];
  player.x=m[0];player.z=m[1];player.vy=0;player.grounded=true;
  player.y=climb.dir<0?top+(bot-top)*e:bot+(top-bot)*e;player.peakAirY=player.y;
  if(climb.t>=1){climb.on=false;manholeCovers[climb.i].visible=true;}
}

}

/** Push levelData.params into the live game tables (weapon stats, HP, cash, gearbox caps). */
export function applyLevelParams(data = levelData) {
  const p = data.params;
  for (const id of Object.keys(p.weapons || {})) {
    if (RIGHT_WEAPONS[id]) {
      const w = p.weapons[id];
      if (Number.isFinite(w.damage)) RIGHT_WEAPONS[id].damage = w.damage;
      if (Number.isFinite(w.cooldown)) RIGHT_WEAPONS[id].cooldown = w.cooldown;
      if (Number.isFinite(w.reach)) RIGHT_WEAPONS[id].reach = w.reach;
    }
  }
  gameState.maxHp = p.player.maxHp;
  gameState.hp = gameState.maxHp;
  gameState.cash = p.player.startCash;
  (p.vehicles.gearCapsMph || []).forEach((mph, i) => { if (i < GEAR_CAPS_MPH.length && Number.isFinite(mph)) GEAR_CAPS_MPH[i] = mph; });
}

 */

  mpActive: () => false,

  mpLocalId: () => '1',
  mpTargets: () => [],            // other players: {id,x,y,z,yaw,crouching,inCar,hp,attacking}
  mpHitPlayer: noop,              // (id, amount, sourceLabel) authority-routed player damage
  mpAward: noop,                  // (id, cash, itemId?) kill/loot credit

  mpVehicleImpact: noop,          // (targetCarIdx, impulseX, impulseZ, yawImpulse) car-to-car network RPC

  mpNpcLoot: () => 'local',       // (npc) -> 'local' (award now) | 'pending' (host grant)

  mpSpawnBomb: noop,              // (x,y,z,vx,vy,vz) networked nuke
  mpFestivalLead: () => 70,
  mpSetFestivalLead: noop,
  mpAdoptTraffic: noop,           // new host: rebuild traffic route progress from positions
  mpDrawMap: noop,                // (ctx, X, Y) remote player dots
  mpShutdown: noop,               // graceful leave (host_bye / bye)
};

/** Axis-aligned footprint of an upright box/cylinder (half extents) after the object's yaw + scale. */
export function uprightFootprint(obj, halfX, halfZ, height) {
  const sx = Math.abs(obj.scale.x), sy = Math.abs(obj.scale.y), sz = Math.abs(obj.scale.z);
  const c = Math.abs(Math.cos(obj.rotation.y)), s = Math.abs(Math.sin(obj.rotation.y));
  const hx = halfX * sx, hz = halfZ * sz;
  const wx = c * hx + s * hz, wz = s * hx + c * hz;
  return { minX: obj.position.x - wx, maxX: obj.position.x + wx, minZ: obj.position.z - wz, maxZ: obj.position.z + wz, height: height * sy };
}

export function flashEdge(cssColor) {
  const el = document.getElementById('screen-flash');
  if (!el) return;
  el.style.background = `radial-gradient(circle, transparent 52%, ${cssColor} 100%)`;
  el.style.opacity = '1';
  clearTimeout(el._t);
  el._t = setTimeout(() => { el.style.opacity = '0'; }, 190);
}

export function pushFeed(text, borderClass = 'border-l-amber-400') {
  const container = document.getElementById('feed-container');
  if (!container) return;
  const div = document.createElement('div');
  div.className = `toast-item glass-panel px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-tactical font-bold uppercase text-white border-l-2 ${borderClass}`;
  div.textContent = text;
  container.appendChild(div);
  while (container.children.length > 4) container.removeChild(container.firstChild);
  setTimeout(() => { if (div.parentNode) div.remove(); }, 2000);
}

export const WORLD_BOUNDS = Object.freeze({ minX: -710, maxX: 540, minZ: -590, maxZ: 325 });

export function computeSupportFloor(x, z, footY, radius) {
  if (footY < -1.0) return sewerFloor(x, z);
  const inJungle = x >= 368 && x <= 533 && z >= -180 && z <= 240;
  let highest = (inJungle ? 1.5 : (x >= 164 ? 0.02 : 0.18)) + terrainHeightOffset(x, z);
  if (z < -170 || x < -196 || (x >= -700 && x <= -50 && z >= 108 && z <= 320)) highest = Math.max(highest, terrainH(x, z));

  for (let r = 0; r < driveableRamps.length; r++) {
    const rm = driveableRamps[r];
    if (x + radius > rm.minX && x - radius < rm.maxX && z + radius > rm.minZ && z - radius < rm.maxZ) {
      const t = rm.axis === 'z'
        ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (z - rm.minZ) / (rm.maxZ - rm.minZ)))
        : Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (x - rm.minX) / (rm.maxX - rm.minX)));
      const ry = rm.startY + (rm.endY - rm.startY) * t;
      if (ry <= footY + 0.85 && ry > highest) {
        highest = ry;
      }
    }
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < worldBoxes.length; i++) {
    const b = worldBoxes[i];
    if (x + radius > b.minX && x - radius < b.maxX && z + radius > b.minZ && z - radius < b.maxZ) {
      if (b.maxY <= footY + 0.46 && b.maxY > highest) {
        highest = b.maxY;
      }
    }
  }
  return highest;
}

export function collidesHorizontally(nx, nz, footY, bodyH, radius) {
  if (nx < WORLD_BOUNDS.minX + radius || nx > WORLD_BOUNDS.maxX - radius || nz < WORLD_BOUNDS.minZ + radius || nz > WORLD_BOUNDS.maxZ - radius) return true;
  if (nx < -196 && nx > -560 && (nz < -170 + radius || nz > 170 - radius) && !(nx >= -700 && nx <= -50 && nz >= 108 && nz <= 320)) return true;
  if (footY < -1.0 && !sewerOK(nx, nz, radius)) return true;
  if (footY > -0.5) { const th = terrainH(nx, nz); if (th > -90 && th - footY > 1.0) return true; }
  for (let i = 0; i < worldBoxes.length; i++) {
    const b = worldBoxes[i];
    if (b.maxY <= footY + 0.42 || b.minY >= footY + bodyH - 0.08) continue;
    if (nx + radius > b.minX && nx - radius < b.maxX && nz + radius > b.minZ && nz - radius < b.maxZ) {
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

export let _seed = 1337;

export const rnd = () => { _seed = (_seed * 1664525 + 1013904223) >>> 0; return _seed / 4294967296; };

export const pick = (arr) => arr[Math.floor(rnd() * arr.length)];

export const waterZones = [];

export const iceZones = [];

export function biomeAt(x, z) {
  if (z >= 110 && x > -136 && x < 138) return x < -52 ? 'alpine' : (x < 28 ? 'woods' : 'cove');
  return 'city';
}

export function isInWater(x, z) {
  for (const w of waterZones) if (x > w.minX && x < w.maxX && z > w.minZ && z < w.maxZ) return true;
  return false;
}

export function surfaceGrip(x, z) {
  for (const ic of iceZones) if (Math.hypot(x - ic.x, z - ic.z) < ic.r) return 1.5;
  if (isInWater(x, z)) return 7;
  const b = biomeAt(x, z);
  if (b === 'alpine') return Math.abs(x + 96) < 6 ? 11 : 3.6;
  if (b === 'cove') return Math.abs(x - 72) < 6 ? 14 : 10;
  return 14;
}

// ======================= AURA OVERHAUL MODULE =======================
// Mountains + winding road, sewer cave, police + wanted system, suspension, UI
export function sstep(a,b,x){const t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(x-a)/(b-a)));return t*t*(3-2*t);}

export function hsh(i,j){const s=Math.sin(i*127.1+j*311.7)*43758.5453;return s-Math.floor(s);}

export function vnoise(x,z){const i=Math.floor(x),j=Math.floor(z),fx=x-i,fz=z-j,u=fx*fx*(3-2*fx),v=fz*fz*(3-2*fz);return hsh(i,j)*(1-u)*(1-v)+hsh(i+1,j)*u*(1-v)+hsh(i,j+1)*(1-u)*v+hsh(i+1,j+1)*u*v;}

export function fbm(x,z,o){let a=.5,f=1,s=0;for(let k=0;k<(o||5);k++){s+=a*vnoise(x*f,z*f);f*=2.03;a*=.5;}return s;}

export function ridge(x,z,o){let a=.5,f=1,s=0;for(let k=0;k<(o||5);k++){s+=a*(1-Math.abs(vnoise(x*f,z*f)*2-1));f*=2.1;a*=.5;}return s;}

export function roadC(z){const t=-170-z;return 116+64*Math.sin(t*0.019)+24*Math.sin(t*0.052+1);}

export function roadH(z){return 0.2+Math.min(82,(-170-z)*0.16);}

export const AHC={px:-480,pz:-45};

export const CAR_PTS=[[2.2,0],[2.1,.9],[2.1,-.9],[0,.95],[0,-.95],[-2.1,.9],[-2.1,-.9],[-2.2,0]];

export const ahRz=x=>45*Math.sin(Math.min(-196-x,236)*.02), ahRzD=x=>(-196-x)<236?.9*Math.cos((-196-x)*.02):0, ahRy=x=>38*sstep(0,230,-196-x);

export const AIRFIELD = Object.freeze({ y: 38, rx0: -355, rx1: -208, rz: -106, rh: 13, hx0: -407, hx1: -356, hz0: -129, hz1: -83, hangerX0: -406.5, hangerX1: -356.5, hangerZ0: -129, hangerZ1: -83 });

function distanceToRect(x, z, x0, x1, z0, z1) {
  return Math.hypot(Math.max(x0 - x, 0, x - x1), Math.max(z0 - z, 0, z - z1));
}

function airfieldMask(x, z) {
  const runway = 1 - sstep(0, 11, distanceToRect(x, z, AIRFIELD.rx0, AIRFIELD.rx1, AIRFIELD.rz - AIRFIELD.rh, AIRFIELD.rz + AIRFIELD.rh));
  const hangar = 1 - sstep(0, 6, distanceToRect(x, z, AIRFIELD.hx0, AIRFIELD.hx1, AIRFIELD.hz0, AIRFIELD.hz1));
  return Math.max(runway, hangar);
}

export function hillsH(x,z){
  const env=sstep(0,110,-196-x), hm=env*(6+fbm(x*.007+20,z*.007,4)*85+fbm(x*.03,z*.03,3)*7);
  const pl=1-sstep(70,118,Math.hypot(x-AHC.px,z-AHC.pz));let h=hm*(1-pl)+38*pl;
  const s=-196-x;if(s>=0&&s<300){const w=sstep(7,28,Math.abs(z-ahRz(x))/Math.hypot(1,ahRzD(x)));h=ahRy(x)*(1-w)+h*w;}
  const townEdge=Math.hypot(Math.max(Math.abs(x+285)-62,0),Math.max(Math.abs(z)-94,0));
  const town=1-sstep(0,34,townEdge),townRoad=sstep(13,25,Math.abs(z-ahRz(x))/Math.hypot(1,ahRzD(x))),townBlend=town*townRoad;
  if(townBlend>0)h=h*(1-townBlend)+(ahRy(x)+9)*townBlend;
  const field=airfieldMask(x,z);
  if(field>0)h=h*(1-field)+AIRFIELD.y*field;
  return h;
}

export function riverX(z){return roadC(z)-72+14*Math.sin(z*.04);}

export function alpineH(x, z) {
  return 0.02 + Math.max(0, 8 + Math.sin(x * 0.02) * 3 + Math.cos(z * 0.03) * 2);
}

export function terrainBaseH(x,z){
  if(x<-196&&x>-560&&z>-172&&z<172) return hillsH(x,z);
  if(x>=-700&&x<=-50&&z>=108&&z<=320) return alpineH(x,z);
  if(z>-170) return -99;
  const t=-170-z, env=sstep(0,120,t);
  const wx=x+(fbm(x*.006+9,z*.006)-.5)*90, wz=z+(fbm(x*.006,z*.006+4)-.5)*90;
  const rise=.3+.7*sstep(30,320,t);
  const mass=fbm(wx*.0042,wz*.0042,4), pk=Math.pow(fbm(wx*.0036+3,wz*.0036,5),3);
  let m=env*(8+(mass*mass*60+pk*300)*rise+fbm(x*.025,z*.025,4)*9+ridge(x*.018,z*.018,3)*8*sstep(40,120,t));
  m=Math.max(m,roadH(z)*.85);
  const w=sstep(8,64,Math.abs(x-roadC(z)));
  const hh=roadH(z)*(1-w)+(0.2+m)*w, cv=5*(1-sstep(3,18,Math.abs(x-riverX(z))))*sstep(20,120,t);
  return cv>0.01?Math.max(.3,hh-cv):hh;
}

export function terrainH(x,z){
  return terrainBaseH(x,z) + terrainHeightOffset(x, z);
}

export const SCX=20,SCZ=-30,SFY=-9;

export function sewerFloor(){return SFY;}
